Old v4 / v5-without-`variable` → clean model. **Verified** on the no-store dev server (Archivo Black): add axis WGHT 0–100 via the form → location slider; `+ Master` at base rejected, at WGHT 100 creates "wght 100" + `editMasterIdx=1`; widen master-1 `o` by +40/side → `instanceGlyph('o', {WGHT:50})` width = exact midpoint (595→675 ⇒ 635) and `glyphSubs` at loc 50 matches; `compatOK` true (shared canonical structure); editor read-only between masters, editable + `editMasterIdx→mi` exactly on one; **Bake** with "Dilated" → toast "0 direct · 356 reprojected · 63 skipped", master-1 `o` keeps base 108-pt structure, still interpolates; OTF Worker export at loc 50 re-parses (28 glyphs, `o` 55 cmds = interp+effect vs raw 26); `.lvp` v5 `variable` round-trips (axes, master location + 356 edited keys, `loc`, `editMasterIdx`) byte-exact; loading a v4 `.lvp` afterward fully clears (0 axes, 1 master, no lingering edits) and re-loading the VF `.lvp` restores; `node -c` + css-lint clean; console clean (bar the stale synthetic-pointer artifact from the earlier Phase-1 test). Shipped `4e4a5de` → merge `7151313` on `main`.
  - **Then (Diego: "phase 3") — UFO + designspace export.** `livingpath/index.html` now also loads `shared/ufo-export.js` + `shared/zip.js` (both were already tracked as dormant modules; the SHARED-COMPONENTS.md "zip.js deleted" note was stale — it was re-added `1980bc1`). Export popover gains **`#exUFO` "⬇ UFO + designspace (.zip)"** + a `#exUFOHint` (`fontmake -m <family>.designspace -o variable`), both un-`hidden` by `renderVar()` only when `_gmodel.axes.length && _gmodel.masters.length>1`. Handler: `m.metrics = glyphMetrics()` (real `OS/2` asc/desc/x/cap into each `fontinfo.plist`) → `Organica.ufoExport.bundle(m, {familyName:$('#exName').value})` → feed the file map into `Organica.zip()` → `download(z.blob(), '<name>-ufo.zip')` + a `toast('✓ N masters · M glyphs')`. No new deps (`zip.js` is first-party, STORE-only). **Verified** on the no-store dev server: 2-master WGHT setup, `bundle` → 853 files (1 `.designspace` + 2 `.ufo` dirs, one glyphs/ + contents/metainfo/fontinfo/lib.plist each); real button → `LivingPath-ufo.zip`, 12.1 MB, valid EOCD, 858 entries; a 3-glyph subset written to disk → **`unzip -t` "No errors detected"**, `.designspace` well-formed (axis WGHT, 2 `<source>` with matching `filename`, 1 `<instance>`), `A_.glif` = valid UFO format-2 (`<advance>` + `<unicode hex="0041">` + cubic `<point>`s, 307 pts), both masters carry their own glyphs; full-charset export toast "✓ 2 masters · 422 glyphs"; `node -c` (inline + both modules) + css-lint clean; console clean (bar the two stale test artifacts). **The variable-font arc (Phases 1–3) is complete.** Committed next.
  - **Then (Diego: "fammi un'analisi di typoclast.com... capiamo come potremmo modificare il nostro per farlo simile a questo in termini di engine")** — competitive analysis + a plan-mode pass, then implementation. Inspected typoclast's live bundle (`assets/index-*.js`, ~1MB, no source maps): built on **fontkit** (keeps each glyph's native `glyf`/`cff` point structure, `onCurve` terms in the code), **zero raster code anywhere** (no `ImageData`/`OffscreenCanvas`/marching-squares/noise libs) — every slider (twist/pinch/pull/perspective/wave/refraction/bulge/noise/inflate/sharpen/ripple/flip/crush/wind/curl/spikes/polygonize/echo/pixelate/slice/groviera/"vector griddler") is a pure analytic point-space function, synchronous, no Worker, one `<canvas>` redrawn every `input` tick — that's the "instant" feel Diego meant. **The finding:** Living Path already had the *other half* of this — `shared/pathfx.js`'s `FX` registry + `applyVectorGroups()` (7 effects: jitter/wobble/inflate/roughen/smooth/twist/scatter), live in Sinew, disabled in Living Path (`const CHAIN=false, vectorActive=()=>false` — now removed) since the font-only split. Reviving + extending it, not building a new engine, was the plan (approved: **combine in the same stack** as raster, not a Sinew-style exclusive toggle; **near-full parity**, ~19 new effects in one pass).
    - **`shared/pathfx.js`** — 19 new `FX.*` entries added after `scatter` (pinch, bulge, pull, perspective, wave, refraction, noisefield, sharpen, ripplev, flip, crush, wind, curl, spikesvec, polygonizevec, pixelatevec, echo, slice, griddler — each self-normalising off its own `bboxOf(subs)`, reusing `twist`/`wobble`/`inflate`'s centre/normal/seed math); `RFX.groviera` (a one-slider wrapper on the existing `particles(...,carve:1)` punch — literally what "Frog-eggs"/"Cahn cells" already do, just named). Two new `PRESETS.vector` combos ("Perspective drift", "Thorned"). The trickiest ones: `polygonizevec` decimates + injects tight corner-clamp point pairs (the same trick `Organica.glyphModel.canonicalContour`'s `cornerClamp` uses) so the shared Catmull-Rom `dFromSubs` re-emits near-straight facets; `slice` shears points above/below a cut line — an **approximation**, not a true polygon boolean cut (no clipping lib in the project); `echo` bakes N offset duplicate contours into the same `subs` (even-odd fill), not a multi-glyph render.
    - **`livingpath/index.html`** — `GROUPS.vector[0]` is now a real group (was `[]`, vestigial). `LP_CATEGORIES` gained an `engine:'vector'|'raster'` tag per category + 3 new categories (**Distort** twist/pinch/bulge/wave/ripplev/perspective/wind/curl/flip · **Deform** jitter/wobble/inflate/roughen/smooth/sharpen/noisefield/crush/pull · **Break** scatter/echo/polygonizevec/pixelatevec/slice/spikesvec/griddler); `groviera` folded into raster's existing Texture category. New `TYPE_ENGINE`/`defFor(type)`/`groupsFor(engine)`/`layersFor(type)`/`layersSig(layers)` helpers thread the dual registry through every function that used to hardcode `RFX`/`GROUPS.raster` — `lpControls`/`identityParams`/`isIdentity`/`freshStack(engine)`/`stackFromOverrides(ov,engine)`/`normalizeStack(layers,engine)`/`presetToStack(p)` (now returns `{raster,vector}`, routing a preset's mixed `fx:` list by `TYPE_ENGINE`)/`syncActive()`/`buildLayerBlock`/`renderStack`/`catActiveCount`/the drag-drop handler. `processGlyphEm` gained `applyVectorStack(norm)` (calls `Organica.pathfx.applyVectorGroups`, cheap, synchronous) run **before** the raster `applyStackNorm`, same order in `buildModifiedFont`'s main-thread fallback (free, since it already calls `processGlyphEm`) and hand-wired into `buildModifiedFontWorker`'s glyph-prep loop (vector step runs on the main thread pre-`postMessage`; **when raster is inactive, the glyph is marked `baked` and skips the Worker's rasterize pass entirely** — no lossy round-trip on a purely geometric export, a real benefit of reusing this engine noted in the plan). `stackIsIdentity()`/`famSig()`/`buildTag()`/`SEEDED_FX`/`stackHasSeed()` extended to read both `GROUPS.raster[0]` and `GROUPS.vector[0]`. **The instant-render fix:** `render()`'s `heavy` flag changed from "any non-identity stack" to `rasterActive()` only, and the non-heavy path now returns **synchronously** (`doit()` called directly, no `setTimeout`) instead of going through the same 110 ms debounce as before — a vector-only slider drag was previously *also* debounced (just without the scrim); now it genuinely renders on every `input` tick, matching typoclast. New **Transform** panel section (Rotate/Slant/Stretch X/Stretch Y) — `XFORM` state, applied as a final em-space affine in `processGlyphEm` (after denorm, not a stack layer, so it never touches the effect-stack's own 1000-box math); folded into `famSig`/`buildTag`. `.lvp` **v5→v6**: `vectorGroups` + `transform` keys; `loadProject` defaults both to identity for older files. `#varBake` (Phase 2's "Bake stack → master") now composes `applyVectorStack` + `applyStackNorm` (was raster-only — a real gap caught while wiring this, since a live Distort layer would otherwise have been silently dropped from a baked master).
    - **Verified** on the no-store dev server: 9 categories render (6 raster + 3 vector, divided by a "Vector · instant" label); dragging Twist's angle slider applies synchronously (~40 ms wall-clock incl. full specimen re-layout, `procOverlay` never shown, `rasterActive():false`/`vectorActive():true`) — screenshot shows the specimen visibly per-glyph-twisted; Transform slant confirmed via direct `processGlyphEm` bbox math (matches `tan(θ)` exactly) and a live `CACHE` bbox diff; **composability** — Avulsion (raster) + a hand-dialed Twist (vector) both active simultaneously, both present in `famSig()`; **OTF export** (Worker) with raster+vector combined re-parses clean (28 glyphs); vector-only export re-parses clean and confirmed to skip the raster pass (`rasterActive:false`); `.lvp` v6 round-trips the vector stack + transform byte-exact; an old v5 (no `vectorGroups`) and a synthetic v5 with real raster layer objects both load correctly (vector defaults to identity, raster restores exactly); **Sinew regression** — loads clean, `FX`/`RFX` counts 26/20 (7+19 vector, 19+1 raster), `FX.pinch.apply` and `RFX.groviera` run correctly on a synthetic shape, Engine toggle UI unaffected. `node -c` (inline + `pathfx.js`) + css-lint clean. Docs: `docs/LIVINGPATH.md` new §7 subsection ("Distort & Deform — the vector engine") + a staleness note on §2–§6 (pre-font-only architecture, unrelated to this pass), this note + Tools row. **Not committed** — pending Diego's "porta in prod".
  - **Then (Diego: "fai un debug visivo sui nuovi filter") — a real per-effect visual pass, not just the pipeline-level checks above.** Drove all 20 new effects (19 vector + `groviera`) one at a time on a live glyph (`Og&Q` specimen + a single big letter for the subtle ones), reading `processGlyphEm`/bbox deltas alongside screenshots since a couple of real bugs turned out to be invisible at small specimen scale and only showed up numerically. **4 real bugs found and fixed in `shared/pathfx.js`:** (1) **`perspective`** — `k = amount/2000` was calibrated for a coordinate space ~25× smaller than the actual normalised 1000-box; at `amount:70` the top half collapsed to the `Math.max(0.05,…)` floor and the bottom exploded to ~13× width (screenshot: a flat smear with spikes). Rewritten to scale by the glyph's own `bb.h` (`t = 1 + ((y-cy)/halfH) * (amount/100)*0.6`, clamped `[0.25, 2.5]`) — now a legible trapezoid skew. (2) **`pull`** — weighted by radial distance from the centroid, which is nearly *uniform* on a roughly round glyph (an O), so it moved every point by about the same amount = a rigid **translation**, not a deformation (bbox height unchanged, shape untouched — confirmed numerically before the visual check even caught it). Rewritten to weight by position **along the pull axis** (the same projection `wind` already uses, inverted), so one side of the glyph stretches toward the anchor — verified an O compresses 728→462 in height at `amount:70`. (3) **`noisefield`** — max amount was 40 on a 0–1000-unit glyph space → ~7 unit average displacement, imperceptible; range widened to 100 (matches every other Amount slider's scale). (4) **`sharpen`** — pushed each point away from its *immediate* neighbours' midpoint, but on a font's densely-flattened curve (FL=8) that midpoint sits ~1 unit from the point itself (tiny "sagitta") — completely invisible regardless of `amount`. Now reaches ~5% of the contour's point count on each side before averaging, giving a real base deviation to amplify (verified: subtle-facet at 20, dramatic-crystalline at 45, chaotic at 75+ — a legitimate destructive range, not a regression once large). (5) **`echo`** — `spread` was a small fixed-unit range (2–40) on a ~700–1000-unit glyph (invisible), **and**, the real finding: every shape in Living Path renders `fill-rule="evenodd"` (required for real counters), so heavily-overlapping same-winding duplicate copies **cancel to a hollow outline** in doubly-covered regions instead of reading as solid repeats — the screenshot showed only a ghost outline, main fill gone. `spread` is now a **%** of the glyph's own size (5–80, was 2–40 absolute) so copies separate enough to read as a legible layered-outline "echo" look; full solid-repeat compositing isn't achievable under the shared evenodd convention without per-copy alpha (not part of this pipeline) — documented as the accepted shape of the effect, not a further bug. **Confirmed correct-but-subtle-at-small-scale (not bugs):** `pinch` (bbox 760→577, real, just proportionally-uniform on a round glyph), `slice`/`griddler` (both changed `processGlyphEm`'s bbox substantially — 40+ unit deltas — but read as near-invisible in a 4-glyph 96pt specimen; confirmed dramatic and correct on a single big glyph). **Left as-is, pre-existing (not introduced by this pass):** `scatter` (`FX.scatter`, one of the original 7 effects, shared with Sinew) reads weak even at `dist:100` — its per-*subpath* (not per-point) random magnitude can draw a near-zero offset for a glyph with few subpaths; flagged to Diego rather than silently rewritten, since it predates this work and any change would need to be verified against Sinew's own "Shatter" preset too. **Verification method note:** several of these bugs were invisible in the small multi-glyph specimen screenshot (`Og&Q` at 96 pt in the 1400-wide stage) even when working correctly (`pinch`/`pull`-before-fix/`slice`/`griddler`) — the reliable check was always a direct `bboxOf(processGlyphEm(glyph,upm))` delta against the untouched glyph, with the single-big-glyph Preview view for a final visual sanity pass. `node -c` + css-lint clean after all 5 fixes; re-verified each fix live (hot-patched in-page first to work around static-asset browser caching on the plain `python -m http.server` dev setup, then confirmed identical code landed on disk). **Not committed** — pending Diego's "porta in prod" (same pending batch as the Distort/Deform/Break feature itself).

- **September 8, 2026 (later) — Living Path: the central canvas is now an ALT.TF-style type tester.** Diego: make the canvas like `alt-tf.com/products/alt-riviera-mono` — one big **editable** specimen through the effect stack + a control bar pinned inside the bottom. Decisions (AskUserQuestion): the tester **replaces** the canvas (drops the ≤12-char `typedLayout` fit-to-box view *and* the `text specimen` toggle; `full family` stays as the one secondary view); bar = **SIZE / TRACK / align L·C·R / CAPS / RESET / night-mode ◐**; **night-mode swaps the real Ink/Paper swatches** (`#cp-ink` ↔ `#cp-bg` via `swatches.{ink,bg}.set`), so it flips the on-canvas preview *and* every PNG/SVG/OTF export and the right-panel Colour rows; **default face auto-loaded on boot** so the tester is never empty.
  - **`shared/vendor/archivo-black.ttf`** — Archivo Black (Omnibus-Type, **SIL OFL 1.1**), an Arial-Black-alike grotesque, glyph-outline extraction only (not `@font-face`'d). *First attempt used a Diego-supplied Arial Black `.ttf`; swapped to Archivo Black same session once the repo was confirmed public — Arial Black is Monotype-proprietary and not redistributable.* Boot: `fetch('/shared/vendor/archivo-black.ttf') → arrayBuffer → loadFontBuffer(buf,'Archivo Black')`, `.catch` leaves the `#empty` state.
  - **`livingpath/index.html`** — markup: `.specctl`/`#stageText`/dead `#specLang`/`#specNew` and `#tgSpec` **removed**; added `#specEdit` (a transparent `contenteditable="plaintext-only"` overlay, `color:transparent; caret-color:var(--ink)`, `font-family:'lp-specimen'` — a `FontFace` built from `window.__FONT_BUF = buf.slice(0)` so its wrapping/caret track the SVG within a few px) + `.lp-testbar` (absolute, `bottom:76px`, above the fixed floatbar; token-keyed light bar, not the dark `#compute-bar`/`.hud-btn` pattern). `buildSpecimenSubs()` rewritten as `buildSpecimenSubs({size,track,align,caps})` — **two-pass** (pass 1 word-wrap measuring width incl. `trackEm = size*track/1000`; pass 2 place each line with `ox` = left/`x0+(maxX-x0-w)/2`/`maxX-w`), `caps` uppercases per word in pass 1, `track` added per glyph + per gap, NVAR=4 seed variation + `(char,variant)` cache kept, bottom clip kept as a `break`. `renderSpecimen`→`renderTester` (calls `buildSpecimenSubs(tester)` + `syncSpecEditBox()`). `render()` dispatch is now `isFont ? (showFamily ? renderFamily() : renderTester()) : computed()` and it toggles `#specEdit`/`#testbar` `hidden`. `typedLayout`/`TYPED_GHOST`/`stageStr`/`renderFontText`/`showSpecimen`/`SPECIMEN_SAMPLES`/`fetchWikiText` **deleted**; `renderFontText` → a small `seedFontSource(name)` (SRC sentinel + `getPath('Agr')` fallback geometry). Slider readouts + `--fill` reuse `panel.css` bare `input[type=range]` + `Organica.enhanceSliders` (already booted); `wireTbSlider` does readout text + `tester[key]` + `render()`. Align/CAPS = `.seg-ctrl`/`.seg-btn` with `aria-pressed`; RESET/night = `.mini-btn`. `syncSpecEditBox()` glues the overlay to `board.getBoundingClientRect()` (1000-box→px), called on load / every `renderTester` / a `ResizeObserver` on `#canvas-wrap` / window resize. **WYSIWYG**: size/track/align/caps only *place* subs already 1000-box-normalised by `processGlyphEm`, so preview and OTF export stay identical in effect strength (comment + LIVINGPATH.md §11).
  - **`.lvp` → v4** — `projectJSON` drops `text`, adds `specimen` (the string) + `tester:{size,track,align,caps}`. `loadProject` tolerant: no `tester` → `TESTER_DEFAULTS`; no `specimen` → old `text` → current string. Night-mode needs nothing extra (swapped `ink`/`bg` persist through the existing `p.ink`/`p.bg` round-trip).
  - **Not Sinew** (no text specimen — untouched). No `shared/*` code changes.
  - **Verified** on the no-store dev server: boot auto-loads the default face → tester renders immediately, `#empty` never shows, no console errors; SIZE 24→240 rescales + re-wraps + clips at the bottom (no scroll); TRACK readout tracks; align L/C/R moves `aria-pressed` + re-offsets lines + the overlay `text-align`; CAPS uppercases both; RESET → 96/0/centre/off (default align is centre), Ink/Paper + effect stack untouched; night ◐ swaps `#cp-ink`↔`#cp-bg` (`hex-*` panel fields update, board bg goes black), click again restores; editing the overlay re-renders the board and the overlay box computes to `45*px` inset / `910*px` wide / `size*px` font over `#board`; `full family` still switches to the grid and hides the bar+overlay; `.lvp` v4 round-trips `specimen`+`tester`, an old v3 blob restores the string from `text` and the tester falls to defaults; SVG export contains the exact board `<path>` (WYSIWYG) + the background rect. `node -c` + `python3 scripts/css-lint.py` clean. Docs: `docs/LIVINGPATH.md` §7 rewrite + §9/§11 touch-ups, `CLAUDE.md` Tools row + this note, `THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md` Archivo Black entry.
  - **Follow-ups same session (Diego):** (1) default face swapped Arial Black → **Archivo Black** (see note above); (2) the stage box is now **landscape** — `#board` `viewBox` `0 0 1000 1000` → `0 0 1400 900` (Living Path only), threaded through as `BOX_W`/`BOX_H` consts in `buildSpecimenSubs` (`maxX`, bottom clip), `buildFamilySubs` (grid math + col count), `syncSpecEditBox` (px scale + overlay width), `currentSVGString` (viewBox/rect/width-height); `#board` CSS `max-width:96%; max-height:88%`. Per-glyph `processGlyphEm` normalisation stays its own 1000-box, so WYSIWYG is unaffected. Verified: board renders 1.56:1, overlay tracks, family grid + SVG export all landscape. (3) the specimen block is now **vertically centred** in the stage box — `y0` computed after pass 1 from the line count (`blockH = (n-1)*lh + size`, `y0 = max(size, (BOX_H-blockH)/2 + size*0.82)`); overflows fall back to top-aligned + bottom clip. Verified: ink bbox mid-Y ≈ `BOX_H/2` for 1 line, 4 lines and size 200.

- **September 8, 2026 — Living Path effect stack: 10 new raster effects + 3 extra params on existing ones, and the stack UI regrouped into collapsible category sections.** Diego asked what other parameters could become effects, then "sviluppali tutti". Two decisions via AskUserQuestion: the ~25-effect stack becomes **category sections, collapsible** (the Step-F "all 9 always present, flat" model doesn't scale past ~9); the vector post-trace group (Roughen/Wobble/Twist/Jitter/Simplify) — Diego "non lo so" → **deferred**, it needs a new pipeline stage mirrored in the OTF Web Worker, its own pass. Scope this round = **A (extra params) + B (new raster field effects)**, both pure additions to `rasterFieldFromGroups`.
  - **`shared/pathfx.js`** — 10 new field functions before the marching-squares section, all self-contained (own `mulberry32`/value-noise/distance-transform, no `Organica.noise` dep so load order and Sinew are untouched) + matching `RFX` entries: **`smear`** (`dirBlur` — 1-D directional box blur), **`chamfer`** (morphological open+close → round/bevel corners), **`warp`** (`warpField` — value-noise domain warp), **`halftone`** (AM dot/line/square screen on a rotated grid), **`shatter`** (Voronoi F1/F2 ridge → cracks), **`isobands`** (2-pass chamfer distance transform → concentric rings), **`ripple`** (sinusoidal row/col shear), **`glitch`** (random per-band x-offset), **`mosaic`** (nearest-sample pixelation, distinct from `polygonize`'s average-then-threshold), **`spikes`** (short thick hairs shot along the field gradient from a fraction of edge pixels). Extra params, all backward-compatible (default = prior behaviour, `grayScott` verified byte-identical at `aniso=0` — the split `lapUx+lapUy` collapses to the old 4-neighbour `lapU`): **Particles `bias`** (−1 interior / +1 edge — biases the seed pool), **Seam carve `dir:2`** (H then V in one pass, handled in `RFX.seam.apply`), **Reaction-diffusion `aniso`** (−90..90 → anisotropic `Du`/`Dv` per axis → real parallel stripes instead of isotropic blobs). New funcs added to the `Organica.pathfx` export list.
  - **`livingpath/index.html`** — `LP_CATEGORIES` (Mass / Texture / Fracture / Distort / Structure / Reaction, 19 effects) drives a derived `LP_FX_ORDER` + `LP_CAT_OF` + `LP_PRIMARY` (each new effect's "0 = off" primary; the sub-min guard also lives in each `apply()` so Sinew is safe if a user drags a primary below its natural min). `renderStack()` rewritten to emit one `.fx-cat` section per category — collapsible `<button class="fx-cat-head">` (chevron + label + active-count badge), auto-open iff the section has a non-identity layer, explicit toggles remembered in `LP_CAT_OPEN`. Drag-reorder is **within a section only** — blocks carry `data-cat`, the drag/drop handlers bail when `el.dataset.cat !== DRAG_CAT`, and the flat `layers[]` stays category-contiguous in `LP_CATEGORIES` order (verified invariant: collapsing the per-layer category sequence == the category id order, after a within-section move). `presetToStack`/`normalizeStack` rewritten to always emit in canonical `LP_FX_ORDER` (was "preset's own order first" — meaningless once sectioned) via one `stackFromOverrides(ovByType)` helper. **Worker OTF export** (`livingPathWorkerURL`): the 10 new funcs added to the `shared=[…].map(f=>f.toString())` list + 10 `applyLayerW` branches + the 3 param passes — verified WYSIWYG (an export with warp+chamfer+ripple active re-parses as a valid `OTTO`, 679 glyphs, glyph R = 43 path cmds reflecting the distortion). `SEEDED_FX` gained `warp`/`shatter`/`glitch`/`spikes` so the randomised-alternates path fires for them.
  - **Outline is now a stack block** (this session, before the effects work): the old stage `outline` toggle button removed; `showOutline` (bool) → `outlineWidth` (px, 0 = filled). This round added **Rings (1–6) + Ring gap** to that block — `strokeRingsSVG(d, ink, paper, w, rings, gap)` draws `rings ≥ 2` as back-to-front alternating ink/paper centred strokes of growing width so each reads as a real gap-separated concentric echo, no geometry offsetting. Wired into `repaint`/`renderFamily`/`renderSpecimen`/`currentSVGString`/`iconFor` + the family-cache sig + `buildTag`. Also removed the "overlay original" stage toggle (`#tgOrig`/`showOrig` + the faint ghost path) and the "Living Path" wordmark from the empty-canvas state.
  - **`.lvp` v3** now also persists `smooth` / `outline` / `outlineRings` / `outlineGap` (was dropping contour-smooth and outline entirely); older files without the keys load with defaults.
  - **Sinew** (`sinew/index.html`) — shares `shared/pathfx.js` `RFX`, so the 10 new effects auto-appear in its add-effect menu via the generic `controls`/`defaults`/`apply` contract; no Sinew code touched, loads clean, 19 effects, no regressions. Its long generic labels ("Shape (0 dot · 1 line · 2 square)", "Axis (0 H · 1 V · 2 both)") truncate a bit in the narrow panel — acceptable.
  - **Not done / deferred**: the vector post-trace group (C); a few PRESETS showcasing the new effects; `docs/LIVINGPATH.md` update. The 4 untracked `shared/{glyph-model,glyph-editor,ufo-export,zip}.js` from the earlier removed variable-font plan are still untracked — not part of this work, left for Diego to decide whether they ship.
  - Verified: `node -c` + `python3 scripts/css-lint.py` clean; all 13 new/changed effects produce sane contour output in-browser with no console errors; category collapse + cross-section drop-guard + outline rings confirmed live; OTF export via Worker re-parses valid. Committed on `staging` → `main`.

- **September 7, 2026 (later still) — CRITICAL FIX: every production deployment since the Sep 6 CSS-audit merge had been silently failing.** Diego noticed a failed deployment in the Vercel dashboard and asked for it to be checked. `vercel.json` had gained two non-standard top-level keys, `_comment_headers` and `_comment_order` (added in commit `2313902`, "perf: cache headers for shared assets + preload the font", part of the Sep 6 CSS-audit session), as a way to attach rationale to the new `headers` block — but Vercel's `vercel.json` schema validation is `additionalProperties: false`, so **every deployment since that commit has failed at the config-validation step**, before any build step even runs (`get_deployment_build_logs` on the failed production deployment showed zero build/error events — the failure never reached the build). Confirmed via the Vercel MCP tools (`list_deployments`/`get_deployment`): production had been silently stuck on the pre-Sep-6 deployment (`Merge staging: Living Path/Sinew panel markup rewrite`) through the entire Sep 6 CSS-audit merge, the Sep 7 DPI+bleed foundation merge, and the Sep 7 Colornet print-production merge — three full "porta in prod" cycles that never actually went live, with no visible error anywhere in this session's own tooling (git push succeeds regardless; only the Vercel dashboard/API shows deployment state). **Fix:** removed both `_comment_*` keys from `vercel.json` (JSON has no comment syntax, and Vercel's schema forbids unrecognized properties outright — there is no valid way to keep prose inside this file); the rationale they carried is preserved here instead of in the config: the `headers` block (added the same commit) fixed a real gap — no headers block at all meant every static asset served with Vercel's default `max-age=0, must-revalidate`, so every tool-to-tool navigation re-validated 8 shared stylesheets, `core.js`, and a 165 KB font, 10+ conditional round-trips per page load, forever; `shared/vendor/*` gets `immutable` (vendored libraries/the font are replaced by a differently-named file, never changed in place); `shared/*.css|js` gets `max-age=300, stale-while-revalidate=86400` (instant navigation, a deploy picked up within 5 minutes); order matters in the `headers` array — Vercel applies every matching rule and the last one to set a given header wins, and `shared/vendor/` also matches the first (broader) rule, so the immutable rule is listed second specifically so it wins there. **Lesson, now in the Repo Structure comment next to `vercel.json`**: never add `_comment_*` (or any non-schema key) to this file again — it deploys silently-broken with no error surfaced anywhere except the Vercel dashboard/API itself, which nothing in the normal git-push workflow checks. Verified: `python3 -c "import json; json.load(open('vercel.json'))"` parses clean; the file's actual behavior (headers + rewrites) is byte-identical to before, only the two comment keys are gone.

- **September 7, 2026 (later same week) — Colornet print-production phase: real physical lpi AM screening, per-plate SVG export, registration marks — plus a new shared plate-export module wired into Pollen and Spore.** The natural next step after the DPI+bleed foundation, which had explicitly deferred `lpiToPx`, registration marks, and per-plate vector export to "the future Colornet separations phase" (see that phase's own note below, and `shared/print-size.js`'s own header at the time). Scope confirmed with Diego via AskUserQuestion: do the Colornet work AND build a shared "N ink colours → N plate files" module now (not wait for a hypothetical future 2nd consumer) — Pollen and Spore already produce genuine multi-colour output via RMX palette mode, and a real screen-printer/riso operator needs each ink split into its own file, which counts as real consumers #2/#3 alongside Colornet #1 (this repo's own "extract at the 2nd consumer" convention, per `shared/pollen-engine.js`/`shared/radial.js`/`shared/shapes.js`'s own extraction-history comments); build registration marks now, not defer again.
  - **`shared/print-size.js` additions** — `lpiToPx(lpi,dpi) = dpi/lpi`: a screen ruling's cell size in raster px, with **no dependency on trim size** (a worked derivation is in the code comment) — the actual fix for Colornet's "Lineature" control, which previously divided an arbitrary internal mask-resolution width by the UI value. `registrationMarks`/`registrationMarksSVG`/`drawRegistrationMarksCanvas` — a crosshair-in-circle glyph (unfilled circle + 4 short arm ticks starting at the circle's edge, not crossing into it) placed at the **midpoint of each trim edge** (top/bottom/left/right centre), not the corners crop marks already occupy — guarantees zero collision by construction and gives a print operator both an X-shift and a Y-shift reference. Silently withholds marks (returns `[]`) when the bleed is too small to fit them without clipping.
  - **`shared/palette.js` additions** — `rmxColor`'s own posterize/random/tonernd branches (already computing a discrete index into `colors[]`) pulled out into `rmxIndex(score,rnd,colors,submode)` (returns `null` for `'tone'`, a continuous blend with no single index), which `rmxColor` now delegates to — verified byte-identical across a 140-case battery (4 submodes × 7 scores × 5 rnd values) before/after. `isSplittable(mode,submode)` is the one-line UI gate (`mode==='rmx' && submode!=='tone'`) Pollen/Spore's new Plates section uses.
  - **New `shared/plate-export.js`** — `Organica.plateExport.run(count, {build,stagger,onDone})` generalizes Colornet's own inline `setTimeout(…,i*220)` per-channel PNG loop into a reusable driver; `build(i)` may return a plain `{blob,filename}` or a Promise of one (Print-mode PNG plates need an async `arrayBuffer()` round trip to embed a pHYs chunk), returning `null`/`undefined` skips an empty plate.
  - **Colornet** — wired onto the shared Screen/Print panel (same pattern as the 5 prior tools); `channelMatrix(ch,s,W,H)` generalized (W/H default to `S.W/S.H`) with two call shapes documented in code: non-AM channels reuse the existing formula via `channelMatrix(ch,outScale)` (mask-space content scaled up, unchanged from Screen mode), AM channels via `channelMatrix(ch,1,trimWpx,trimHpx)` since their dots are generated directly in trim-px space by the new `amDotsOfPrint` (verified: passing trim dimensions into the non-AM branch would have double-applied the scale — a real bug caught by tracing the existing formula's own math before writing the fix, not assumed). `amDotsOfPrint` is `amDotsOf`'s exact formulas with the cell size from `lpiToPx` instead of `mask width / screenFreq`, sampling coverage from the *existing* mask via a `maskScale` coordinate mapping (no need to regenerate `computeBands` at print resolution). Only AM's "Lineature" becomes true lpi this phase — Noise/Dither keep their existing abstract units. `svgFromPlan` split into `svgPlanItemsMarkup` (shared, now reading `it.dots` attached at build time instead of re-deriving — removes a prior duplication between `drawPlan` and `svgFromPlan`) + a thin wrapper (verified byte-identical). New `buildPrintPlateSVG(chId)` — a real physical-mm single-channel document with bleed, crop marks, **and** registration marks (plate exports only — never the composite/non-plate export, since they're meaningless without a second plate to align against). `exportSeparations`/new `exportSeparationsSVG` migrated onto `Organica.plateExport.run`.
  - **Pollen** — new **Plates** popover section (hidden unless `colorMode==='rmx'` with a discrete submode). Classification via `Organica.palette.rmxIndex(p.b,p.rnd,rmxColors,rmxColorMap)` — the exact same `(score,rnd)` pair `pointRGBA()` already feeds `colorAt`, so a point's plate assignment can never drift from its real on-screen colour (Pollen never stores a resolved colour per point, unlike Spore). `buildPointsMarkup`/`buildSVG`/`buildPrintSVG`/`paintPoints` gained an optional `forceInk` override (paints solid black, full opacity, no background rect — transparent, matching Colornet's own plate convention) and `buildPrintSVG` an `opts.registrationMarks` flag, both defaulted off so every existing call site is unaffected.
  - **Spore** — same feature, but classification is a plain colour-equality filter (`marks.filter(m => m.color === rmxColors[i])`) since Spore's marks already store a resolved `.color` string at generation time, and the 3 discrete submodes always store one of the literal `rmxColors[]` strings unchanged — no `rmxIndex` re-derivation needed. `buildSporeMarksMarkup`/`buildSporeSVG`/`buildPrintSporeSVG`/`paintSporeMarksScaled` gained the equivalent `overrides`/`forceColor` shape.
  - **Verified end-to-end in-browser**: a 65lpi test at 300dpi produced the exact expected cell size (`300/65`) and — the actual point of "real" lpi — the identical dot count at 600dpi (dot count depends only on trim size and lpi, not raster resolution, confirming the physical correctness of the fix); every plate PNG's dimensions/pHYs chunk matched the expected math; every plate SVG's crop-mark (8 lines) and registration-mark (16 lines + 4 circles) counts matched exactly, for circle/line/euclidean AM dot shapes; Pollen/Spore's point/mark-count accounting summed exactly to the total RMX-mode count with zero unmatched/dropped marks; tone-mode and non-rmx-mode correctly hide the Plates section in both tools; Screen mode confirmed byte-identical to pre-phase behaviour in Colornet (a fixed gradient-image + AM-channel scenario hashed identically for both the composite SVG and rendered canvas, before and after the whole change) and in Pollen/Spore (regular non-plate export unaffected — background rect present, no registration marks). A missing `shared/plate-export.js` script include in Pollen was caught by this same testing before it shipped. `python3 scripts/css-lint.py` stayed clean throughout. Docs: `docs/SHARED-COMPONENTS.md` (Print size row extended, new Plate-export row), `CLAUDE.md` (this note, Repo Structure, Tools table rows for Colornet/Pollen/Spore). 9 commits on `staging` (palette.js, print-size.js, plate-export.js, Colornet, Pollen, Spore, docs) — not yet pushed to `main`, pending Diego's "porta in prod".

- **September 7, 2026 — DPI + bleed foundation shipped: `shared/print-size.js` + `print-size-panel.js`, wired into Loom/Pollen/Spore/Halide/FVS.** Part of the print-production evaluation (screen-print/riso output) flagged in the MVL Organica2 commercialization notes — real physical dimensions, a verifiable DPI, and real bleed are a genuine prerequisite for that direction, and (later) for Colornet's own halftone screen-frequency math to mean anything. **Design correction mid-plan (Diego):** the first draft used one unit dropdown (px/mm/in) that silently grew extra fields when a physical unit was picked — technically backward-compatible, but it framed print export as a hidden extra state of the normal export rather than a real, visible second thing you can do. Corrected to an explicit, always-visible **Screen | Print** `.seg-ctrl` mode switch (Screen = today's export, exactly, no revealed fields at all) before implementation began.
  - **`shared/print-size.js`** — pure classic-script math, no DOM, no `export` statements (Loom's ES modules read it as a bare global like every other `Organica.*`, since a classic `<script>` loading first is enough — no `import` needed, confirmed against how Loom already consumes `Organica.noise`/`Organica.download`). `UNIT_TO_MM` (mm/cm/m/in — `in` is new, no tool exposed inches before this), `toMM`/`mmToPx`/`pxToMm`, a from-scratch PNG `pHYs`-chunk writer `embedPngDpi` (parses the signature+IHDR, splices a 9-byte pixels-per-metre payload right after IHDR with a locally-implemented CRC32 — verified byte-level via a Python-side chunk parser), and unit-agnostic `bleedBox`/`cropMarks`/`cropMarksSVG`/`drawCropMarksCanvas` (8 short corner marks, offset into the bleed zone). `lpiToPx`/registration marks/JPG-DPI (JFIF, a different writer) deliberately not built — named in the file's own header as later, not silently skipped.
  - **`shared/print-size-panel.js`** — `Organica.printSizePanel(target, opts)` builds the one shared `.panel-section`: a Mode `.seg-ctrl` (Screen/Print) + unit(mm|in)/size/DPI/bleed rows that stay `display:none` until Print is active, returning `{getMode,getUnit,getSize,getDpi,getBleed,setPixelPreview}`. First real consumer: Pollen (Loom built its own bespoke Screen/Print split instead — it already had a real unit selector, mm/cm/m/in, so promoting it into the shared panel would've meant discarding working infra for no gain).
  - **Loom** — canvas panel gets a Mode row above the existing Width/Height fields; Unit (now mm/cm/m/in only — `px` moved to being implicit in Screen mode, no longer a `<select>` option) and a new DPI field only show in Print. Both renderers now genuinely read `canvas.bleed` (previously stored, displayed, **never read** by either renderer — a real gap, not cosmetic): `raster-renderer.js`/`svg-renderer.js` grow the exported bitmap/SVG to `trim + 2×bleed`, translate trim content by `(bleed,bleed)`, flat-fill the full bleed box, and draw crop marks at the real trim corners when bleed > 0 — v1 discipline is background extension + crop marks only, no per-generator edge extrapolation (a Voronoi cell has no natural "continue past the edge" rule). The SVG's trim-content `<g transform="translate(bleed,bleed)">` wrapper is **unconditional** (identity translate at bleed 0) so `main.js`'s existing Overlay-grid string-splice point (`'</g></svg>'`, was `'</svg>'`) never has to move depending on bleed state — a bug caught in design, before any live testing, by tracing through the existing splice mechanism. `exportPNG()` derives its raster scale directly from DPI (`dpi/25.4`, exact since `canvas.width` is already the canonical mm-equivalent number canvas-manager.js's own header documents) instead of the old scale-multiplier, and pipes the PNG through `embedPngDpi` in Print mode.
  - **Pollen/Spore/Halide/FVS** — each tool's raster export function gains a Print branch: bleed-inclusive canvas, flat-fill + crop marks, `embedPngDpi`. Each tool's SVG export was split into a shared inner-content builder (`buildPointsMarkup`/`buildSVGInner`/`buildSporeMarksMarkup`+`sporeMarksContext`/`buildComponentSVGBody` — a pure refactor, verified byte-identical to the pre-split output in every tool) + the pre-existing Screen-mode wrapper, plus a new `buildPrint*SVG()` that wraps the *same* inner markup in a physical-mm bleed box with crop marks. Real per-tool wrinkles: Spore's `exportPNG()` had **no real Print path at all before this** (`canvas.toDataURL()` of the live preview canvas can't carry a `pHYs` chunk) — it now re-renders the stored marks via the same replay loop `exportJPG()` already used (factored out as `paintSporeMarksScaled()`), and `exportJPG()`'s own native-resolution heuristic now also honours Print mode's size (no embedded density — JPG DPI is the one deferred piece, per the shared module's header). Halide's `outputDims()`'s existing `block` concept (px-per-work-unit) turned out to generalise directly to the SVG path: a Print-mode "block" is simply mm-per-work-unit instead of px-per-work-unit, so `buildSVGInner(block)` needed no separate vector-vs-raster code path — just a different multiplier; a latent "100%"-sized background rect was also fixed to an explicit `workW*block`/`workH*block` (byte-identical in Screen mode, where the two were always numerically equal, but load-bearing once that markup can sit inside a bleed wrapper whose own `<svg>` is bigger than the trim content). FVS's trim is the **selected Component's own square frame**, not a canvas, so its panel title reads "Print (selection)" rather than the generic "Output" — flagged as a real wrinkle during planning, confirmed correct in the shipped UI.
  - **Verified end-to-end in-browser, all 5 tools**, at a shared reference case (100×80mm / 300dpi / 5mm bleed, or Loom's own 300×300mm since its default Bento grid needs a real canvas floor — confirmed a genuine pre-existing "canvas too small for Kiwi" guard, unrelated to this work, while probing a too-small case): PNG dimensions match the exact expected pixel math (`(trim+2·bleed)/25.4·dpi`, e.g. 1299×1063 in the shared case) with a correct `pHYs` chunk (11811 ppu, unit=1) parsed byte-by-byte from the raw file; the sampled bleed-zone pixel is genuinely opaque and colour-matches the tool's own background/paper swatch (not transparent, not left un-painted); SVG output carries the right `width/height="…mm"` + `viewBox`, an unconditional `translate(bleed,bleed)` group, and exactly 8 crop-mark `<line>` elements when bleed > 0. **Screen mode confirmed byte-identical to pre-change behaviour in every tool** — no `pHYs` chunk, no crop marks, identical pixel dimensions, no unit suffix on SVG `width`/`height`. `python3 scripts/css-lint.py` stayed clean throughout (Sep 6's own audit + lint infra). Docs: `docs/SHARED-COMPONENTS.md` §3 new "Print size / DPI" row, `CLAUDE.md` (this note, Repo Structure, Tools table rows for the 5 tools). Committed in 5 separate commits (shared modules + Loom together, then one per remaining tool) — not yet pushed, pending Diego's "commit in prod".

- **September 6, 2026 — Repo-wide CSS/markup audit, cleanup, and the rules + lint to stop it recurring.** Diego: audit the inline styles and page-level CSS across every tool, decide what to change (including "the performance of class imports"), update the design-system page so the UI is simple and clean, verify the token naming/semantics still hold, and **write rules for future migrations and new tools**. Prompted by three bugs in one week that all had the same shape — a tool carrying local CSS that quietly disagreed with the shared system, found only by hand-diffing computed styles against Loom. Scope agreed via AskUserQuestion: all phases, Genesis limited to its type scale, plus the non-CSS performance items and the mobile gate everywhere. **Ten commits, each independently verifiable.**
  - **Method.** A temporary harness (`_css-audit.html`, deleted after) iframed all 23 tools at 1440×900 and recorded 49 selectors × 27 properties per page, diffing before/after. The governing rule: a commit either **deletes provably-dead code** — and then the sweep must come back *IDENTICAL*, which is the proof it was dead — **or changes appearance**, and then it needs screenshots and a functional check. Never both. Phases 2, 3a/b/c, 4 and 8a all returned IDENTICAL; phases 1, 5, 6, 7 changed appearance deliberately and were screenshotted and exercised.
  - **Three real bugs (Phase 1).** (1) **Genesis's floatbar sat 6px off-centre.** `floatbar.css` centred on `var(--panel-w, var(--panel-width-right, 248px))`, whose inner fallback *could never fire* — `panel.css` sets `--panel-w` on `:root` unconditionally and Genesis links it, so Genesis's real 260px panel was ignored. Replaced with an explicit opt-in, `--floatbar-offset`; **a fallback chain cannot detect "this tool is different"**. Verified: floatbar-centre vs canvas-centre delta 0px (was 6), all 22 other tools byte-identical. (2) **`.org-check` was defined in both `header.css` (15px) and `panel.css` (13px)** — panel won on *specificity* (0,2,1 vs 0,1,1), not load order, so reordering links would not have helped; its only consumer (halide) has it inside an `.org-popover`, a header-family component, so header.css is now sole owner. (3) **`.icon-btn` moved from `palette.css` to `panel.css`** — 7 consumers, only one palette-related, and `palette.css` is opt-in. *The audit's premise here was wrong and was corrected in place*: colornet was **not** unstyled, it carried its own complete copy; the move turned that into a partial override (the same trap as mote below), so it was reduced to its four genuine deltas expressed through the component's own `--icon-btn-w/h` tuning vars.
  - **Dead code (Phase 2), all grep-verified at 0 consumers**: `.org-app`/`.org-drop-hint`/`.org-zoom-hud` (shell), `.section-label`/`.panel-label--group`/`.org-panel__title`/`.row .lbl`/`.group-label` + the `.sec h3`/`aside h3`/`.panel h3` aliases (panel), `.org-sep` (header), `.org-floatbar__btn--primary`/`--rec` (floatbar), and the tokens `--sans`/`--lh-tight`/`--ls-tight`/`--fs-display`. Two judgment calls settled rather than deferred: `--fs-display` described a `clamp(1.5rem, 9vw, 13rem)` hero wordmark, but the hub's is a 0.95rem corner logo — **the scale comment documenting it was the real liability** and went too; `.org-header__title`/`__sub` were **kept** and labelled archive-only, since their only consumer is the preserved `genesis/archive/indicators-55.html` and deleting the CSS would silently rot an artifact rather than break it visibly.
  - **Duplication (Phase 3).** Byte-identical `:root` palette blocks in mycel/tunesutra/design-system; local rules restating `shell.css` in membrane/vortex/colornet/design-system; camo-turing's copy of `seeds-panel.css`. **Two audit claims did not survive checking and were deliberately not acted on:** pollen/spore "use `.shape-*` without linking seeds-panel.css" — they have *different* components sharing two class names (pollen keys off `#shape-grid`, uses `.selected` not `.active`, adds a hover; spore's is `.mark-thumb`), so linking the sheet would collide rather than tidy; and tunesutra "duplicates palette.css" — its `.rmx-color` is expressed purely through the documented `--rmx-cell-*` tuning vars, which is the supported mechanism. Also found: membrane's and vortex's `#canvas-wrap` comments claimed "the p5 canvas fills the wrap (no padding)" — **it never did**, omitting padding inherits shell's `var(--space-7)` rather than overriding it; the dead rule was deleted and the comment corrected rather than the padding silently changed.
  - **Phase 4.** Load order normalised in 6 tools — checked first rather than assumed: `panel.css` and `floatbar.css` share **zero** selectors and floatbar's popover rules are `.org-floatbar .org-popover` (more specific, order-independent), so it was a genuine no-op. `letter-spacing: 0` ×11 → `--ls-normal`. Colour literals → tokens; rhizome's `.rz-wire.is-selected` was `#c93ed6`, **TuneSutra's identity hue copy-pasted** — not repointed to rhizome's own `--tool` (unselected wires already use it, which would have erased the selection state) but to `--accent-warm`. New `--accent-hover` for a raw `#333` written twice. Legacy type aliases retired in full.
  - **Phase 5 — one presets picker.** Five tools showed one; three used the shared component, two carried a drifted copy, and **mote had a different component wearing the shared class names** — it redefined `.preset-trigger`/`.preset-menu` while setting only *some* properties, so its padding/background/border came from `panel.css` by accident and any future edit there would have changed mote silently. That was the real find. Reconciled **per property, not per file**: Living Path/Sinew won three (`.pt-ico` 22px split from `.pi-ico` 26, `--radius-sm` on trigger/menu/item, the hover tint), shared won three (padding, shadow, scoped transition). Mote renamed to `.mote-preset-*`; dropping back to `.panel-select` — which its markup carried all along — *improved* it, to exactly `--row-h` with `--border-strong`. Every picker then opened and picked from for real, since `position: fixed` menus only prove their anchor math on a live click.
  - **Phases 6–8a.** mycel + tunesutra onto `shell.css` (both were the same undocumented-hand-roll state that caused Living Path's shipped `body{font-size}` bug — both were running the browser's 16px default; the change is invisible because all 16 affected elements are unpainted SVG `<title>` tooltips, checked rather than assumed). Mobile gate on all 22 tools, deliberately **not** on privacy/terms/sign-in/design-system. Genesis's parallel `--fs-xs/-sm/-md` scale retired — 26 uses, byte-identical output verified by a full per-element sweep of all three Genesis shells (548/548/563 elements, zero diffs).
  - **Performance, honestly (Phase 9).** The CSS cleanup is worth ~0.5 KB gzipped and was done for correctness, not speed — all 8 shared sheets are 20.6 KB gzipped, and **bundling was considered and rejected** (saves 1–2 KB, costs per-file caching, needs a build step this project has never had). The real win: **`vercel.json` had no `headers` block at all**, so every tool-to-tool navigation re-validated 8 stylesheets, `core.js` and a 165 KB font — 10+ conditional round-trips per load, forever. Added `immutable` for `shared/vendor/` and `max-age=300, stale-while-revalidate=86400` for `shared/*.css|js` (order matters: Vercel applies every matching rule and the last wins, and vendor also holds `.js`). Font `preload` on all 29 pages. **WOFF2 deferred with a finding the plan didn't have**: the `.ttf` has a second consumer — opentype.js reads it for glyph extraction in `seeds-panel.js` and `membrane/js/seeds/text.js`, and cannot parse WOFF2 — so it is an *addition*, not a conversion, and needs its own session (fontTools also isn't installed here).
  - **The design-system page now checks itself (Phase 10).** Token tables are generated from the CSSOM instead of typed (typed tables are exactly how `UI-SHELL.md`'s panel type table came to have 3 of 4 rows wrong); an orphan check lists any `:root` token the page fails to document; and component contracts are asserted against the live demos, with a red banner on failure. **It immediately found 17 undocumented tokens** — the entire `--t-*` role layer plus the line-height and letter-spacing scales — which are now documented. Verified it catches a real regression by simulating `--row-h: 30px`. Stale claims corrected: "Five shared files" → eight (and the page is now the *single* place that list is written; UI-SHELL and SHARED-COMPONENTS link to it, which is why the repo had four different answers); `.org-header--catalog`/`--editor` marked deprecated (their documented consumers are all redirect stubs now); the floatbar centring note rewritten.
  - **The rules (Phase 11).** New **`docs/CSS-RULES.md`** — creating a tool, migrating onto a shared component, when a value may be hardcoded, what earns a place in `shared/`, and how aliases are introduced *and retired* — each rule paired with the bug it came from. Executable half: **`scripts/css-lint.py`**, 7 checks. Two design decisions matter more than the checks: it distinguishes **redefining** a shared class (`.hint {}`, page-wide) from **scoping** one (`.fvs-cell-group .ctrl-row {}`, anchored on something the tool owns) — without that the first run produced 62 findings, almost all false — and it separates **blocking regressions** from **known debt**, so Genesis's deferred panel re-implementation prints every run without failing the build. A lint that is green only because everything is allow-listed tells you nothing. Currently **clean**.
  - **Two harness bugs worth remembering**, both the same class of error as the cleanup itself — a measurement that looked authoritative and wasn't: a `display:none` iframe reports `innerWidth: 0`, which silently matches every `max-width` media query (the first mobile-gate sweep showed the gate firing on all 23 pages at a nominal 1440px); and splitting property values on spaces hid a real change inside `calc(50% - 130px)`, making a fixed bug look like a no-op. Both are written up in `CSS-RULES.md`.
  - **Still deferred, with reasons recorded**: Genesis's 47-selector panel re-implementation and its 19 inline `.panel-unit` width overrides (those fight a shared rule — "cleaning" them would silently break the layout); the ~195 inline `display:none` toggles (all JS-driven, need an `Organica.show()` helper first); WOFF2; and the hub's 1.48 MB `index.html`, which dwarfs everything here.

- **September 5, 2026 (later still) — Living Path/Sinew: markup rewritten to the real `id="panel"`/`class="panel-section"`, replacing a CSS alias.** Diego rejected the previous fix (aliasing `.panel`/`.sec` onto `#panel`/`.panel-section` in `shared/panel.css`) and asked for the markup itself to be brought in line with Loom's actual definitions — the old class names were the real problem, not something to paper over with more CSS. Reverted the `panel.css` alias. In both `sinew/index.html` and `livingpath/index.html`: `<aside class="panel right">` → `<div id="panel">` (matches Loom's own `<div id="panel">` exactly — dropped the now-redundant `right` variant and its local `.panel`/`.panel.right` CSS rules entirely, since the shared `#panel` shell rule already assumes right-hand placement); every `<div class="sec">` → `<div class="panel-section">` (5 sections per tool), dropping the local `.sec h3{margin:0 0 8px}` override in favor of the shared rule's own `margin:0 0 var(--space-4)` (10px, was 8px — a 2px difference, imperceptible); the stale comment claiming `.sec` was "already aliased by shared/panel.css" (false — only `.sec h3` was) rewritten to describe what's actually still aliased (`.row`/`.row .val`). Updated the one JS reference in each file, `document.querySelector('.panel.right')` (the preset-menu-close scroll listener) → `document.getElementById('panel')`. Verified `.panel`/`.sec` were used by no other tool in the repo before touching shared CSS. **Verified**: `document.getElementById('panel')` truthy and `.panel-section` count 5 in both tools, zero remaining `.sec`/`.panel` class matches; screenshots show the same section padding/dividers as the CSS-alias attempt (visually unchanged, now for the right reason); Genesis-grid interaction in Sinew still expands correctly inside the section padding; console clean (only the pre-existing `_vercel/insights` 404); both files pass a Node syntax check. **Not committed yet** — pending Diego's "commit in prod."

- **September 5, 2026 (later still) — Living Path/Sinew: the actual "Presets dropdown" bug Diego first flagged, found and fixed.** Diego asked for a rigorous style/class comparison between Loom's right column and Sinew's, testing the code rather than eyeballing it — a repeat of the exact request that led to the border-radius/body-font-size note above. Re-ran the computed-style diff against Loom, this time widening it to the effect-stack-specific classes (`.layer`, `.group`, `.preset-trigger`, `.preset-item`, `.mini`, `#techSeg .seg-btn`, `.chips button`). Found the real cause of the original complaint: `.preset-trigger` and `.preset-item` (the "Choose a preset…" dropdown button and its menu rows) were set to `font-size:var(--fs-medium)` (12px) in both tools, while every sibling control in the same panel — `.panel-select`, `.mini-btn`, `.panel-input`, `.seg-btn` — sits on `--fs-small`/`--t-control-size` (10px); body baseline is 11px. A visibly larger dropdown next to normal-sized selects and buttons is exactly "lo stile è diverso dalle altre." Fixed: `--fs-medium` → `--fs-small` on both selectors, in both `livingpath/index.html` and `sinew/index.html`. **Also resolved, this time by tracing the actual code path instead of asserting "pre-existing, not a bug"**: the earlier "non si vedono più le thumbnails" report. Read `presetIcon()`/`sampleSRC()` directly — a preset's preview icon is generated by running that preset against a real sample glyph (`window.__FONT.charToGlyph('g'|'a')` in Living Path) or a sample source shape (Sinew), so with **no font/SVG/Genesis form loaded yet** there's no geometry to render and the icon is legitimately a blank white square — not a CSS regression. Verified by loading the vendored Manrope into Living Path (`loadFontBuffer`) and a Genesis "flower bloom" form into Sinew: every preset thumbnail rendered correctly (a distorted "g" per preset in Living Path, a distorted flower per preset in Sinew) — confirming the earlier "pre-existing, not a bug" call was right for the *thumbnails*, while the dropdown text size was a genuine, previously-undetected miss. **Verified overall**: `.preset-trigger`/`.preset-item`/`.pt-name`/`.pi-name` now compute to 10px in both tools; screenshots show the dropdown reading at the same density as every other control; selecting a preset (Living Path "Eroded", loaded font) correctly rebuilds the effect stack; console clean on both tools; both files pass a Node syntax check. **Not committed yet** — pending Diego's "commit in prod."

- **September 5, 2026 (later still) — Living Path/Sinew: two more real style mismatches found by diffing computed styles against Loom.** After the design-system alignment pass (previous note), Diego flagged the Presets dropdown as visually inconsistent. Rather than eyeball it, diffed `getComputedStyle()` of matching selectors (`.panel-section h3`, `.ctrl-row`, `.ctrl-label`, `.panel-select`, `.seg-btn`, `.mini-btn`, `.color-row`) between Loom (a tool that links `shared/shell.css` and uses shared classes with zero local overrides — confirmed by reading its own `<style>` block, which contains only canvas/margin-grid/json-view rules, nothing panel-related) and Sinew. Found two real, measurable mismatches, both pre-existing (confirmed via `git show`, present before today's session too): (1) **every custom class in Living Path/Sinew's own CSS used `--radius-lg` (8px)** — the preset dropdown, `.layer`/`.group` cards, dropzones, `.blendsel`, `.mini` — while every shared component (`.panel-select`/`.seg-btn`/`.mini-btn`/`.color-swatch`) uses `--radius-sm` (2px), and `shared/panel.css`'s own `.org-layer-card` (the closest shared analog to `.layer`/`.group`) has no radius at all. This was the actual cause of "lo stile è diverso dalle altre" — a systemically softer/rounder look next to sharp-cornered shared controls. Fixed: all 11 (Living Path) / 9 (Sinew) `--radius-lg` references → `--radius-sm`. (2) **`body{font-size:var(--fs-large)}` (14px)** in both tools, vs `shared/shell.css`'s own `body{font-size:var(--fs-base)}` (11px) that every shell.css-linked tool (Loom included) inherits — confirmed via computed-style diff that `.row`/`.ctrl-row` itself rendered at 14px in Sinew vs Loom's 11px (the row container has no explicit font-size of its own in `panel.css`, so it was silently inheriting the wrong body baseline). Living Path/Sinew don't link `shell.css` (own canvas surface, like Genesis/Rhizome/FVS), so they never got its `body` rule and had carried their own — at the wrong size — since before this session. Fixed: `--fs-large` → `--fs-base` in both. **Verified**: re-ran the same computed-style diff post-fix — `.row` now reads 11px in both tools matching Loom exactly; `.mini-btn`/`.panel-btn`/`.hint`/`.color-swatch`/`.color-name` all matched the shared spec byte-for-byte (colors, radii, min-heights) in the same pass. Screenshots confirm the panel now reads at the same density as Loom/every other aligned tool. Full functional re-check (load a real font, switch Raster, apply a preset) still works, console clean on both tools. **Not committed yet** — pending Diego's "commit in prod."

- **September 5, 2026 (later still) — Living Path + Sinew: UI-controls debug and design-system alignment.** Diego asked for a debug pass on the controls UI plus alignment to the design system. Both tools (Sinew inherited the pattern when it was split from Living Path earlier the same session) redeclared the entire surface palette locally under Living-Path-only names (`--card`/`--bg`/`--ink-soft`/`--ink-muted`/`--line`/`--line-soft`/`--pollen`/`--pollen-bright`) instead of the shared tokens, several at values that quietly diverged from the system defaults (`--mid` was `#726a5e` vs the system's contrast-audited `#696254`; `--line` was black-alpha vs the system's warm-tan `--border`). Worse, a **local `--accent:#b0532a` override collided with the shared system's own `--accent`** — `shared/panel.css`'s `.ctrl-label`/`.color-name`/`.seg-btn` all read `--accent` as "control-label text colour, near-black," but Living Path had redefined it to mean "danger/delete red" for its own `.gdel`/remove-button styling — the same token name, two conflicting semantics. Root cause: `shared/panel.css` already ships alias rules mapping Living Path's own historic class names (`.sec`→section, `.row`→`.ctrl-row`, `.row .val`→`.ctrl-val`) to the shared system *by design* (its own header comment: "adopting this is 'link the file, delete the local copy' rather than a markup rewrite") — but Living Path's local CSS block still fully overrode those aliases with its own bespoke values, so linking the shared sheet bought nothing. **Fixed**: stripped both `:root` blocks down to `--tool` + the one legitimate optional override panel.css itself supports (`--border-strong`) — every local color reference remapped to the real shared token (`--card`→`--panel`, `--bg`→`--paper`, `--ink-soft`→`--ink`, `--ink-muted`→`--mid`, `--line`/`--line-soft`→`--border`, `--pollen`/`--pollen-bright`→`--tool` — each tool's own identity hue now drives its own "active/selected" affordance, matching how `.check-row input[type=checkbox]{accent-color:var(--tool)}` already works elsewhere); the `--accent` collision resolved by deleting the local override entirely and repointing the 2 real delete-affordance call sites (`.gdel`, the inline-styled "remove" button) to `var(--danger)`, the token that already exists for exactly this semantic. **Markup gaps** (classes referenced by the shared CSS that the markup never actually used, so the shared rules were silently inert): `#techSeg`'s buttons were bare `<button class="on">` with no `.seg-btn` class at all — `shared/panel.css`'s real segmented-control styling (black-fill active state, proper border) never applied; now `class="seg-btn"` + `.active` (was `.on`, which nothing in the shared system recognizes). Every row's "Text"/"Font name"/"Include"/per-effect-slider label was a bare `<label>` — `panel.css`'s `.ctrl-label` alias list is `.ctrl-label, .panel-label`, deliberately **not** bare `label` (confirmed against Loom/Trellis's own convention: `<div class="ctrl-label">Width</div>`, not a semantic `<label>`) — so every label in both tools was rendering at full body size/weight instead of the system's small/light row-label type; converted to `<div class="ctrl-label">`. `.field`→`.panel-input.panel-input--full`; `.chk`→`.check-row` (2 checkboxes); the primary "Export modified font (OTF)" button and the "+ new group (overlap)" button were both `class="btn ..."` referencing a **local `.btn` rule that itself only existed because of long-dead `.top`/`.brand`/`.home`/`.status` header CSS from before Living Path adopted the shared `org-header` component** (grepped: zero markup used those classes) — `.btn`/`.btn.pri` deleted along with the dead header rules, buttons repointed to `.panel-btn` (primary) and `.mini-btn` (paired Save/Load .lvp, wrapped in the shared `.row-btns`). **Real functional bug, not just cosmetic**: `.gdel{color:var(--accent)}` (and Sinew's copy) never actually painted red even before this pass, in the *original* code — `.mini{color:var(--ink-soft)}` and `.gdel{color:var(--accent)}` have equal specificity, and `.mini`'s rule came later in the stylesheet, so it silently won the cascade regardless of which token `--accent` pointed to; fixed by upgrading the selector to `.mini.gdel{color:var(--danger)}` (two classes beats one, order-independent). **Real accessibility bug**: `Organica.autoLabelPanel(document)` runs once at boot, but `renderStack()` rebuilds every layer/group `.row` from scratch on any edit (add/remove/reorder an effect, switch technique) — every freshly-built slider had zero accessible name past that point. Fixed by calling `Organica.autoLabelPanel(host)` (scoped to `#stack`) at the end of `renderStack()` in both tools. Caught a copy-paste slip while editing: an `.exName` text-input rewrite briefly dropped its `value="LivingPath"` default — caught and restored before it shipped. **Verified**: screenshots of both tools' full control surface (Engine segmented control, Input drop-zone, per-layer sliders, group cards, "remove"/"×" delete buttons, Save/Load) — labels now read at the correct small/light weight, active states show the tool's own accent (violet for Living Path, berry magenta for Sinew) instead of a shared amber "pollen," delete affordances render in the real `--danger` red; a direct DOM check confirmed every dynamically-rebuilt slider/color-row control now carries a real `aria-labelledby` resolving to readable text; the Rhizome "Sinew Preset" bridge node still round-trips correctly (raster + vector presets) through the restyled iframe. Console clean on both tools and Rhizome throughout, bar the pre-existing `_vercel/insights` 404. Pure CSS/markup pass — no JS logic touched beyond the 2 `autoLabelPanel` calls, so the engine/preset behavior verified in the earlier same-day sessions is unaffected. **Not committed yet** — pending Diego's "commit in prod."

- **September 5, 2026 (later) — Living Path/Sinew: fixed a real raster-engine bug (counter collapse on thin-weight glyphs) + softened Vortex's default.** Diego asked to investigate how to get better font transformations out of Living Path and visually check what the Vector/Raster engines actually produce. Investigated empirically (not just by reading code): loaded the vendored Manrope (ExtraLight — the only weight available offline) and ran all 29 presets through `processGlyphEm` against counter-bearing test glyphs (`g o a e B 8 O A R &`), diffing contour counts to mechanically detect a closed counter, then confirmed the worst offenders with real screenshots of "Reg8a". **Finding 1 — the Vector engine (`FX`) is reliably safe for type**: no preset ever changed contour topology; "Eroded"/"Type-safe" read as clean, fully legible hand-etched text. One exception: "Vortex" (`twist` 90°/70%) processes each glyph independently in its own EM box, so a multi-letter word spins every letter around its own centre — striking on one big glyph, illegible as a word. **Finding 2 — the Raster engine had a real, measurable defect**: `RES` (170, `shared/pathfx.js`) is shared by both the live preview *and* the actual OTF export (no separate hi-fidelity export pass), and at ExtraLight weight the narrowest counter gap in `g`/`R`/`a` measured **1 raster pixel wide** — so any preset with a positive `dilate` (11 of 24) reliably sealed it into a solid blob, confirmed visually (`Reg8a` under "Avulsion": `R`/`g` unreadable smudges next to a perfectly fine `e`/`8`/`a`). Erode-only presets ("Thin line") were unaffected; heavy-texture presets (Frog-eggs, Cahn cells, Dotty *, Gridouille, Coral) have the *same* underlying collapse but their particle/noise overlay incidentally re-punches small holes near the same spot, so they read as fine almost by luck. Raising `RES` outright wasn't viable — every preset's `amount`/`radius`/`block`/`spread` is calibrated in pixels of the 170 grid, so a blanket bump would silently weaken every preset's relative strength (confirmed: the same `dilate amount:1` goes from "guaranteed to close a 1px gap" to "barely dilates" between RES 170→800) and need every preset re-tuned. **Fix (AskUserQuestion → adaptive per-glyph resolution)**: `shared/pathfx.js` gained `measureMinGap(g)` (narrowest bounded, edge-excluding gap in a rasterized field) and `pickAdaptiveRes(subs, {targetGap=4, maxRes=600})` (one extra rasterize at the base RES to measure; only glyphs whose gap is already `< targetGap` get re-rasterized at a higher, capped resolution — everything else is untouched, byte-identical) — exported alongside the existing engine functions; `rasterize(subs, res)` gained an optional `res` param (default RES, so every non-opted-in caller is unaffected). Wired into **three** call sites that all previously shared one fixed `RES`: Living Path's and Sinew's `rasterCompute()` (live preview), Living Path's `processGlyphEm` (specimen/typedLayout/the `buildModifiedFont` non-Worker fallback), and — the one that actually ships — the OTF-export Web Worker's `processGlyphW`, which got its own worker-local `measureMinGapW`/`pickAdaptiveResW` pair (built on the Worker's own `rasterGlyphW`/`OffscreenCanvas`, following the same hand-adapted-duplicate pattern the worker already uses for `rasterGlyphW`/`applyLayerW` — a Worker can't share the main-thread `rasterize`). **Vortex**: softened the *preset's* override (not the underlying `twist` effect's own already-mild defaults) from `{angle:90,falloff:70}` to `{angle:45,falloff:50}` — the strong look is still one slider-drag away. **Verified**: re-ran the full 24-raster-preset × 5-wide-counter-glyph (`e o O 8 &`) hash battery against `git show HEAD` — `e`/`o`/`O` (already-adequate counters) came back **byte-identical** in every preset, confirming zero regression for normal text; `8`/`&` (genuinely thin, same finding as `g`/`R`/`a`) correctly changed. Screenshots: `g`/`R` under "Avulsion"/"Faceted" now show a clearly open bowl (was a solid blob); a real Manrope OTF export via the Worker path (preset "Avulsion") re-parsed to confirm `g`/`R` now have 3 subpaths each, matching the vector baseline exactly (was collapsed); Vortex's new default reads as a legible word on `Reg8a`. Sinew regression-checked too (same `rasterCompute` fix, Genesis-form raster preset renders unchanged). Console clean throughout, bar the pre-existing `_vercel/insights` 404. **Not committed yet** — pending Diego's "commit in prod."

- **September 5, 2026 — Living Path split into a font-only tool + a new standalone vector tool, Sinew.** Diego asked to evaluate splitting Living Path's font work from its generic SVG/vector transformation, with the vector half either becoming its own tool or folding into Genesis's shape creation. Two Explore passes first: Living Path's Vector+Raster engine turned out to be **100% font-agnostic already** — every effect (`FX`/`RFX`, the raster pipeline, groups/blend, all 24 presets) operates on a generic `subs` polyline structure regardless of source; only ~430 of 1855 lines are genuinely font-specific (opentype parsing, the live text specimen, OTF export + its Web Worker). Genesis's own Edit-mode Paper.js editor (`shared/paper.js` `createPaperDrawEditor`) was ruled out as a host — it's a per-anchor bezier editor (move point/handle, smooth, simplify, undo) with no boolean ops/offset/warp/procedural-effect-stack concept, and porting Living Path's model there would be a bigger rewrite than a dedicated tool, plus it would blur Genesis's own identity as a static-seed library. Decisions (AskUserQuestion, across two rounds): generic-vector half → **new standalone tool**, named **Sinew**; deliverable → analysis + a full technical plan (delivered as a plan-mode document first), then **"procedi"** to execute it in this same session.
  - **`shared/pathfx.js`** (new) — the extracted engine: `cubicPts`/`quadPts`/`fromSVGString`/`bboxOf`/`normalise`/`dFromSubs` (path model), `FX` (jitter/wobble/inflate/roughen/smooth/twist/scatter), the raster pipeline (`rasterize`/`morph`/`boxBlur`/`addNoise`/`grayScott`/`particles`/`skeleton`/`seamCarve`/`polygonize`/`contours`/`smoothPoly`/`rasterFieldToSubs`), `RFX`, `blendField`/`rasterFieldFromGroups`/`applyVectorGroups`, and `PRESETS` (24 built-ins) — moved verbatim. Two small stateful knobs genuinely read inside the effect closures (`SEED_OFFSET` — per-instance seed shift for the specimen; `CONTOUR_SMOOTH` — some presets override it) became internal module state with `setSeedOffset`/`getSeedOffset`/`setContourSmooth`/`getContourSmooth`, since both cross the shared/local boundary bidirectionally (read in shared code, written from each tool). **Not moved** — stays duplicated as tool-local orchestration in both Living Path and Sinew, the same "engine in shared/, orchestration stays local" split as `shared/radial.js` (Radial/Pulsar): the live `GROUPS`/`TECH`/`uid` document state, `addEffectToGroup`/`newGroup`/`curGroups`/`aFX`, `chainedSubs`/`rasterCompute`/`CHAIN`, `presetToGroups`, and all DOM (`buildLayerEl`/`renderStack`, preset-menu UI, `computed`/`repaint`).
  - **Gotcha found and preserved, not fixed:** Living Path's OTF-export Web Worker builds its source by `.toString()`-embedding the raster functions into a Blob (a Worker has no `Organica`, so the functions' *text* has to travel, not a reference) — including a 1-line `mulberry32` wrapper that itself calls `Organica.mulberry32(a)`, which is undefined inside the Worker's isolated scope. This looks like a bug (and may be one — untested before this session), but it's **pre-existing**, confirmed identical in `git show HEAD` before any edit. Preserved byte-for-byte: the wrapper was re-added locally in Living Path (`function mulberry32(a){ return Organica.mulberry32(a); }`) purely so the Worker's `.toString()` list still has something to embed, and every raster field function needed by the worker (`morph`/`boxBlur`/`addNoise`/`grayScott`/`particles`/`skeleton`/`seamCarve`/`polygonize`/`contours`/`smoothPoly`/`blendField`) is destructured from `Organica.pathfx` into the same names so `.toString()` yields identical source. **Verified byte-identical**: a real Manrope OTF export via the raster Worker path (preset "Grain", which exercises the `mulberry32`-via-noise code path) produced the exact same output before and after the whole refactor — 11,692 bytes, 64 glyphs, 62 non-empty — proving whatever the Worker's actual runtime behavior is, it didn't change.
  - **Living Path** (`livingpath/index.html`) trimmed to font-only: dropped the SVG/Genesis input tabs, `buildGenesis()`/`PRIMORDIAL`/the Rhizome bridge listener, and the now-dead `genesis/forms.js` + `shared/seeds-panel.js` script includes; kept the Font drop-zone, text field, specimen, charset chips, alternates/HTML-specimen checkboxes, OTF export, and `.lvp` save/load untouched. This also fixed a real pre-existing bug as a side effect: `buildGenesis()`'s per-item `div.title='form '+num` referenced an undefined `num` (a stale iteration-variable name from before `PRIMORDIAL.forEach(id=>...)` dropped its second arg), throwing on the very first Genesis form and aborting the whole grid build — confirmed identical in `git show HEAD`, so genuinely pre-existing, not introduced by this session. Removing the whole Genesis picker removed the bug's only call site.
  - **`sinew/index.html`** (new, single-file, Living Path's own shape apart from input/storage): **Input** uses the real `Organica.seedsPanel` component (`svg`+`genesis` tabs) instead of hand-rolling a picker like Living Path's own bespoke tab bar (flagged as tech debt in Living Path's own header comment) — Sinew is a fresh tool with no legacy debt, so it gets the Genesis-grid-for-free treatment Soul/Camo Turing/Membrane already have. **Engine** — identical `Organica.pathfx.*` usage, same Engine toggle / groups-and-layers UI / 24-preset menu with live icon previews (icons fall back to a cached Genesis flower-bloom sample when nothing is loaded yet, since there's no font glyph to default to). **Presets** — `Organica.presetStore('sinew')` (the standard localStorage mechanism every other tool uses — Loom/Pulsar/Trellis/Mote) instead of propagating Living Path's older bespoke `.lvp` file format; a save/load round-trip test confirmed the full state (`tech`/`groups`/`chain`/`ink`/`bg`) restores correctly. **Export** — SVG/PNG only, no OTF, no specimen. Accent `--tool:#b8467a` (berry magenta, an open hue distinct from Living Path's violet `#b48cf0` and TuneSutra's own magenta `#c93ed6`); hub nav entry added alongside Living Path in "Tracing & vectorization"; `vercel.json` rewrite; `shared/tokens.json` accent entry.
  - **Rhizome bridge repointed** (`rhizome/js/nodes/livingpath-bridge.js` → `sinew-bridge.js`): the bridge always drove the Genesis-form input path (never Living Path's Font tab), so once that path moved to Sinew the bridge had to move with it — `id`/`label` → `sinew-preset`/"Sinew Preset", `src` → `/sinew/`; `node-registry.js` import/export renamed; `docs/RHIZOME.md` bridge table updated. **Verified live in Rhizome**: added a "Sinew Preset" node (no stale "Living Path Preset" anywhere in the add-node menu), default `raster:Coral` rendered a real bubbly-texture thumbnail via the iframe round-trip; switched the param to `vector:Vortex` and got the correct pinwheel-twist shape — both techniques confirmed working through the repointed bridge, console clean throughout.
  - **Verification method throughout**: byte-identical regression via hash+length comparison of `dFromSubs(computed())` for **all 29 presets** (5 vector + 24 raster) against a fixed test shape, run against `git show HEAD`'s original file before touching anything and re-run after each phase — zero mismatches at every checkpoint (engine extraction, Living Path trim, and again against Sinew's independent implementation of the same presets). Console clean on Living Path, Sinew, the hub, and Rhizome throughout, bar the pre-existing `_vercel/insights` 404. **Not committed yet** — pending Diego's "commit in prod" for this session's work.
  - **Deferred, explicitly out of scope this session**: exposing `Organica.pathfx` as its own Rhizome node (distinct from the preset bridge); modernizing Living Path's `.lvp` format to `Organica.presetStore` (left as-is, only Sinew got the modern pattern); any new transformation vocabulary for Genesis's Edit mode (boolean ops/offset/warp) — the standalone-tool direction was chosen instead; `docs/SINEW.md` (Membrane/Vortex/Trellis precedent — write once the shape is confirmed further).

- **September 4, 2026 (later) — Trellis shipped: Phase 1 of a new "fill & animate a Loom grid" tool under Motion & growth.** Diego: "lavoriamo su un tool che faccia due cose, questo è un test: 1) usa una grid salvata per essere riempita o animata, 2) scegli come animare il contenuto e la griglia — pensalo come un link separato in motion, iniziamo con colori e forme base." Two Explore passes mapped the reusable pieces first: `Organica.loadLoomGrid` (the established grid-import contract), FVS's Symbols tier (the closest analog — per-cell placement math, all static/computed-once today), and Pulsar's track model (the closest analog for *time*-driven params, seamless-loop discipline, `renderFrameAt(tNorm)` feeding preview+PNG+video from one function).
