Decisions (AskUserQuestion): name **Trellis**; engine **redraw-per-frame like Pulsar** (not GSAP timelines — precise scrubbing/export, no dependency); shapes **FVS's `SEED_TYPES`, extracted to shared**. **`shared/shapes.js`** (new) — `triangleGeometry`/`arcGeometry`+`arcPathD`/`arcTruchetGeometry`+`arcTruchetPathD` (pure, `{d,normTx,normTy,normScale}` in a 0..100 box) + `resolveGridCells`/`resolveCellPlacement`/`cellColRow`/`frameSize`/`median`, extracted verbatim from `fvs/index.html` at Trellis's arrival (2nd consumer, the `noise.js`/`motion.js`/`radial.js`/`pollen-engine.js` "extract at 2nd consumer" rule) — the one real change is `cellColRow(grid, rawCells, gridMeta)` taking its FVS-only state (`state.symbolGrid.cells`/`.grid`) as explicit args instead of reaching into shared state; `fvs/index.html` now aliases (`const {…} = Organica.shapes`) + a 4-line `cellColRow` wrapper, zero behaviour change. **`trellis/index.html`** (new, single-file, Pulsar's own shape): **Grid** section reads a saved Loom preset (`Organica.presetStore('loom')`, the same store Loom itself writes — no JSON round-trip needed for a same-browser workflow) or an uploaded `.json`. **Content** = one shape (Triangle/Arc/Arc truchet) + Scale/Padding, filled at every cell's `resolveCellPlacement`. **Palette** = Ink (RMX chips, `colorAt(i)` index-cycling, FVS's own convention) + Paper. **Motion** = up to 2 tracks, each `{type: colour|scale, wave, period, phase, ease, staggerBy, staggerAmount}` — `trackValue`/`ease01`/`tri`/`easedCyc` are Pulsar's own wave functions ported verbatim; the per-cell phase offset (`staggerPhase`, `by: index|row|col|distance|noise`) is `shared/motion.js`'s still-"retained but unused" `staggerDelay` formula, ported as pure math (not its GSAP wrapper) + row/col added (natural for a grid). Colour track lerps two stops via `Organica.palette.mix`; Scale track multiplies the cell's placement scale — first per-frame consumer of `palette.mix`/`colorAt` in the repo (every other tool drives them from tone/index, never from a clock). `renderFrameAt(tNorm)` builds one per-cell draw list `{d,cx,cy,offsetX,offsetY,scaleX,scaleY,colour}` that canvas preview, PNG, SVG (`buildSVG`, byte-faithful at a shared `frameSize`-square frame — same convention Pollen/Mote/Loom's own SVG export uses) and video (`shared/recorder.js`, Pulsar's exact integration shape: `onStart:()=>play(true)`, lazy `duration`) all read. Element-count guard `CEIL 2000` (Pulsar's own `CEIL` precedent, lower since Trellis draws filled shapes not thin strokes). Hub: nav link in **Motion & growth**, `--tool:#c9587a` (dusty rose — an open hue gap, distinct from Membrane's coral/TuneSutra's magenta), `vercel.json` rewrite. **Verified** on the no-store dev server: FVS regression — Symbols tier (Bento 7-cell + Hexagonal 17-cell polygon grids, Oscillator rule) and Components tier (16-component Square-2×2 gallery) both render correctly, console clean (FVS exercises `resolveGridCells`'s both branches — loom rect/polygon AND the plain-square non-loom branch — plus `cellColRow`'s both branches — real col/row AND centroid-binning); Trellis loads a saved Bento (12 cells, rect) and Hexagonal (23 cells, polygon) grid correctly, all 3 shapes render, a Colour+Scale track pair staggered by index/distance produces a visibly-staggered wave at rest (t=0) and genuine motion on Play (`buildDrawList(0)` ≠ `buildDrawList(0.5)`); seamless-loop check corrected mid-verification — comparing `t=0` vs the exact boundary `t=1` isn't the real guarantee (Pulsar's own `%1` wrap never evaluates exactly 1 either), the real check is continuity approaching the wrap: `buildDrawList(0)` vs `buildDrawList(0.999999)` differ by ~4×10⁻⁶ (visually a no-op, no pop at the seam); PNG (103 KB) and SVG (12 `<g>`/12 `<path>`, `viewBox` a `frameSize`-square) export real files; console clean throughout (env 404 only). **Deferred, documented not built:** rotation/drift track types, grid-geometry animation (gap/track-size breathing, whole-grid rotate/zoom — Diego's 2nd stated axis, "animate the grid itself"), a goo-filter collective-merge track; `docs/TRELLIS.md` (Membrane/Vortex precedent — write once the shape is confirmed).
  - **Same-session follow-up — "nulla si muove."** Debugged with real UI interaction (clicks/`form_input`, not just synthetic events) rather than the JS-driven repro above. Root cause: **both Motion tracks default to Off**, so a fresh grid load + Play correctly animates nothing — indistinguishable from broken. The engine itself was never broken: per-cell `buildDrawList` scores at `tNorm=0.25` on a 12-cell Bento ranged `scaleX` 1.39→2.58 across cells (real per-cell stagger), and the actual painted triangle bounding boxes matched 1:1 (h 100px→184px) — confirmed by measuring canvas pixels directly, not by eyeballing screenshots (two screenshots taken ~2 s apart during real playback looked deceptively similar at a glance despite genuine, provable pixel-level change — a reminder that verifying animation visually needs a pixel-diff, not a glance). **Fix:** Track 1 now defaults to **Scale pulse** (index 0's `<option>` `selected` moved from Off), Track 2 stays Off; added a hint line under the Motion header ("A track set to **Off** does nothing — pick Colour cycle or Scale pulse to animate the grid; press Play…"). **Verified:** fresh grid load → Play, zero manual setup → real measured motion (92→189 ink pixels in one row over 1.5 s); console clean.
  - **Same-session follow-up — "margini superiori alti."** Diego flagged an asymmetric top margin around the loaded grid. Traced to `shared/shapes.js` `resolveGridCells`'s `'loom'` branch: it centred every cell on `grid.width/2` / `grid.height/2`, assuming cell coordinates start at `(0,0)` — but `Organica.loadLoomGrid`'s `loomResolveCellRects` (`shared/core.js`) offsets every cell's `x`/`y` by `inner.x`/`inner.y` (the canvas margin), i.e. cell coordinates are **absolute canvas-space**, not grid-local. The mismatch shifted the whole grid down-and-right by exactly the margin, most visible on a grid's *shorter* axis (a small saved grid, e.g. Diego's 8-col/5-row "DIego Wave Grid" at 4% margin, showed a large top gap and a triangle row nearly touching the bottom edge, while the wider axis absorbed the same pixel shift almost invisibly). Same bug was live in FVS before the Trellis extraction — just harder to notice there since FVS's canvas isn't forced square. **Fix:** `resolveGridCells` now derives the centring origin from the cells' own bounding box (min/max of `x`/`x+width`/`y`/`y+height` for rect, of each polygon's own point extents) instead of the caller-supplied `grid.width`/`grid.height` — correct regardless of any margin/offset baked into the source coordinates. One shared function, so both FVS and Trellis are fixed at once. **Also added, per Diego's request** — a **"Show grid outline"** checkbox in Trellis's Grid panel section (mirrors FVS's own "Show loaded grid" toggle): strokes every cell's bounding box (from the same `centers` array content is placed against, in `--tool` colour) over the live preview, on demand, so a grid import's alignment can be checked directly instead of inferred from the painted shapes. **Verified**: reloaded the 40-cell "DIego Wave Grid" in Trellis — grid now centred symmetrically (top/bottom and left/right margins match); toggling "Show grid outline" draws 40 cell-boundary rectangles exactly flush with the triangle rows/columns; FVS regression — Bento (7 cells, rect) and Hexagonal (17 cells, polygon) built-in Loom grids both still show their own "Show loaded grid" overlay correctly aligned (no regression from the shared-code change); console clean throughout (env 404 only). **Not committed.**
  - **Same-session follow-up — defining default behaviours: "default le forme sono tutte delle stesse dimensioni."** Two parts. (1) The default Motion track (Scale pulse, added by the earlier "nulla si muove" fix) staggered by index at amount 0.6, which meant even the **rest frame** (t=0, before Play) already had differently-scaled shapes per cell — contradicting "same size by default." Fixed: the shared `buildTrackRow` template's default **Stagger amount → 0** (was 0.6), so a fresh Track 1 pulses every cell **in sync** (uniform at rest, uniform throughout — a synchronised breathing motion) rather than in a staggered wave; stagger amount is still a slider the user can raise. (2) Diego then flagged, after that fix, that shapes still varied cell-to-cell on the "DIego Wave Grid" — traced to the grid itself: it's a Sinusoidal Loom export with **non-uniform column widths** (113–138px, rows constant at 198.72), and Trellis's placement was hardcoded to `fitMode: 'contain'` (`shared/shapes.js` `resolveCellPlacement`), which scales each shape to its own cell — correct behaviour for a data-driven "fit the grid" look, but not what "same size by default" means when the grid's cells themselves aren't uniform. Diego's clarification ("a me sembrano tutte uguali le celle, mentre i triangoli dentro sono diversi" — the cells read as the same size to the eye, but the triangles inside don't) confirmed the fix needed at the **content** level, not the grid level. **Fix:** new **Fit** control in Trellis's Content section — `Uniform size` (new default) vs `Fit to cell` — a `seg-fit` toggle. Uniform size uses `resolveCellPlacement`'s existing `fitMode: 'fixed'` branch with `fixedSize` = `Organica.shapes.median` of every cell's own `min(cellW,cellH)` (so the constant size stays sensibly scaled to the grid as a whole, not an arbitrary fixed number) times `(1 − padding)` (so the Padding slider still does something in this mode, which `resolveCellPlacement`'s fixed branch otherwise ignores). Also refactored: the **Scale slider** (`baseScale`) used to multiply inside `resolveCellPlacement`'s `scale` param, which only the `'contain'` branch honours — moved to a single post-multiply on `scaleX`/`scaleY` in `buildDrawList` so it applies identically in both fit modes. **Verified**: `buildDrawList(0)` on the 40-cell Wave grid → all 40 `scaleX` values collapse to one distinct value (`1.0293`) in Uniform size, i.e. byte-identical across every cell, and screenshot shows all 40 triangles the same size; switching to `Fit to cell` restores the expected column-width variation (screenshot confirms visible per-column size difference matching the grid's own 113–138px track spread); console clean throughout.
  - **Same-session follow-up — a debug pass on the cell sizes themselves, then a new Loom feature.** Diego: "a me sembrano tutte delle stesse dimensioni" (re: the grid's own cells, not Trellis's shapes) — asked for a direct debug of every cell's width/height. Confirmed via `centers` (the resolved per-cell array): row heights genuinely identical everywhere (198.72px × 5 rows), column widths genuinely vary 113.52–137.89px (21.5% spread) following the grid's own sine modulation — real, not a bug, just gradual enough (a smooth ramp, not a hard step) to read as uniform without an outline overlay. Diego: "sviluppiamo in loom una funzione che distribuisce la larghezza e l'altezza di alcune grid in maniera omogenea, analizza dove è possibile e applica la feature." Explored first: only **3 of Loom's 15 generators** ever populate a `grid.tracks` array at all (`GENERATORS[type].cellShape === 'rect'` — Bento, Wave/Sinusoidal, Rectangular); the other 12 are polygon-cell generators with no shared column/row track to equalize, out of scope entirely. **Shipped — "Equalize columns" / "Equalize rows"**, two buttons in Loom's generic Grid section next to Randomize (`loom/index.html`, `#row-equalize`) → `equalizeTracks(axis)` (`loom/js/main.js`) — the exact same move as the existing WYSIWYG drag-to-resize handles (`onHandlePointerMove`): mutates `currentModel.grid.tracks[axis]` in place via `solveTracksKiwi` (reused verbatim — the same equal-by-default solve Bento's own default already relies on, no new "divide evenly" math written) and repaints, never calling `build()` so cell topology/spans/gap survive untouched — transient like a drag edit, a later parameter change discards it. Row visibility (`syncEqualizeRow()`, wired into `syncGeneratorRows()`) keys off the same `cellShape === 'rect'` flag `renderTrackHandles()` already uses. **Verified** on the no-store dev server: Wave/Sinusoidal at Amount 0.5/Frequency 2 → `grid.tracks.cols` `[117.2, 174.33, 91.77, 71.38, 163.02, 142.63, 60.07, 117.2]` → Equalize columns → all 8 flatten to `117.2` exactly (JSON view + screenshot both confirm); rows already uniform (axis was `cols`-only) → Equalize rows correctly no-ops; **Bento** (already equal by construction) → Equalize columns is a confirmed no-op (`239.4` × 4 before and after); **Rectangular** with its default typed ratio `"2,1,1"` → Equalize columns flattens `[484.8, 242.4, 242.4]` → `[323.2, 323.2, 323.2]` while the ratio text field itself stays `"2,1,1"` untouched, and a subsequent `build()` correctly reverts to the typed ratio (same documented behaviour as a drag edit); switching to **Hexagonal** hides the Equalize row (`display:none`), switching back to Bento shows it again (`flex`); saved an equalized Wave grid and re-imported it in **Trellis** — even in `Fit to cell` mode every cell now produces the identical `scaleX`, confirming the fix reaches downstream consumers with zero Trellis-side change needed. Console clean throughout (env 404 only). Docs: `docs/LOOM.md` (new paragraph in §7b, next to Randomize's own). **Not committed.**
  - **Same-session follow-up — "voglio che equalize sia una feature e options da scegliere però."** Diego: the one-click buttons should be a **standing option**, not a transient action — the original design meant a subsequent Amount/Frequency/ratio tweak silently threw the equalize away (same documented behaviour as a drag edit), which isn't what "an option to choose" implies. **Redesigned:** the two buttons became two checkboxes (`#ck-equalize-cols`/`#ck-equalize-rows`, same `#row-equalize` location/visibility gate) whose state is read and re-applied inside `build()` itself, right after `generator.generate()` returns — so the equalize now survives every future rebuild (slider tweak, Randomize, a saved-grid reload) for as long as it's checked, instead of needing a re-click each time. `equalizeTracks(axis)`/its `window` export were deleted; the same `solveTracksKiwi` call moved into `build()`'s own body, gated on `generator.cellShape === 'rect'` — **caught a real bug while wiring this in**: the generated `grid` object itself never carries a `cellShape` field for rect-track generators (only polygon generators set `cellShape: 'polygon'` on their own return value — confirmed by grep, `renderTrackHandles()`'s own header comment already flags this exact asymmetry), so the first version's `grid.cellShape === 'rect'` check was **always false** and silently did nothing; fixed by reading `generator.cellShape` (the static registry entry, `GENERATORS[type].cellShape`) instead, the same source `syncEqualizeRow()` already correctly used. The checkbox state also now bakes into the saved model as `grid.equalizeCols`/`grid.equalizeRows` and round-trips through `saveGridPreset`/`loadGridPreset`, restored explicitly (`loadGridPreset` sets both checkboxes from the saved model right before its own `build()` call), the same pattern `rg-padding` already follows. **Verified** on the no-store dev server: checking Equalize columns on Wave (Amount 0.5) flattens `[117.2, 174.33, 91.77, 71.38, 163.02, 142.63, 60.07, 117.2]` → all `117.2`; cranking Amount to 0.9 **afterward** — tracks stay perfectly equal (the actual fix — this would have reverted under the old button); `randomizeParams()` (scoped to `#block-sinusoidal`, outside `#row-equalize`) leaves the checkbox checked and the freshly-randomized column count still comes out equalized; unchecking correctly restores the real sine-modulated variance (`[264.28, 234.94, 198.35, 239.03]` at Amount 0.9/Axis cols); Bento stays a confirmed no-op; Rectangular's typed ratio (`"2,1,1"`) still resolves normally when unchecked (`[484.8, 242.4, 242.4]`) and flattens when checked (`[323.2, 323.2, 323.2]`), ratio field itself untouched either way; save → uncheck → load the same preset → checkbox correctly returns to checked and tracks re-equalize. Console clean (env 404 only). Docs: `docs/LOOM.md` (rewrote the Equalize paragraph to describe the checkbox/standing-option design + the `generator.cellShape` bug). **Not committed.**

- **September 4, 2026 (later) — Loom: gated the "Seed" row so it isn't a dead control, across nine generators.** Diego: "a cosa serve il parametro Seed visto che spesso non funziona." It's the `mulberry32` reproducibility knob (same seed + params → byte-identical grid) — but for most generators its randomness is gated behind another parameter (Bento `Variety`, Hex/Tri/Diamond/Circular/Linear/Diagonal `Jitter` or Spin mode Random/Noise, Fractal `Split variance`, Radial/Angular Distortion), and every built-in preset ships those at 0/Off, so the row was shown unconditionally and dragging it was a visible no-op (verified: bento module call, `variety:0` → seeds 7/42/99 produce the identical grid). Sinusoidal's `row-sin-seed` already got the conditional-row treatment; the other nine hadn't. Fixed the same way (`docs/LOOM.md`'s own "Komorebi control audit" rule): new `seedMatters(type)` + `syncSeedRow(type)` (`loom/js/main.js`) toggle each gated row's `display`, wired into `syncGeneratorRows`, `syncDistortRow` (+ a `DISTORT_PREFIX_TYPE` map — its prefixes `lin/diag/ang/mas` don't match their type names), the three Spin-mode sync fns, `randomizeParams`, and a generic panel-wide range-`input` listener so a live Jitter/Variety/Variance drag re-checks it. Masonry/Organic Seed rows stay unconditional (always-random). Every Seed label (`loom/index.html`) gained a `title` explaining what it does and its gate. **Verified**: Bento Variety 0→hidden, 0.4→shown, back to 0→hidden; Radial hidden at Distortion Off, shown Sine/Noise; Hexagonal hidden at Jitter 0/Spin Off, shown at Jitter>0 or Spin Random/Noise, stays hidden at Spin Checkerboard; Fractal hidden at Variance 0; Masonry/Organic always shown; every built-in saved-grid preset (Swiss/Modular Grid/Rule of Thirds/Isometric/Timeline) loads with the row correctly hidden; `Randomize` stays consistent across repeated rolls; console clean. Docs: `docs/LOOM.md` (new dated paragraph in the Hexagonal section, next to the earlier Spin/Noise Seed-bug note). **Not committed.**

- **September 3, 2026 — Loom / Linear: Sine·Noise distortion refinement + a 3-way `Crop` toggle.** Diego iterated ~10 rounds on `loom/js/generators/linear.js` (Distortion bending the division lines). Fixes, in order: (1) `linear/index.html` `9ebe12d` — a `gap:0` boundary strip landed exactly on the inner rect and `clipToRect` returned a degenerate ≥3-point ~0-area polygon that `pushPoly` counted + numbered (cols=2 → "4 cells"); added `polygonArea` + a `minArea` reject. (2) `stripSubdiv`/`cellSubdiv` scale with the wave's cycle count not a flat 32 — a low-Frequency sine was undersampled into a chord "fin"; **and (later) the per-cycle count rises with amplitude** — at high `Amount` the steep zero-crossing was still undersampled into a near-flat chord that read as a *step* at the frame at Frequency 2/3 (`segPerCycle = clamp(amp·2π/6, 24, 400)`, subdiv cap → 2000; `mergeEps` also capped at 1px so `dedupeClose` can't pull a lobe seam off the main edge). (3) **distorted cells render as real corner-aware curves** — `smooth: 'sharp'`, and `catmullRomPathD` (json-model.js) grew a `keepCorners` 3rd arg that clamps the spline tangents at any vertex turning > ~40°: wavy runs become true Béziers at any zoom, the ~90° wave↔frame joins stay sharp (no overshoot past the canvas). `'sharp'` not `true`, so Diagonal/Angular/Masonry/Radial (no 3rd arg) keep the byte-identical closed-loop spline. An earlier take here — `smooth:false` + brute density (`segPerCycle` up to 400, ~17k pts/grid) — faceted visibly; curves brought it back to ~1–5k pts. `dedupeClose` drops `clipToRect` sub-pixel stubs. (3b) **the SVG export no longer double-strokes each division line** — `dedupeClose` was keep-first (order-dependent), so a shared `gap:0` edge deduped to different vertices from each of its two cells → doubled line in a zoomable vector; it now replaces a close pair with its **midpoint** (order-independent) → both cells produce identical points → reversal-symmetric corner-aware Catmull-Rom → the two curves coincide (≤ 0.06 px). `gap > 0` still = two distinct channel lines. (4) `polygonCentroid` → **area-weighted** (bbox fallback for a self-intersecting heavy-`both` cell) so the cell number sits at the visual centre. (5) `edgeTaper` (keep the frame straight) was tried, then **reverted** — Diego wanted the two outer lines to wave too. (6) then the outer lines' crescents were "missing", so an `OUT` push flattens the outer strip edges onto the frame → edge cells fill solid. (7) Diego: keep that as one option, add two more → **`Crop` control (None / Low / Full)**, Sine/Noise only, **default Full**. *None* = the `OUT` push (edge cells solid, rect frame). *Low* = outer lines wave + clip, open crescents. *Full* = *Low* + each clipped crescent **lobe becomes its own cell** (`splitLobes()` walks the boundary polyline, one closed cell per inside-run, appended after the main strips → trailing numbers). Cols/Rows only; `both`+`full` == `low`; ignored when rotated or Distortion Off. UI: `#seg-lin-crop` in `#block-linear`, shown by `syncDistortRow` when distortion active; `distortCrop` threads through `buildGridParams` + `applyGridParamsToUI` (Save/Load round-trips). Verified: module battery (96 Sine/Noise combos × 3 modes — count/coverage/in-rect/finite-centroid/`smooth==='sharp'`/bounded ~5k pts; `full` main cells byte-match `low`; `off`/`rotated` crop-invariant; `catmullRomPathD` with no 3rd arg byte-identical to before; `both` fallback; `gap:0` guard; determinism; every cell → a valid cubic path) + live UI (toggle hidden for Off, default Full, Cols 5 + Sine → 9 shapes = 5 strips + 4 frame-pinned lobe cells, numbered 1–9; SVG export inspected at 14× — shared division line is a single stroke, no facets, wave↔frame joins sharp; console clean). Docs: `docs/LOOM.md` Linear section. **Uncommitted:** `loom/js/generators/linear.js` + `loom/index.html` + `loom/js/main.js` + `loom/js/json-model.js` (`catmullRomPathD` `keepCorners`) + the 3 renderers (pass `cell.smooth === 'sharp'`) — only `9ebe12d` from step 1 shipped.

- **September 3, 2026 — `forms.js` deep restructure, pass 2: per-form metadata block + `class="aNN"` hooks removed.** Diego: "procedi con questo forms.js — seconda metà del restructure ROADMAP", and after mapping the blast radius (only real consumer of the animation hooks = the **hub bento**), "si anche quella della home hub non ci servono quindi possiamo pulire tutto". **`genesis/forms.js`** rewritten: `window.ORGANIC_SEEDS` = one entry per seed keyed by `seed-<slug>` — `{ label, type, tags:[…], bbox:[x,y,w,h], svg }`. Every `svg` is now **class-free** and paints `fill="var(--ink)"` / `stroke="var(--ink)"` inline (previously the fill came from `animations.css` `.aNN .child` rules; `seed-sun`'s rays + `seed-line` needed explicit `stroke` added — they'd have been invisible without the CSS). `window.ORGANIC_FORMS` (id→svg) + `window.ORGANIC_LABELS` (id→label) are **derived** from `ORGANIC_SEEDS` at the bottom of the file (insertion order preserved) — so **no consumer needed a code change** (`genesis/index.html` `organicForm`/`ORGANIC_KEEP`, `shared/seeds-panel.js` `PRIMORDIAL`, pollen/spore/livingpath, hub `BENTO_FORMS` all read the derived maps). **`index.html` (hub)** — dropped the `<link …/genesis/animations.css>`, deleted the now-dead `<svg class="defs">` block (goo filters + `#cA…#cG` terrazzo chips — grep confirmed 0 `url(#` / `href="#c` references once the seed classes were gone), updated the bento comment. The bento's Genesis cells now render **static** (the `.tp-*` tool-preview cells keep their local keyframe motion); `--ink` is still varied per cell and the seeds' own `fill="var(--ink)"` picks it up. **DB** — the 13 organic `base_seeds` rows updated via MCP `execute_sql`: `svg` column + `data->>'svg'` set to the new class-free markup, `data` gained `tags` + `bbox`, dropped the stale `num` key. `genesis/animations.css` + `genesis/page.css` untouched — the only live consumer left is `genesis/archive/indicators-55.html` (self-contained, its own inline `class="aNN"` markup). `seeds-panel.js` `bakeGeometry`/`ensureAnimationsCss` (camo-turing) left as-is — now a redundant-but-harmless safety pass (the markup already carries literal geometry + fill; `animations.css` still resolves, no 404). Docs: `docs/ROADMAP.md` (item checked, both passes), `docs/SHARED-LIBRARY.md`, `docs/ANIMATION-SYSTEM.md`, CLAUDE.md (Repo tree + Critical Rules + this note). **Not committed** — pending verification + Diego's "porta in prod".

- **September 1, 2026 — Mote promoted from `scratchpad/mote.html` to `/mote/`, under the "Motion & growth" hub group.** Diego: "analizza e pianifica come importare il test mote nel tool sotto la sezione Motion". Three decisions (AskUserQuestion): **extend `shared/recorder.js`** (not keep a local recorder), a **full interaction redesign** (not just shell-conformance), and — for the stipple core — an analysis; the recommendation, taken, was **extract `shared/pollen-engine.js` now** (Pollen + Mote as the two consumers, the `radial.js` "extract at the second consumer" precedent). **Phase 0 — `shared/pollen-engine.js`:** the tone pipeline (`processLuma`/`buildField`), the pure dart-thrower `computePoints(o)` (injectable `rng` / `field` / `isCancelled` / `onProgress` / `hideBand` / `maxPoints` — the shape Mote's prototype had already proven), `spacingAt`/`gradAngle`, `ranged`/`dropoutSkip`/`pointAngle` (radians) / `pointRGBA` (with Mote's `video` branch), and the curved-line + field-streamline geometry (`strokeCenterline`/`smoothOpenPath`[`D`]/`makeFieldAngle`/`fieldStrokePts`). Scoped to the **intersection** — each tool keeps its own `paintPoints` (Pollen dispatches SVG-form marks, Mote draws literal dots) and Pollen keeps its SVG serializer. **`pollen/index.html` refactored** to consume it: the dart-core in `computePoints` (spatial grid + closures + phase loop + Sobel map, lines 1020–1079) replaced by one `PE.computePoints({...})` call with the DOM plumbing (progress bar, `#vl-total`, `computeToken` cancel) threaded back through `onProgress`/`isCancelled`; `processLuma`/`brightnessMap` inner loop → `PE.*`; `pointRGBA` → `PE.pointRGBA`; local `ranged`/`dropoutSkip`/`strokeCenterline`/`smoothOpenPath`[`D`]/`makeFieldAngle`/`fieldStrokePts` deleted, destructured from `PE` at the top; `pointAngle` (returns **degrees**, callers convert) + `sampleWorkB` (reads Pollen's `fieldB` globals) kept local. **Gate (Pollen's dart-thrower has no seed, so end-to-end byte-identical is impossible):** a **seeded-RNG point-array diff** — `PE.computePoints` vs a verbatim copy of the old inline algorithm, same `mulberry32(12345)` + same field → **438 = 438 points, every `{x,y,b,rnd,ga}` field identical**. Plus functional: all 6 Pollen built-in presets regenerate cleanly (9,454 / 2,744 / 5,679 / 8,170 / 8,197 / 4,639 pts), `buildSVG` → valid 761 KB / 9,166-mark SVG, `render()` repaints, `stopCompute()` works, console clean. **Phase 1 — `mote/index.html`** built from `shared/_template.html` (single file, ~700 lines, matches Pulsar). Shell: 5 shared sheets + `palette.css`, real `.org-header--tool`, `class="org-stage"`, `#panel` 248px, `Organica.autoLabelPanel` (0 unlabeled controls). **Panel redesign:** new **Source** section (File / Webcam / Demo seg + a `enumerateDevices` camera `<select>` shown when >1, Mirror checkbox, Capture-seconds slider); **Preset** on `Organica.presetStore('mote')` + 9 curated human-named built-ins + Save/Delete/name-row; **Coherence** seg relabelled **Fixed grid / Flowing / Re-stipple** (A/B/C) with plain hints, "Re-place points" button (was "Relattice"), Re-stipple carries an offline-preview `.panel-note`; **Flow tuning** (B only) = Gentle/Medium/Turbulent seg over the 5 raw params behind a `<details>`; Stipple / Marks / Colour (`palette.swatch` attach for `ink`/`paper` at `cp-`/`hex-`/`sw-` ids + `palette.swatch($('rmx-palette'),{min:2,max:8})` for the RMX strip) / Tone kept. **Floatbar** Pulsar-shaped: Play/Pause · Stop · **Export** popover (PNG frame · **Record** = wall-clock via `Organica.recorder` · **Render** = the offline `MediaStreamTrackGenerator` + timestamped-`VideoFrame` encoder, kept Mote-local — the only non-realtime encoder in the repo). The prototype's `?stream` synthetic path **dropped** (dev-only, never booted headless); `?demo` / `?cam` boot shortcuts kept. `renderMode`/`sec-b`/`seg-render` and the codec-internals hint copy gone. **Phase 2 — `shared/recorder.js`** gained `bitrate` (`number | () => number` → `videoBitsPerSecond`) and `mimeCandidates` (`string[]`, overrides the built-in list) — fully back-compatible (Pulsar/Camo/Vortex/Membrane untouched when absent);   - **Sep 1 (still later) — WYSIWYG fixes on the batch export** (Diego: "l'export non è WYSIWYG"). A `mote-master-*.mp4` from an 800²-ish source rendered a **denser, finer stipple than the preview**. Two causes: (1) **`applyPreset` never re-latticed** — it wrote `sl-sp-*`/`sl-phases`/`sl-detail` but only called `reinit()` when *detail* changed, so a preset that keeps the default Detail but tightens spacing left `basePoints` at the *previous* lattice (e.g. 2,740 pts on screen while a fresh `computePoints` with the new sliders gives ~7–15k). Fixed: `applyPreset` now `reinit()`s on any source (`workW` truthy) — a preset shift always rebuilds the lattice. (2) **`renderJobs` derived the job working res as `min(outW, detail)`** — which *upscales* past a small source's pixels, unlike `onReady()`'s `workDims(srcW, srcH, detail)` (`Math.min(1, …)`, never upscales). Fixed: jobs now use `workDims(crop.sw, crop.sh, detail)`, matching the live pipeline exactly. Plus: for a **Fixed-grid (A) master** whose framing == the live preview (Crop, dims equal), `renderJobs` now **reuses the live `basePoints` verbatim** instead of re-seeding from frame 0 → the export is byte-identical to the on-screen stipple (modes B/C, Fit, and social crops still re-lattice their own framing — B/C mutate `points` in place so a shared ref would corrupt the snapshot). **Verified:** after `applyPreset('Fine dots')` the preview `pts-hint` jumps 2,740 → 7,190 and the master export reuses that same 7,190-point lattice (0 `computePoints` calls in the job); a same-frame, same-resolution pixel diff of the export's frame 0 vs a reference `paintPoints` render = **0.000%**. Social 9:16 still re-latices (`computePoints` called). Console clean.

  - **September 4, 2026 (later same day) — Adapt colour: "Min ink" floor so bright-field marks don't vanish.** Diego drew over the pale beach areas of an **Etching lines** frame: "perché queste aree sono molto chiare". Trace: `paintPoints` fills the bg with Paper, then each mark's colour = `E.pointRGBA` → for Adapt = `Organica.palette.colorAt(b, {mode:'adaptive', ink, paper})` = `mixHex(ink, paper, b)` — so a point at `b → 1` is painted **the Paper colour** and disappears. `Invert` + `Contrast 26` make the tone field near-bimodal (live histogram ~40k px at `b≈1`, ~15k at `b≈0`, almost nothing between), so whole regions saturate to `b=1` and go blank; spacing (`3+2.5·b`) + `dropoutSkip` (`<0.4·b^1.5`) pile onto the same end. **Fix — `shared/pollen-engine.js` `pointRGBA`:** `let score = b; if (P.colorMode === 'adaptive' && P.minInk) score = b*(1-P.minInk);` then pass `score` to `colorAt` — guarded, so Solid/RMX/Video and every Pollen call (no `P.minInk`) are byte-identical. **`mote/index.html`:** new `#sl-minink` slider (0–80 %, `#row-minink`, shown only when `colorMode === 'adaptive'` via `setColor`), `pointParams().minInk` (feeds preview + PNG + `buildMoteSVG` from one object), added to the `repaintStill` id list + `commitPreset`/`applyPreset` (`P.minInk || 0`) + `presetToParams` thumbnails; the 3 `cm:'adaptive'` built-ins (`Etching lines`/`Duotone`/`Tonal drift`) get `minInk: 12`. **Verified** on the no-store server: `E.pointRGBA` adaptive `b=1` → `minInk 0` = Paper `[239,233,220]`, `0.12` = `[212,207,195]`, `0.4` = `[149,145,137]`, `0.8` = `[58,57,54]` — all exactly `round(ink + (paper−ink)·b·(1−minInk))`; Solid/RMX untouched; SVG export of an Etching-lines frame — **3,261 strokes, 1,939 = exact Paper `rgb(239,233,220)` at minInk 0 → 0 at minInk 60**, geometry identical (WYSIWYG via the shared `pointRGBA`); `#row-minink` visible only for Adapt; preset save/load round-trips `minInk 40`; `Fine dots` (no key) → slider 0; Pollen `/pollen/` loads clean, `pointRGBA` adaptive with no `minInk` byte-identical. Console clean (env 404 only). **Not committed.**
  - **September 4, 2026 (later same day) — Flowing (mode B): fixed the excessive per-frame scatter.** Diego: "il Flow tuning — alcune modalità fanno uno scattering eccessivo … verifica come Medium e Turbulent davvero possono essere utili o fixati." Analysis of `stepB` (`mote/index.html`): the presets conflated **two independent things** — *tracking responsiveness* (how fast the cloud re-tracks the moving image: `b-step`/`b-damp`/`b-nbr`, the relaxation stage) and *scatter* (the cull+spawn stage: `budget = round(targetCount·b-rate)` points killed and **respawned at `Math.random()` positions across the whole frame**, `rnd: Math.random()`, no fade, per frame). `FLOW_PRESETS` cranked both together — turbulent `b-rate 0.030` = ~2× gentle's teleport count (~860/frame @ 28k pts) on top of bigger steps; even gentle recycled ~430/frame globally. **Verdict:** Medium/Turbulent are worth keeping but mis-designed. **Fixes, all `mote/index.html`:** (1) re-tuned `FLOW_PRESETS` so Gentle→Turbulent = *tracking speed* — widened `b-step`/`b-damp`/`b-nbr` (0.16/0.30/0.50 · 0.78/0.62/0.48 · 1.30/1.50/1.75), pulled `b-rate` down + nearly flat **0.005 / 0.010 / 0.015** (was 0.015/0.020/0.030), raised `b-cull` for the livelier presets (0.80/0.74/0.70). (2) **Spawn locality** — a recycled point is now jittered ±2.5·ds around a random *live* point (tone-appropriate spot, no cross-frame teleport), global-random kept only as the empty-cloud fallback. (3) **Seeded recycle RNG** — module `flowRng = Organica.mulberry32(SEED ^ 0x9e37)`, reset in `recomputeStipple()`/`onReady()`, drives the stage-4 pick + jitter + new `rnd` → temporally coherent + reproducible (was bare `Math.random()`). (4) **Relax deadband** — a point with `|v| < 0.02·ds` skips its move → no micro-"boil" on near-static footage. **Verified** on the no-store server (`scratchpad/testclip.mp4`, mode B, static mid-clip frame, direct `window.stepB` iteration): Turbulent 1,506 pts / budget 23-recycled-per-frame → **0 points land >50 px from the prior cloud over 6 frames** (old global spawn would scatter ~23/frame anywhere); two full `recomputeStipple` + 16×`stepB` runs are **byte-identical** (seeded `flowRng`); population spread 1.8 % (within the `targetCount·1.05` band); mode-B playback renders an even dot field, no clumps/voids; new preset values load via `setFlow`; console clean. **Note (pre-existing, out of scope):** `commitPreset`/`applyPreset` never persisted the five `b-*` values — the mode-B built-ins use whatever the Flow chip was last set to; not changed here. **Not committed.**
  - **September 4, 2026 (later same day) — Fixed the preset-switch wedge (base layer freezes, Play inert).** Diego: "quando cambio tra un preset e l'altro delle volte si freeza il layer di partenza." Repro: playing → drag a Stipple slider (freezes the frame) → switch preset → **wedged** (`running` false, resume hint stuck, Play does nothing; only Stop recovers). Cause (`mote/index.html`): `recomputeStipple`/`onReady` were **not serialized** — `applyPreset` fired 3–4 concurrent copies (`setColor` + `setCoherence` each ran `recomputeStipple` from the Sep-4 freeze-preview edits, + `reinit`→`onReady`, + the pending `onStippleEdit` debounce's `flushStippleEdit`), all awaiting the multi-phase dart-thrower on shared module state → event-loop saturation; and `startRealtime`'s `if (stipplePending) flushStippleEdit().then(startRealtime)` **self-looped** while `stipplePending` couldn't clear (its flush stuck behind the pile), so every Play press added another stuck rebuild. **Fix:** (1) new **`rebuildStipple(full)`** — the single gate: at most one rebuild in flight, any callers during it collapse to **one** trailing rerun (full if any asked); `recomputeStipple`/`onReady` bodies unchanged, just gated. Every caller routes through it (`flushStippleEdit`, `setCoherence`, `markReady`, webcam, `applyPreset`). (2) **`reinit` deleted** (its only real caller was `applyPreset`; it also captured `was = running` at *timer-fire* time → wrong on rapid switches). (3) `applyPreset` now sets `applyingPreset` (so nested `setColor`/`setCoherence` don't self-trigger a rebuild), clears `stippleT`/`stipplePending`/`stippleEditId`/`stippleFrozen` at the top, captures `resume = running || playBeforeFreeze` once, and ends with `rebuildStipple(true).then(() => { if (resume) startRealtime(); })`. (4) new `playBeforeFreeze` flag (set in `onStippleEdit` only when actually `running`, cleared in `startRealtime`/`stopAll`) so a preset switch from a frozen-mid-play state resumes. (5) `startRealtime` is now `async` with a `startingUp` re-entrancy guard and a single `await flushStippleEdit()` instead of the `.then(startRealtime)` self-loop. **Verified** on the no-store server (`scratchpad/testclip.mp4`): the exact repro → `running` back to true, hint hidden, `pts-hint` reflects the new preset; rapid double `applyPreset` while playing → ends playing; genuinely-paused → switch preset → stays paused, no stuck hint; **max concurrency of `recomputeStipple`/`onReady` = 1** through any sequence; `sl-detail` freeze→Play still works (canvas 950→1200, resumes); `sl-size` live-updates while playing; Stop→Play works; Play/Pause/Play toggles; SVG export (5.3 MB) valid; console clean (env 404 only). **Not committed.**

user rows get `.sm-name` (click → transient `<input class="set-name-input">`, same commit/Esc/blur as `showNewSetInput` → `renameSet`), `↑`/`↓` (`reorderSet`, `disabled` at first/last user row), `×` (`armDeleteSet` — 2-click armed, `.is-armed`, 3s disarm → `deleteSet`). `renameSet` rejects empty + case-insensitive dup names; `reorderSet` array-swaps in `state.library.sets` clamped to index ≥ 1; `deleteSet` removes the set, then `state.library.forms = filter(f => !(member && in no surviving set))` (orphan-delete), falls back to `BUILT_IN_SET_ID` if the active set went. `renderSetsList()` calls `renderSetsManager()` at its tail so the chip bar + panel never drift. `onSetsManagerRowClick` is a `#sets-manage-list` delegate for `↑↓×`; rename is the inline-input path. `migrateLibrary` gains one line — pin the built-in to `sets[0]` for older blobs (`bi > 0` guard = idempotent, no new set field). **B — seed↔set membership:** new `#edit-sets` div in the Edit header (the old read-only "In sets: N" `<dl>` row dropped from `syncEditPanel`). `renderEditSets()` — for a `user-*` seed, a checkbox per non-built-in set (checked = member); `toggleMembership(setId, formId, on)` `push`/`splice`s `set.forms` directly (no mint), guarded so unchecking a seed's **last** membership is blocked with a status pointing at Delete. Built-in seed → count + "Save a copy" hint, no checkboxes. Wired via an `#edit-sets` `change` delegate in `bindCreatorControls`. **C — Source removed:** the whole Source `.lib-filters__group` deleted; `state.filter` → `{ type }`; `filteredForms()` collapses to the Type predicate. `.lib-filters` now holds only the Type seg — `syncFilterUI` / the seg click delegate are already generic and unchanged (the `key === 'set'` guards go dead-but-harmless). **D — Type pills + view persistence:** `syncTypePills()` writes `#type-pills` `.active` from `S.type` (fixes the latent stale-pill bug), called from `resetCreate` + `loadSeedIntoEdit`. New key `organica.library.view` (`{activeSetId, filter}`) via `saveView()` / `loadView()` (value-whitelisted on read via `TYPE_VALUES`, separate from `STORAGE_KEY` so a corrupt view blob can't hurt library data); `loadView()` added to `init()` right after `loadLibrary()`; `activateSet` **stops wiping `state.filter`** and calls `saveView()`; the filter delegate calls `saveView()`. **Verified** on the no-store dev server + `node -c` clean: `⚙` expands the panel (Base Seeds first, controls-less; Diego Seeds `↑` disabled, My Seeds `↓` disabled); `renameSet` ok / dup-rejected / empty-rejected; `reorderSet` moves the chip **and** `#ctrl-set` option order, built-in stays first; `deleteSet` keeps a seed still in another set, orphan-deletes one that isn't, deleting the active set → Base Seeds; `migrateLibrary` idempotent + `sets[0].id === 'organic-forms'`; membership checkboxes add/remove without minting (chip count tracks), last-membership uncheck blocked, built-in shows only the hint; Source seg gone (`.lib-filters .seg` count 1); type pill syncs on Edit-load + Create-reset; reload restores `{activeSetId:'set_my-seeds', filter:{type:'asset'}}`, corrupt view blob doesn't throw, set switch no longer resets the Type filter; `saveForm` + `duplicateFromEdit` regression-clean. All test residue reverted — Diego's real library (Base / Diego Seeds→`user-…383032` / My Seeds→`user-…358118`, original order) restored, `organica.library.view` cleared. CSS: `.lib-sets` + `.lib-sets__bar` + `.seg--sets` re-scoped (was `.lib-filters`-scoped), `#sets-manage-list` / `.sm-row*`, `#edit-sets` / `.es-row` — tokens only. Docs: `docs/UI-SHELL.md` §Genesis, this note + Tools row. **Not committed.**

- **August 31, 2026 (later same day) — Genesis: mode nav split between the Library page and the floatbar.** Diego: Library/Create as buttons **above the filter bar** in the Library page; Create/Edit navigation **in the floatbar**; Edit gets no standalone tab (you still enter Edit by clicking a seed). - **August 28, 2026 (still later same day) — Radial: two presets that were namesakes only, fixed against the live originals; +1 bonus preset.** Diego asked to re-verify `chaos_circles` and `line_based_circles` against Book of Shapes. Both were **name-only** — the preset rendered a different image. `line_based_circles` (real: ~8 concentric circles each *deconstructed* into short disconnected **tangent** dashes, plotter-art look) was shipping as connected jittered 40-gon `<polygon>` rings. `chaos_circles` (real: ~83 large, near-concentric noise-perturbed circle *outlines* piled into a tangle, own noise phase each) was shipping as a jittered lattice of small `<circle>` marks. Two fixes, one pass: **(1)** added a **Line direction: radial | tangent** option to Marks mode (one `+π/2` on the segment angle, in `markSVG` + `drawSceneCanvas`) — a grid of tangent line-marks *is* line-based circles; verified tangent measures 90° to the radius, radial 0°. **(2)** new **`loops` render mode ("Chaos circles")** — draws `Circle count` closed loops, each a 72-pt circle of `Circle radius`, edge wobbled by `Organica.noise.simplex2` with a per-loop phase + random rotation + slight radius spread + gentle centre drift from a dedicated `mulberry32` stream (`seed * 40503`) so they don't nest; params Circle count / Circle radius / Noise. First tuning (`radius 0.5`) read as a cramped wool-ball donut — Diego flagged it; bumped to `radius 0.85 / noise 0.16` for the reference's big-overlapping-circles-with-clean-centre look. Both presets rewritten to the new modes. Diego also liked the *old* Line-Based Circles look, so it's kept as a **"Jittered Rings"** preset (17 total). Group-C audit result: Spiral Dot Field + Halftone Sphere were already faithful; these two were not. Verified: all 17 presets render + WYSIWYG true, loop seed reproducible, 0 unlabeled controls, zero console errors on a fresh tab. `radial/index.html` only. Shipped to production.

- **August 28, 2026 (still later same day) — FVS Symbols: a generative per-cell rule layer, from `bookofshapes.com/patterns/concentric_arc_truchet_3` ("Arc Truchet Butterfly").** Diego: that tool (a Cols×Rows grid, one arc motif per cell, per-cell rotation 0°/90° from a `col + rowShift·row` oscillator) is basically FVS's Symbols model — "in teoria abbiamo tutto" — asked to evaluate integrating it into Symbol definition and to make Symbol authoring "molto più veloce e generativa", so a Loom grid becomes a new editable FVS instance while Components stay fixed. Two Explore agents mapped it: FVS Symbols already imports Loom grids and has full per-cell editing, but authoring is manual-cell-by-cell or a 50/50 random `Generate` — **no rule engine**. Components' own `FAMILIES` rules are all 2×2-only (hand-derived 4-cell literals), not reusable for N×M. **Loom already publishes every saved grid to `localStorage['organica.loom.presets']`** as a `buildModel()` object → directly `Organica.loadLoomGrid`-able; FVS just never read it. Decisions (Diego, via AskUserQuestion): full rule set; support any Loom grid (rect via native `col/row`, regular polygon via centroid binning) + a direct saved-grid picker; rule apply is lock-aware **and** has a hard "Reset & apply to all". **Out of scope, stated**: the concentric-arc "butterfly" Element itself — that's `2 × Arc Count` stacked semicircle paths per cell; FVS renders one path / one fill per cell and reuses one Element per Component, so it needs a new multi-ring `wedgePathD` geometry + cell shape-stacking. The rule layer works with whatever Element/Component is already the cell content. **Shipped in `fvs/index.html`**: `#sel-symbol-fill` gains a **Rule** mode with `#symbol-rule-block`; **`SYMBOL_RULES`** registry (`oscillator`/`checkerboard`/`rows`/`columns`/`radial`/`wave`/`random`), each `{read(), fn(ctx,p,rng) → {rotation?,flipH?,flipV?,scale?}}` — N×M-general, per-cell; **`cellColRow(grid)`** — the genuinely new helper: rect Loom cells read `col/row` from the JSON + `grid.params.cols/rows`; polygon cells bin `resolveGridCells` centres into sorted y→row / x→col bands; always also fills `cx,cy,nx,ny,angle` so Radial/Wave/Random work on any grid; **`applySymbolRule({resetAll})`** mirrors `generateSymbolCells()` — rebuilds only each unlocked cell's transforms from the rule, never touches `source`/content/`fitMode`/anchor/padding; **Vary** checkboxes (Rotation/Flip/Scale) gate which axes the rule writes; a per-cell **`locked`** flag (checkbox in the Cell-properties panel) makes `Apply rule` skip a hand-edited cell, `Reset & apply to all` ignores it; **direct Loom-grid picker** `#sel-symbol-loom-preset` reads `Organica.presetStore('loom')` (repopulated on tab switch) + the two built-ins, so a grid designed in `/loom/` is one click, no JSON upload; **"Apply to all cells"** toggle in the Choose-content overlay + a `Set content for all cells…` button in the rule block → `contentTarget()` routes tile-picks to every cell instead of the selection, so one Component tiles the whole grid then the rule varies it (~4 clicks to a full generative Symbol); the rule state (`RULE_CONTROL_IDS`) is saved into `buildSymbolLibraryEntry().rule` and restored by `applyRuleState()` in `applySymbolLibraryEntryToUI` — additive, old entries without `rule` still load (cells are also stored resolved). **Verified live** (2×rAF awaits): Bento grid → Rule → Oscillator → rotations `[0,0,90,0,0,0,90]` from real `col/row`, Row shift live-changes the flow; Arc content for all + oscillator renders the flowing quarter-circle Truchet composition (screenshot); Hexagonal (17 polygon cells) → centroid binning gives cols:3/rows:5, oscillator produces only `{0,90}`; every rule fills with the right distinct-rotation count; Vary Rotation-only leaves flip/scale byte-identical, enabling them changes them; a saved Loom grid appears in the picker and loads as a new instance; lock survives a rule re-apply, "Reset & apply to all" overrides it; save→mutate-UI→`applySymbolLibraryEntryToUI` restores rule+fill+params+cells; **WYSIWYG** — `buildSymbolSVG()` and the rendered DOM have identical element counts (svg/metadata/defs/clipPath×12/rect×25/g×36/path×12) and byte-identical first-path `d` (the `!==` on raw strings is browser `innerHTML` re-serialisation, same artifact as Radial); Components tier + Symbols Generate/Manual modes regression-clean; 0 unlabeled controls; zero console errors on a genuinely fresh tab. `fvs/index.html` only. Shipped to production.

- **August 28, 2026 (still later same day) — FVS: the "Arc truchet" Element, the piece flagged out of scope in the rule-layer pass, built.** Diego: "let's work on the Out of scope — the concentric-arc butterfly Element itself." The earlier note said it needed "cell shape-stacking" (one FVS cell holds one path) — that turned out to be avoidable: `arcTruchetPathD(count, ratio)` returns **one compound `d`** (2 fans × `count` closed annular half-rings from the top & bottom edge midpoints, non-overlapping so no `fill-rule` needed), which drops into FVS's existing one-`<path>`-per-cell model unchanged. Inscribed exactly in the 0–100 box (max radius 50, the fans meet at the centre — `getBBox` → `{0,0,100,100}`), so **no per-cell clip is needed** anywhere (Element preview strip, Components gallery, Symbols). **One real bug, caught by `getBBox` not by eye**: the first sweep-flag choice put both fans bulging *outward* (bbox `y:-50 h:200`, overflowing the box by 50 each side) — SVG's `sweep=1` on a left→right arc bulges UP in y-down space, and the inner arc (right→left) takes the opposite flag; fixed and re-verified bbox is exactly the unit box. Wired through: `SEED_TYPES.arctruchet`, `#sel-seed-type`, a `#seed-arctruchet-block` (Arc count 3–12 / Arc ratio 10–95%), `getSeed()` (ratio stored 0–1), `syncSeedUI()`, the two sliders' listeners, `buildLibraryEntry`/`applyLibraryEntryToUI` round-trip, and the Symbols tier — `buildSymbolItems` + the Choose-content overlay + `generateSymbolCells` all get a fixed `SYMBOL_ARC_TRUCHET = {arcCount:5, arcRatio:0.7}` (Symbols cells carry no per-seed params, same as they already ignore Base/Height/Thickness). Verified: preview strip shows the butterfly at 0/90/180/270° + flips; Components + Radial rule → concentric-arc mandalas; **Symbols + built-in Bento + Oscillator rule → a faithful `concentric_arc_truchet_3` reproduction** (screenshot); 7 `<path>` for 7 cells, WYSIWYG element counts match; arc-param save/reload round-trips (0–1 internal form); Triangle/Arc seeds unaffected; 0 unlabeled controls; zero console errors on a fresh tab. `fvs/index.html` only. **Not committed — waiting for an explicit "porta in prod".**

- **August 29, 2026 — Shipped Pulsar (`/pulsar/`), the generative Motion tool over Radial, plus `shared/radial.js`.** Diego asked for a Motion tool that animates "some of Radial's parameters", with an analysis of what's possible. Explore + Plan agents first; decisions via AskUserQuestion: name **Pulsar**; **extract** Radial's geometry core to a shared module (not fork); **lean v1** — parameter tracks + 4 Motion modes + whole-field rotate/zoom + Video & PNG-frame export + 8 scenes, deferring a Soul-style per-primitive stagger/reveal layer and SVG-frame export; and confirmed the other deferrals (Soul-engine DrawSVG/MorphSVG per-primitive effects — a Radial export dropped into Soul already covers those; true animated `.svg`; a Rhizome node; `jitterPhase` line-boil). **Architecture (the analysis's conclusion)**: Soul can't regenerate geometry, so bloom / warp-flow / petal-morph / threshold-dissolve are all outside it — Pulsar instead drives Radial's camelCase `P` with time-varying tracks and calls `Organica.radial.buildScene` → `drawSceneCanvas` every frame (Vortex's redraw-from-scratch model; `buildScene` re-seeds its own RNG from `P.seed` each call, so no drift/accumulation). **`shared/radial.js`** (extracted at the second consumer, same as `noise.js`/`motion.js`): `warpPoint`/`buildPoints`/`arcPathD`/`buildScene`/`sceneSVG`/`drawSceneCanvas`/`BP`/`BUILTIN_PRESETS` moved verbatim + a new `PARAM_RANGES` map. Four inert hooks added, each an exact no-op when unset: `+ (P.warpPhase||0)` on the warp noise-domain offset (field flows), `+ (P.fillPhase||0)` on the fill offset (dissolve wave), `+ (P.loopPhase||0)` inside the chaos-circle noise (tangle writhes), and a `P.rotate`/`P.zoom` group transform in `drawSceneCanvas`/`sceneSVG` only. `markSVG`'s hardcoded `SIZE/2` mark-angle centre parameterised to `prim.W/2, prim.H/2` (identical at 760). `radial/index.html` now just aliases `Organica.radial.*` and keeps its DOM glue. **Verified byte-identical**: captured `currentSVG()` (length + hash) for all 17 Radial built-in presets on `main` before the refactor, re-ran after — **0 mismatches**. **Track model**: `{param, wave, amount, cycles-per-loop, phase, ease}`. `wave` = sine / triangle / looping-noise (circular-sampled `fbm(cosθ, sinθ)` so integer cycles/loop return exactly — Komorebi's single-phase trick) / ramp-once / spin. `amount` is in the parameter's own units, added to the base and clamped to `PARAM_RANGES` (rounded for int params); the amount slider's own range follows the parameter (`count` needs ~760, a fraction ~1) via `amountRangeFor()`. `cycles/loop` is a 1–4 integer so periodic tracks always close the loop; a scene with any `ramp-once` or phase-`spin` track shows a "one-shot — will not loop" note in the panel and the loop popover. Whole-field `rotate` is a `<g transform>` / `ctx.rotate` (the one track later expressible as animated SVG), not baked into every point. **Motion modes** (`applyMode`) fill the 4 track slots + whole-field + loop length, **re-mapped per placement/render** so no mode leaves a dead control — a real bug caught in verification: Drift's default `warpPhase`/`warpAmt` tracks are inert on `arcs`/`loops` renders (which never touch the warp pipeline), and Bloom's `rings` ramp is inert on `loops`; fixed by giving arcs (`arcSpan`/`arcGrowth`/`arcGap`…) and loops (`loopPhase`/`loopCount`…) their own mode track sets, and adding `loopCount` to the Chaos-circles param group (it was missing, so `writeTracks` silently dropped the Bloom track). **Gravity deliberately excluded** — a single global directional pull destroys the centre symmetry that defines a radial field. **Verified end to end** on a fresh no-store server: all 8 scenes render + move, 7 seamless + Phyllo Bloom correctly flagged one-shot; every Motion mode × 9 placement/render combos writes tracks and produces visible motion; PNG frame exports (555 KB at ×2), Video exports a real 3.9 MB `video/mp4;codecs=avc1` that auto-stops at the loop length; Play→Pause (freezes in place) → Play (resumes, no jump) → Stop (frame 0); element guard fires at 7323 el (mesh 72×50 + diagonals), CEIL 6500; zoom/pan CSS transform doesn't touch the PNG bitmap; 60–62 fps on both a light (rings) and mid-weight (fill, ~1500 cells) scene; 0 of ~40 controls unlabeled; console clean (only the pre-existing Vercel-insights 404). Radial re-regressed byte-identical again after all Pulsar edits. Hub nav ("Motion & growth" group, accent `#dd5140` / nav hover `#c4402f`) + `vercel.json` rewrite added. `docs/PULSAR.md` not written (optional; this note + the file's own header comment cover it). Deferred, not built: the Swarm stagger-reveal layer, SVG-frame export (trivially `sceneSVG` + the whole-field `<g transform>`), animated `.svg`, a Rhizome node, `jitterPhase`. **Follow-up same session** (Diego: "verifica che sia il componente Palette centralizzato"): the colour section was first a verbatim copy of Radial's older `syncColor` + bare `<input type=color>` markup (the pre-`color-swatch-wrap` pattern whose latent popup-anchor bug `panel.css` documents). Re-done properly — split into a `Palette` section (**`Organica.createColorSwatch('ink'|'paper', …)`** from `core.js`, the same single-swatch component FVS/Vortex/Colornet use, with `.color-swatch-wrap` markup) + a `Base — seed & jitter` section; local `syncColor` and the local `input[type=color]{…}` hack removed. RMX chips (`createPaletteChips`) deliberately not used — a radial field draws in one ink, `sceneSVG` takes one `P.ink`/`P.paper`. Verified: swatches paint, both edit paths (hex field / native picker) update `buildParams().ink/paper`, scene colours round-trip through save/load, 0 unlabeled, console clean.

- **August 30, 2026 — Palette code centralised into one component: `shared/palette.js` + paired `shared/palette.css`.** Diego: "voglio che tutti i componenti Palette siano in un unico file, serviti diversamente attraverso tutti i tool — iniziamo a centralizzare funzioni." Three Explore agents mapped the state: the RMX chip *builder JS* was already de-duplicated into `organica-palette-chip.js` (7 consumers) but its header still claimed otherwise; `createColorSwatch` lived in `core.js` (5 consumers); `Organica.Palette.colorAt` in `palette.js` (2 consumers, Pollen/Spore); the `.rmx-*` CSS was **not** shared — 5 byte-identical `<style>` copies + a divergent TuneSutra 30×26 variant + Vortex additionally re-declaring `.color-*` at 26×22; `mulberry32` triplicated. Decisions via AskUserQuestion (all after a first pass): **consolidation only** (no tool behaviour change), **merge** the single-swatch and RMX-chip UIs into one polymorphic **`Organica.palette.swatch(target, opts)`** — `target` string ⇒ ATTACH mode (wire pre-existing `#cp-`/`#hex-`/`#sw-<prefix>`), `target` element ⇒ GENERATE mode (build `.rmx-color` chips; `opts.max>1` = RMX strip); both shapes return the **same** object `{get,set,getColors,setColors(arr,{notify}),setActive(i),rebuild}`; **paired CSS file** `palette.css` (not folded into `panel.css` — `.color-*` stays there, universally linked & documented in `/design-system/`). Additive `chips` improvements: `setColors(arr,{notify})` (default off), `setActive(i)` + module-managed `.chip-active` (absorbs TuneSutra's `wrap.children` reach-in). `P.mulberry32` deleted (0 callers) → use core's. Back-compat: `Organica.createColorSwatch` / `createPaletteChips` / `Organica.Palette.colorAt` are thin aliases delegating to `Organica.palette.*` — **every existing call site untouched** (~14 of them); `createColorSwatch`'s legacy `onChange(hex, rgb255)` 2-arg signature preserved by attach mode. `organica-palette-chip.js` **deleted**; its 7 `<script>` tags repointed to `palette.js`; the 7 tools that use generate mode add `<link palette.css>`; pulsar + colornet (attach mode, loaded bare core) gained `<script palette.js>`. Local `<style>` cleanup: deleted the 5 identical `.rmx-*` + 5 `.icon-btn` copies; Spore/Pollen add `#rmx-palette{--rmx-palette-mb:0}` (their strip has no bottom gap); Vortex keeps its `.color-*` overrides (→ Phase 2); TuneSutra keeps its rounded flex-centred `+`/`×` local, tunes the cell via `--rmx-cell-w/h`/`--rmx-cell-radius`/`--rmx-cell-border`; FVS keeps `.fvs-library-item .rmx-x`. Header comment `*/`-in-block-comment bug caught by `node -c`. **Verified** on the no-store server: all 7 chip consumers (camo-turing/membrane/vortex/fvs/tunesutra/pollen/spore) — strip renders same chip count + 36×28 cell (TuneSutra 30×26 rounded `border-strong`, chip-active outline in `--tool`), `+`/`×` add/remove within min/max, chip background repaints on recolour; all 5 attach consumers (membrane/vortex/fvs/pulsar/colornet) — swatch edits repaint `sw-*` and update tool params, 2-arg `onChange` still delivered; `Organica.palette.colorAt` byte-identical across tone/posterize/random/tonernd + solid/adaptive/mix, alias `=== `; Vortex `.color-swatch-wrap` still 26×22 (untouched); zero `<script src=…organica-palette-chip.js>` anywhere (5 remaining string matches are deliberate history prose). `docs/UI-SHELL.md` §2 + §7, `design-system/index.html` (new "Palette — RMX chip strip" section + TOC), CLAUDE.md Repo Structure updated. **Deferred (Phase 2):** migrate the 8 hand-rolled `syncColor` tools to `palette.swatch` attach mode (Komorebi/Warping/Radial/Camo/Halide/Blob + Spore/Pollen `swatch-`→`sw-` id rename); migrate the ~14 alias call sites to `Organica.palette.*` then delete the aliases + core breadcrumb; TuneSutra → `setActive`/`notify`; port Membrane `js/color.js rmxColorAt` + Camo Turing's JS SVG-export path → `palette.colorAt`; reconcile Vortex's `.color-*` overrides; `mulberry32` 3rd copy in `transformer.js`; livingpath bare-input + tunesutra RGB/HSB editor. **Not committed** — pending Diego's "porta in prod" (same branch as the still-uncommitted Pulsar work).

- **August 30, 2026 (still later same day) — Palette consolidation Phase 2: every colour-picker tool now on `Organica.palette.swatch`, aliases removed; new `docs/SHARED-COMPONENTS.md` captures the reusable pattern.** Diego: "salva la tabella come repository e piano di quello che dobbiamo fare. come approccio lo useremo su altri moduli. procedi alla phase 2." **New doc** `docs/SHARED-COMPONENTS.md` — the 8-step centralization playbook (inventory → canonical home → one polymorphic entry point → back-compat aliases → repoint+delete → docs → verify zero-diff → Phase 2 remove aliases), the Palette adoption table, and a backlog of the same-shaped concerns (zoom/pan inline copies, `mulberry32` ×3+9, Seeds panel, `.upload-btn` drop-zone, floatbar `captureStream` recorder). **Wave A** (`sw-`-id tools, byte-parallel `syncColor`): komorebi (4 scene colours), warping, radial, camo-turing (keeps its `THREE.Color` uniform push in `onChange`), blob-boundary (sets `bgRect`/`boundary` SVG `fill` in `onChange`) → `Organica.palette.swatch('<prefix>', {initial, onChange})`, markup wrapped in `.color-swatch-wrap` (fixes the latent popup-anchor bug), local `syncColor` + `input[type=color]{…}` hack deleted, `<script palette.js>` added. **Real bug caught in verification**: radial + warping threw `Cannot access 'renderQueued' before initialization` — the `swatches` block sat where `syncColor` used to (mid-script), and `initial:` → `onChange: scheduleRender` → `if (renderQueued)` hit the `let` in TDZ; fixed by moving the `swatches` population into the INIT block (after `scheduleRender`/its vars), keeping only `const swatches = {}` at the old spot. Now a documented rule in SHARED-COMPONENTS.md. **Wave B** (`swatch-<x>` → `sw-<x>` id rename): halide (`ink/paper/bgfill`, `swapColors()` → `swatches.ink.get()/.set()`), spore (3-arg `syncColor`/`openColorPicker`/`syncHex` deleted; label after the hex field kept), pollen (`setColorAll`/`randomColor`/`COLOR_IDS` deleted; `.ctrl-label` → `.color-name`; random buttons → `#btn-random-<x>` id so the component wires them). **Wave C** (retire aliases): ~14 call sites migrated — `Organica.createColorSwatch(…)` → `Organica.palette.swatch(…)` (membrane/vortex/fvs/pulsar/colornet), `Organica.createPaletteChips({wrap:X, …})` → `Organica.palette.swatch(X, {…})` (camo-turing/membrane/vortex/fvs/tunesutra/pollen/spore), `Organica.Palette.colorAt` → `Organica.palette.colorAt` (pollen/spore); then the whole alias block deleted from `palette.js` + the core breadcrumb trimmed; `Organica.Palette.mulberry32` dropped (grep: 0 callers). **Verified** on the no-store server, all 14 tools: `Organica.createColorSwatch`/`createPaletteChips`/`Organica.Palette` all `undefined`, no tool throws; attach swatches paint from `initial` + edit repaints `sw-*` + tool side effects fire (camo-turing render goes mint-green on ink edit — screenshot; blob `bgRect`/`boundary` fills track; halide `swapColors` swaps); RMX strips add/remove within min/max + repaint on recolour (TuneSutra 30×26 rounded, `(colors,index,kind)` onChange + `highlightActiveChip` still work; fvs min 1); `palette.colorAt` byte-identical (tone `#808080` at 0.5); radial 4-preset regression BYTE-IDENTICAL; 0 console errors beyond the pre-existing environment 404s. Docs: `docs/UI-SHELL.md` §7 `syncColor` note rewritten (resolved), `CLAUDE.md` Repo Structure + this note. **Export check** (Diego: "fai un check sull'export"): drove real SVG/PNG exports on the migrated tools — camo-turing SOLID SVG carries `hex-ink`/`hex-paper`, RMX SVG carries exactly the chip colours (`buildRandomGridSVG(…, rmxColors)` — `rmxColors` stays synced via the migrated `onChange`); radial SVG (ink→stroke, paper→`<rect>`); halide SVG (real dithered portrait, only the 2 swatch colours); fvs component SVG (paper + the 3 cycled palette colours); pollen SVG (`fill="#bg"` + `fill="rgb(255,34,0)"` for the alpha'd point colour); spore colour plumbing + `palette.colorAt` mid-stop. **No export regression.** **TuneSutra Phase-3 done**: `highlightActiveChip()` → one-liner `chips.setActive(state.activeIndex)`; the `rmx-palette.children` reach-in gone, `.chip-active` survives add/remove rebuilds (verified: active follows to the new chip on add, clamps on remove). **Still Phase 3** (own sessions, need output/screenshot sign-off, tracked in `SHARED-COMPONENTS.md` §3): Membrane `rmxColorAt` (its `tonernd` smooth-lerp vs `colorAt`'s discrete stop) + Camo Turing JS-export `rmxLerpColor` (THREE.Color linear-space lerp vs `palette.mix` sRGB) → `palette.colorAt`; Vortex `.color-*` 26×22 → shared 18×18; livingpath bare `<input>` + read-only span → `.color-row` + `palette.swatch`. `transformer.js`'s `mulberry32Fallback` is deliberately kept (guarded `Organica.mulberry32 ? … : fallback`).

- **August 30, 2026 (still later same day) — The two open centralization mini-projects: `Organica.recorder` shipped in full; `Organica.seedsPanel` shipped + adopted by Soul & Membrane (Camo Turing pending).** Diego: "procediamo con l'analisi… i 2 mini-progetti veri." Two Explore inventories mapped every copy; decisions via AskUserQuestion: build **both full**; recorder **Soul is OUT** (SVG-DOM + GSAP, no live canvas — keeps its per-frame rasteriser + local recorder); seedsPanel **full component** (tab strip + panes + slot system + per-tool output adapter). **`shared/recorder.js`** (no paired CSS — ships no UI): `Organica.recorder({canvas,tool,fps,duration,durationPadMs,onStart,onStatus,onStateChange}) → {toggle,start,stop,isRecording}`. `canvas` may be a **function** (p5 tools whose `state.p.canvas` isn't up at construct time). Merged best version of the 5 copies: superset **5-entry MIME list** (`mp4;avc1`→`mp4`→`webm;vp9`→`webm;vp8`→`webm`, each `isTypeSupported` in try/catch), extension **sniffed from the negotiated `mediaRecorder.mimeType`** (not the candidate pair — the browser may pick a codec not first in the list), `duration` omitted = manual-stop / number|fn = auto-stop (a function is resolved at `start()` so **Pulsar** reads `loopSec` then; `durationPadMs:90` keeps the closing frame). Migrated **Pulsar** (`pulsar/index.html:1109-1144`, auto-stop, `onStart:()=>play(true)`), **Camo Turing** (`:2936-2994`, module ctx, `onStart:()=>{if(!running)togglePlay()}`, constructed in INIT), **Vortex** (`js/main.js:269-322`) + **Membrane** (`js/main.js:475-528`) — both already byte-identical, both keep `addEventListener` wiring + write status into `#record-hint`. Each keeps its own floatbar button; `toggleRecording` became a 1-line delegate. **Verified** on the no-store server, all 4: fresh tab, console clean (only the env 404); Start→button relabel + status ("Recording 6s…" preserved on Pulsar); Pulsar auto-stops at `loopSec+90ms` → "Video"/"Recording saved"; manual stop on the other 3; `Organica.download` called once per cycle with `<tool>-<ts>.mp4` + `video/mp4;codecs=avc1` (ext sniffed); force-run path fires playback first. **MediaRecorder byte output is ~0 in this Chromium-only Browser pane for BOTH mp4 and webm** — an environment limit (no real encoder / no canvas-frame capture headless), identical to what the old code produced here; the full behavioural chain is what's verified (already logged in the cross-browser backlog). **`shared/seeds-panel.js` + `seeds-panel.css`**: `Organica.seedsPanel({target,idPrefix,tabs,order,initial,slots,subControls,extraControls,genesis,text,svg,image,applyMode,onSeed,onTabChange,onFontReady,onError}) → {el,getTab,setTab,getDescriptor,getSVGString,apply,emit,slot,refreshGenesisGrid,setFont,setText,destroy}`. Polymorphic on `typeof target` (element = generate / string = attach). **Core decision — emit BOTH**: `onSeed(result)` carries `svgString` (canonical vector, matches Soul + the "primitives are vectors always" contract) **and** a raw `descriptor` (`{kind,formId,svgText,text,font,glyphPaths,fusedPath,imageEl,perGlyph}`), so Camo's canvas-mask path and Membrane's point-array path each keep their own model step — the component centralises only the *picker + the vector serialisation* (Genesis grid, tab strip, panes, SVG read, opentype load/parse/font-upload, per-glyph vs fused SVG assembly, `bakeComputedGeometry`). `text.perGlyph` (Soul/Membrane one `<path>` per glyph vs Camo/LP fused), `applyMode:'immediate'|'manual'` (Camo defers to its own Apply button), `genesis.bakeGeometry` (only Camo — injects `/genesis/animations.css` if absent), `text.preloadFont:false` (Membrane keeps its own `loadMembraneFont`/`seeds/text.js`). **Slot system**: custom tabs (`slots[]`, `render(pane)` called ONCE, pane shown/hidden never rebuilt so bound listeners survive), `subControls.<kind>(pane)` (inject rows into a built-in pane once), `extraControls(host)` (rows below the pane stack). CSS = `.shape-grid`/`.shape-thumb`/`.thumb-num` lifted byte-for-byte from Soul's + Camo's identical local `<style>` blocks; `--seeds-grid-cols` tunable. **`.PRIMORDIAL = [7,56,1,2,14,33,38,31]`** on the component (was byte-identical in 5 files). **Soul migrated** (`soul/index.html`): local seeds `<style>` + `PRIMORDIAL`/`populateGenesisGrid`/`setSeedSource`/`applyTextSeed`/`opentype.load` deleted, markup → `<div id="seeds-host">`, `Organica.seedsPanel({tabs:['genesis','svg','text'], genesis:{initialForm:null}, text:{perGlyph:true, initial:'Soul', applyButton:true}, applyMode:'immediate', onSeed:r=>{if(r.svgString)loadSVG(r.svgString)}})` in INIT; drop-hint "+" + canvas-drop kept (still route to `loadSVG`). Verified: 3 tabs, genesis pick → "1 primitives loaded", "AB" + Apply → 2 primitives (per glyph), CSS computed-identical to the old local rules. **Membrane migrated** (`membrane/`): Procedural + Image are **custom slots** (Membrane keeps 100% of its own image DOM + listeners — `#seedsrc-image` reparented into the slot pane, `imgHolder.hidden=false`), Text is the built-in tab with `preloadFont:false` (`#font-status-hint` reparented via `subControls.text`, `seeds/text.js` + `loadMembraneFont`/`isFontReady` untouched, word field's input → `onSeed` → `state.seedText` + `seedFromText()`), `onTabChange:id=>{state.seedSource=id; syncMembraneSeedRows(); reseedCurrent()}` replaces `syncSeedSourceUI` + the tab listener loop; `#txt-seedtext` listener deleted, font-hint toggle → `.hidden`. Kept + rewired: `rasterizeCurrentSeed` → `seedsB.getDescriptor()` switch (`getSVGString()` for genesis/svg — genesis path is baked+serialised by the component now; `d.text`+`d.font` for text; `d.imageEl` for image); `rasterizeShapeA` → `seedsA.getDescriptor()` likewise; `applyPaperImageMode` → `seedsB.getDescriptor()` (`d.imageEl`/`d.svgText`); `rasterizeTextSource` gains a `fontArg` param (was `withFont`-wrapped, now sync); `rasterizeImageSource` drops its `seedImageEl` default; `applySeed`'s status strings `seedSourceType` → `seedsB.getDescriptor().kind`. `ensureGenesisRasterHost`/`bakeComputedGeometry`/`rasterizeGenesisSource` **kept** as internal helpers — `rasterizeGenesisSource(formId, cw, ch, done)` parameterised by form id — only the 3 `window.test*` two-shape probes still call it (they want a fixed form regardless of the panel's tab). **Verified live** (fresh no-store tab, console clean bar the env 404): both panels build (B genesis+form 1 active / A genesis+form 2 active, 8 thumbs each); genesis-form seed → a real reaction-diffusion ring pattern (bakeGeometry pipeline works end-to-end); **text "S" via the component's opentype font → a clean "S" letterform pattern**; fill / trace / boundary all apply without error; Two-seeds ON → `#seedsrc-a-block` + `#seedb-label` show, famine apply clean; `bgimage` with no image → the correct "needs an Image or SVG source" message (proves `applyPaperImageMode` reads the descriptor). Living Path's own Font/SVG/Genesis picker stays bespoke on purpose — its `.seg`/`data-src`/`.on` tab strip, `[data-pane]`+`hidden` panes, `.forms`/`.f` grid with its own CSS, visible `.drop` zones, CDN opentype, and the "Font" tab being a full OTF-export workbench (glyph-range chips / alternates / `.lvp` projects) genuinely don't map onto the shared component, and it links none of the shared panel CSS; a full adoption is gated on a separate "Living Path → shared shell" pass.
