- **August 30, 2026 (still later same day) — Shared-library files renamed: the redundant `organica-` / `organic-` prefix dropped; third-party isolated into `shared/vendor/`.** Diego: "i file iniziano tutti con organica… se un domani dovessimo cambiare il nome sarebbe un problema. i file devono avere una semantica e naming in linea con regole di buon sviluppo del codice." Two Explore inventories first (reference map + dynamic-path risk; the JS/CSS/storage identifier map). **The `shared/` folder already namespaces its files, so `organica-` on every filename repeated the project name and coupled ~30 HTML files to it.** Confirmed safe: no build, no CI, no `package.json`; `vercel.json` rewrites are prefix passthroughs (no filenames); **exactly one** dynamically-built path (`seeds-panel.js` `ensureAnimationsCss` — a substring guard + a hardcoded `/genesis/organic-animations.css` fallback); every other `organica-*`/`organic-*` in JS is a comment. Decisions via AskUserQuestion: **flat, prefix removed** (the folder is the namespace; JS+CSS pairs stay side by side); **Genesis renamed too** (same rule — resolves the three-way `organica` / `organic-strata` / `organic-` spelling split); **all 8 vendored files → `shared/vendor/`** (font included — makes the flat first-party names unambiguous). **Renamed** (`git mv`, history follows): `shared/organica-core.js`→`core.js`, `-tokens.css/.json`→`tokens.*`, `-header/-panel/-floatbar/-shell/-mobile-gate.css`→bare, `-palette.js/.css`→`palette.*`, `-noise/-motion/-radial/-paper/-transformer/-recorder.js`→bare, **`-seedspanel.js/.css`→`seeds-panel.js/.css`** (also un-ran-together the name — the JS identifier stays `Organica.seedsPanel`); `genesis/organic-forms.js`→`forms.js`, `-defs.js`→`defs.js`, `-animations.css`→`animations.css`, `-page.css`→`page.css`; **`genesis/organic-library.css` retired** (dead `@import` shim — nothing `<link>`ed it; `page.css`+`animations.css` link directly now). **Moved to `shared/vendor/`**: `gsap.min.js` + `gsap-morphsvg/drawsvg`, `kiwi.min.js`, `three.module.js`, `opentype.min.js`, `paper-full.min.js`, `manrope-variable.ttf`. **References rewritten** in one atomic commit via a scripted `perl` sweep over all tracked `*.{html,js,css,md}`: literal `<link>`/`<script>` (all ~28 tool `index.html` + `_template.html` + `design-system` + `genesis/*.html` + hub + the `_test-*.html` scratch files), the one dynamic path (`seeds-panel.js` guard `'organic-animations.css'`→`'animations.css'` + fallback `/genesis/organic-animations.css`→`/genesis/animations.css`), the ES import (`camo-turing` `import … from '/shared/vendor/three.module.js'`), the two dynamic font refs (`seeds-panel.js` `fetch` + `membrane/js/seeds/text.js` `opentype.load` → `/shared/vendor/manrope-variable.ttf`), the load-order header comments in every renamed file (`noise.js`/`radial.js`/etc. sibling names + `radial.js`'s runtime `throw`), `THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md` (the `shared/vendor/` paths), and the docs (this file's Repo-Structure tree + Critical Rules, `UI-SHELL.md` §2, `SHARED-COMPONENTS.md` playbook step 2, `SHARED-LIBRARY.md` + `ANIMATION-SYSTEM.md` — the "add new form animations to X" guidance now points at `genesis/animations.css`, `README.md`, `SOUL.md`, `LOOM.md`). **Deliberately NOT touched** (an "if we ever rebrand" checklist, in the commit body): `window.Organica` / `Organica.*` (a library carrying its name as its global is standard — `THREE`, `gsap`, `jQuery`; ~800 refs, aliasable in one line if ever needed); `ORGANIC_FORMS` / `ORGANIC_MOTION` globals; `'organic-forms'` the built-in-set data id in Genesis; `.org-*` CSS classes (component-namespace prefix — and already inconsistent, most control classes carry none); `organica.<tool>.*` localStorage keys (**persisted user data** — needs a forward-migration per the Critical Rule); `'organica-svg'` Figma postMessage type (cross-process contract with the plugin); the `<title> … — Organica` / `<b>Organica</b>` logo text (~60 cosmetic); the repo/folder name `organic-strata`. **Verified**: `grep` for any `organica-*.{js,css,json}` / `organic-{forms,defs,animations,page,library}.{js,css}` filename ref → zero. Browser pass on the no-store server — every tool + hub + `genesis/` + `design-system/` load styled, console clean (only the env 404), no `/shared/*` or `/genesis/*` 404s; exercised the risky paths (Camo Turing `three.module.js` ES import + a baked Genesis seed → `genesis/animations.css` via `ensureAnimationsCss`; Soul/Membrane Text seed → `shared/vendor/manrope-variable.ttf`; Loom `kiwi.min.js` grid; Genesis `paper-full.min.js` Draw). The `.claude/worktrees/` mirror keeps the old names — ignored (detached branch). **Committed + pushed to `main`.**

- **August 30, 2026 (still later same day) — Genesis restructured: from a 4-mode motion workbench to "the seed library".** Diego: keep only base forms that integrate with the seed module + Set creation; single-click a seed → its data, double-click → Create/editor; strip Indicators to the filter-bar layout and archive the animations; drop the right Grid column; fold Import into Create; land in the full library. "valuta tutto sia da un punto di vista di UX che concettuale." Assessment (agreed): the 55 animated forms never functioned as *seeds* — only 8 (`PRIMORDIAL`) fed other tools; motion is Soul + Pulsar's job; two parallel catalogs (`forms.js` vs `indicators.html`'s hand-inlined 55) were a drift risk. **Decisions via AskUserQuestion:** Set = a plain ordered list of seed ids (drop `gridConfig`/`formLayout`/spans/alignment/drag-fill); `ORGANIC_FORMS` genuinely shrinks to 13 (ids unchanged `[1,2,3,7,9,13,14,21,26,28,37,41,56]`; slug-key/metadata restructure = tracked follow-up); one built-in **"Base Seeds"** set = 13 organic (synth) + 6 procedural primitives (synth), merging in the old separate `basic-seeds` set; single-click → inline full-row detail card, double-click → Create editor (fork built-ins); keep the left Sets sidebar, right panel Create-only, main = filter bar (Source / Type / Density) + responsive auto-fill grid; Duplicate while Base Seeds active → auto "My Seeds" user set. **`genesis/index.html`:** removed the Compose mode wholesale (+ its ~340-line JS section, CSS, markup grid), gutted the layout model (`applyGridVars`/`getLayoutMap`/`layoutFor`/`setLayoutFor`/`updateFormLayout`/`updateGridConfig`/`alignToCSS`/`selectForm`/… all deleted), `fixForm35()` gone. New: `ORGANIC_KEEP`/`PROCEDURAL_IDS`/`PROCEDURAL_CANON`/`MY_SEEDS_SET_ID` consts; `proceduralForm(id)` (synth, not stored); `migrateLibrary(lib)` (idempotent, never drops `forms[]` — ensures/renames the `organic-forms` set to "Base Seeds", removes `basic-seeds`, drops plain canonical primitive entries but keeps user-renamed/referenced ones, strips `gridConfig`/`formLayout`); `resolveFormById` reordered (organic-N synth → stored lookup → procedural synth fallback — fixes a user-renamed primitive showing canonical); `getFormsForSet`/`filteredForms`/`renderGrid`/`createTile`/`buildDetailCard`/`svgFacts`/`setsContaining`/`ensureMySeedsSet`/`duplicateSeed`/`deleteSeed`/`syncFilterUI`/`openInCreateEditor`/`syncImportUI`/`loadDensity`. `.app` is 2-col (`[sets | grid]`); `.app--create` adds the right panel. Import folded into Create as an always-visible "Import SVG" section; every `S.mode === 'import'` → `!!S.importInner`; `saveForm` guards the `genType` recipe on `!S.importInner` and ends with `openSeedDetailById`. Density persists in `localStorage['organica.library.density']`. **`genesis/forms.js`** reduced 56 → 13 (form 7's `.sp` splash baked to literal `cx/cy/r`); `ORGANIC_LABELS` trimmed. **`genesis/indicators.html`** → thin redirect to `/genesis/`; the full page moved to **`genesis/archive/indicators-55.html`** (self-contained, back-link fixed to `/genesis/`). **Ripple:** `shared/seeds-panel.js` `PRIMORDIAL` → the 13; `pollen/index.html` + `spore/index.html` local literal → the 13 (+ "keep in sync" note); `livingpath/index.html` `ORGANIC_FORMS[38]`→`[13]`, `payload.formId || 38`→`|| 13`; hub `index.html` `BENTO_FORMS` rebuilt to the 13 surviving ids, `fillPhyllotaxisSpiral()` + its call deleted, bento-link text de-staled. **Verified** on the no-store server: Genesis fresh → Library home, 2 tabs, Base Seeds = 19 tiles (13 organic + 6 primitives), filter bar, single-click detail card (Type `asset · built-in`, ID, viewBox, elements, In sets; Name readonly, no Delete), double-click → Create 3-col with the shape on the artboard, filters (Organic → 13 / Primitives → 6 / My seeds → 0), Duplicate a primitive while Base Seeds active → auto "My Seeds" set with "Circle copy"; `migrateLibrary` idempotent. Hub: 24 bento cells, 0 empty, `fillPhyllotaxisSpiral` undefined. Soul: 13 thumbs, `PRIMORDIAL` = 13 ids. Camo Turing: 26 thumbs (2 panels), canvas renders. Pollen: 13 forms + its own "wavy line" primitive. Spore: 13 marks, right labels. Living Path: 13-form Genesis grid, no `[38]`. FVS / Colornet: boot clean reading `organica.library.forms`. All redirect stubs (`indicators`/`creator`/`library`.html) → `/genesis/`; archive page serves (63 KB, forms intact). Every page console-clean bar the pre-existing `_vercel/insights` 404. Docs updated: `docs/SHARED-LIBRARY.md` (blob shape, Base Seeds built-in, `migrateLibrary`), `docs/UI-SHELL.md` (Genesis = 2 modes, `.app`/`.app--create`, `PRIMORDIAL` = 13), `docs/ANIMATION-SYSTEM.md` (Genesis static, catalog archived, `fixForm35` retired), `docs/SHARED-COMPONENTS.md` (`PRIMORDIAL` = 13 + pollen/spore literals), CLAUDE.md (Tools table + Repo tree + Critical Rules). **Follow-up (named in `docs/ROADMAP.md`):** `forms.js` deep restructure — slug keys, per-form metadata block, drop the `class="aNN"` hooks, update every consumer + `PRIMORDIAL` to slugs. - **August 31, 2026 (later same day) — Genesis Create: "Save to Library" moved to a floatbar popover.** Diego: the Save-to-Library button should be a floatbar icon (tooltip "Save to library"); clicking it opens a popover with the settings that were in the right-panel **Seed** section. **`genesis/index.html`:** the whole `<section>` "Seed" (Name / Type pills / underlying-layer block / Save-to `<select>` + new-set row / `#btn-save`) was relocated into **`#save-popover`** — a `.org-popover` inside a `.org-popover-wrap` in the floatbar, opened by **`#btn-save-open`** (a disk icon) via `Organica.popover()`. `#btn-save-edit` was **removed** — Edit's Save is now the same `#btn-save-open`/popover (so Name/Type stay editable in Edit); `#save-to-block` keeps `data-modes="create"` so Edit's popover has no Save-to. `saveForm()` + `confirmAndSave()` now return `true`/`false` so the popover only closes on a successful save. `confirmAndSave` lost its `btn`/armed param. **`populateSets()`** now lists **every** set in Library-bar order (built-in "Base Seeds" shown but `disabled` — can't save into the synthesized set) + `+ New set…`, and defaults to `state.activeSetId` (falling back to the first user set); `resetCreate()` clears `#ctrl-set` + re-runs `populateSets()` on entering Create so the dropdown tracks whatever set the Library was showing. `saveForm` bails with a message if a built-in set is somehow selected. **Verified** on the no-store dev server: panel has no Seed section; Create — draw → `#btn-save-open` enables → popover (Name focused, Type 2×2 grid, Save-to, "Save to Library") → save mints a `user-*` seed, popover closes, returns to Library; Edit built-in — dirty → popover "Save a copy" → forks to My Seeds, `S.editForm` becomes `user-*`, stays in Edit; Edit user seed — rename in popover + "Save changes" → `forms[].svg`/`name` rewritten, `S.dirty` false; Mask pill → underlying-layer block shows in the popover; `+ New set…` → new-set row shows. Console clean. `node -c` clean. Docs: `docs/UI-SHELL.md`, `CLAUDE.md` (Tools row + this note). **Committed** next.

- **August 31, 2026 (later same day) — Soul deleted.** Diego: "valuta, ma credo che soul abbia zero valore e penso di eliminarlo." Three Explore passes concurred: Soul's premise ("animate ANY tool's SVG export") is only half-delivered — `parsePrimitives` walks a hard-coded 7-tag allowlist (`circle/ellipse/rect/path/polygon/polyline/line`), silently dropping `<text>`/`<use>`/`<image>`/filters/gradients/clip-paths/embedded rasters (Camo Turing's raster-SVG export → nothing; Living Path's 24 raster presets → nothing); no `transform` composition; wobble stalls past ~3000 primitives; SVG export is a *static* mid-motion snapshot (no animated SVG / Lottie); nothing downstream consumes a Soul output; the Rhizome `soul-pass` bridge node was an inert pass-through. Pulsar owns the one motion input anyone pipelines (Radial → looping WebM); Membrane / Vortex / Blob Boundary carry their own intrinsic motion. **Decisions (AskUserQuestion):** full removal; `shared/motion.js` stays intact (Rhizome still uses `parsePrimitives` for its `svg-to-points` node + `svg→points` adapter — `PATTERNS`/`staggerDelay`/`animate`/`morph`/`organicBlobPath` go dead-but-harmless); `docs/SOUL.md` → `docs/archive/SOUL.md`. **Deleted:** `soul/index.html`, `shared/vendor/gsap-drawsvg.min.js` (the one fully-orphaned asset — Blob Boundary loads GSAP core + MorphSVG but never DrawSVG), `rhizome/js/nodes/soul-bridge.js` (+ its 2 lines in `node-registry.js`; `soul-pass` node id gone — a saved graph with one would fail to load, acceptable, none are persisted). **Edited:** hub `index.html` (nav link + `.nav__link--soul` CSS + bento `TOOL_PREVIEWS` entry + `.tp-soul` keyframe rules), `vercel.json` (rewrite), `shared/tokens.json` (`soul` accent), `THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md` (GSAP entry → Blob Boundary, drop DrawSVG; opentype/p5/Manrope "Used by"), `shared/motion.js` header, `docs/RHIZOME.md` (bridge table 10→9), `docs/ANIMATION-SYSTEM.md` / `docs/VISION.md` / `docs/ROADMAP.md` / `docs/DESIGN-SYSTEM.md` / `docs/SHARED-COMPONENTS.md` / `docs/SHARED-LIBRARY.md` / `docs/UI-SHELL.md` / `design-system/index.html` (de-stale), CLAUDE.md (Tools row + tree + stack table + this note). Comment-only Soul mentions in mycel/pulsar/fvs/membrane/livingpath left as history. **Verified** on the no-store dev server: hub loads, "Motion & growth" nav has 5 links, bento no empty cell; Rhizome loads (ES-module `node-registry.js` import graph still resolves), "add node" menu bridge category has no Soul, an `svg-to-points` node still parses via `Organica.motion.parsePrimitives`; Blob Boundary Play still morphs (GSAP core + MorphSVG intact); membrane/camo-turing/livingpath/design-system boot clean; `vercel.json` + `tokens.json` parse; `grep -ri soul` → only the deliberately-left comments. **Not committed.**

- **September 26, 2026 (even later) — FVS: debug pass on the "Figure all in one" algorithms.** Diego: "fai un debug ora sugli algoritmi 'figure all in one'". A background Explore agent mapped the Rules engine (`slotClassContext`/`ruleMatches`/`applyClassRules`/`isFormRule`), cell painting (`paintCell`), and the checks/stats engine (`figureChecks`/`state.figureLevelStats`) against what `runFigureRecipe` actually renders; live testing in parallel covered the quick Symbol-type pickers, chained grids, and mirror+rotate combos. **Bugs found and fixed, all `fvs/index.html`:**
  1. **The hex-ring range from the same-day size-ladder session was miscalibrated.** `syncFgNOptions()`'s comment assumed `hexLoomModel(n)`'s cell count follows `1+3n(n+1)` (giving "n=3→37, n=4→61") and set the range to **3–4** accordingly — but calling `hexLoomModel` directly on the live page showed the real formula is `1+3n(n-1)` (n=3→19, n=4→37, n=5→61), off by one ring. Fixed the range to **4–5** (37/61 cells, correctly inside the 25–64 target band) and corrected the comment.
  2. **`figureFirstLevelOf()` — the function behind the quick Triangle/Square/Hexagon thumbnail buttons on the Symbol step — had its own separate hardcoded `rows:2`/`rings:2`/`cols:2,rows:2` defaults, entirely bypassing the whole size-ladder change.** Caught live: clicking "Hexagon" fresh still built a 7-cell figure (rings 2) despite every other picker now defaulting into the 25–64 band. Fixed to `rows:5`/`rings:4`/`cols:5,rows:5` (the lower bound of each range, matching the convention used elsewhere) — verified live: Hexagon → 37 cells, Triangle → 25, Square → 25.
  3. **Confirmed bug from the mapping agent: `figureFormFromRecipe`'s rule→form sync loop ignored `r.off`**, unlike `isFormRule` (which correctly treats an off rule as not form-representable). A switched-off custom rule (e.g. a triangle "up" or "down" class rule turned off via the rule-chip UI) would still be read as active when populating the Advanced form (`fg-up`/`fg-down`/etc.), and the next unrelated form edit (`onForm` → `figureRecipeFromForm`) would silently push a **new, active** duplicate of the rule — reactivating something the user had explicitly turned off, with the original off'd rule still sitting alongside it. Fixed with a one-line guard (`if (r.off) return;`) at the top of the sync loop. Verified with a direct repro (an off `{class:'up', content:'empty'}` rule) both before and after the fix — before: `fg-up` read back as `'empty'` and an unrelated ink edit added a second active `{class:'up',...}` rule; after: `fg-up` correctly reads `'filled'` (its default) and the off rule is preserved untouched, no duplicate.
  4. **A real gap in `validateFigureRecipe`, also from the mapping agent: a recipe could carry `transform.rotate`/`.mirror` with zero Grid levels** (`levels.length < 2`) and pass validation, even though `runFigureRecipe`'s only transform-consumer is `buildFvsGridSVG` (the Grid step's own renderer) — with no grid level, the transform is silently never applied, and `figureChecks`'s mirror-symmetry check would then evaluate symmetry on the never-mirrored SVG, potentially reporting a false "ok" by coincidence. The UI already prevents reaching this state normally (Mirror/Rotate controls only appear once a Grid exists), but hand-authored recipe JSON could still trigger it. Fixed by rejecting such a recipe outright in `validateFigureRecipe` ("A transform (rotate/mirror) needs at least one Grid level to apply to"), consistent with the file's existing "an invalid recipe must never become the current one" philosophy. Verified: a gridless recipe with `mirror:'v'` is now rejected, the same recipe with a Grid level appended is accepted.
  **Investigated and ruled out (real numeric verification, not eyeballing):** a rotate-90°+mirror+chained-two-grid composition that visually looked "clipped" (a horizontal band with white top/bottom) turned out to be a correctly-rendered 440×440 square SVG (confirmed via `getBBox()` — content fills the viewBox exactly, `pathCount` matches the expected `filled × tiles₁ × tiles₂ × mirrorCopies` math) being letterboxed inside the app's taller (618×824) preview container — not a rendering bug. Two narrower gaps documented but **not fixed** (both only reachable via hand-edited recipe JSON, not through the UI): `isFormRule`'s row-rule matcher can collapse two separate hand-authored single-row rules into one on any Advanced-form sync (the form only has one combined `fg-emptyrows` field); `paintCell`'s Shift-click ("whole class") computes the new value from only the one clicked cell's current state and applies it to the whole class — correct given rules are declarative, but can look like "corruption" if cells in that class had already diverged from a partial rule match. Rules engine matching (`ruleMatches`/`slotClassContext`), the checks/stats multiplication across chained+mirrored grids, and every pipeline-thumbnail/gallery/variation code path (`figureStepSVGs`, gallery, variations) were all confirmed to funnel through the single `runFigureRecipe` render path with no drift. Regression suite re-run clean at 331/331 after every fix. Not committed.

- **September 26, 2026 (later still) — FVS: the Element→Component→Symbol→Figure size ladder corrected.** Diego, after a live Element→Figure test (Circle seed all the way through): the mental model should be a strict complexity ladder — Element (one shape) → Component (an N-type grid of it, already 1–4×1–4) → Symbol (a properly complex figure, **above 4×4, not more than 8×8**) → Figure (the final composition on a **way bigger canvas**) — and today's ranges didn't reflect that (Symbol's 12 Loom generators each had independent, often-smaller-than-Component bounds; Figure's tiling topped out at 6×6). Full plan-mode pass first (`~/.claude/plans/zany-leaping-beaver.md`): read every generator's real cell-count formula off `loom/js/generators/*.js` directly rather than guessing (bento/sinusoidal/radial/rectangular are exact `cols×rows`-family formulas; fractal is a strict `2^depth` binary split; organic/spiral's own param is ≈ the cell count directly; hexagonal/triangular/diamond/circular clip an oversized lattice, count scales with `cols` but isn't closed-form). Decisions via AskUserQuestion: Component unchanged; **all 12** Symbol generators reinterpreted into one consistent band (not just the literally-grid-shaped ones, since more generators are coming and should start in the same band); Figure's Composition/Grid enum ceiling widened to square12×12.
  **`fvs/index.html`**: `SYMGRID_GENS` — every generator's cols/rows (or rings/sectors) clamped to **5–8** so cell count lands in **25–64** (a 5×5 to 8×8 square's own range) — Bento/Sinusoidal/Masonry/Hexagonal/Triangular/Diamond/Circular/Radial all 5–8; Organic `points` and Spiral `count` (each ≈ cell count directly) set to **25–64**; Fractal `depth` — since `cells = 2^depth` exactly — is the one deliberate exception, **5–6** (32 or 64 cells, the only two integers that land in-band); Rectangular has no slider (cols/rows = however many comma-separated weights are typed) so gained a **`clampWeightTrackCount()`** helper, called from `symbolGridModel()`, padding/truncating the typed weight list to 5–8 entries at generate time (default bumped `'1,1,1'`→`'1,1,1,1,1'`, hint text added). Symbol's legacy "Other grids" square/triangle pickers (`sel-symbol-square-cols/rows`, `sel-symbol-tri-n`) — populate loop `2..8`→`5..8`, default `2`→`5`. Figure's own inline mini-Symbol lattice (`fg-lattice`/`fg-n`/`fg-cols`) got the same 5–8 treatment via a new **`syncFgNOptions()`** (hex rings get their own **3–4** band — `1+3n(n+1)` cells grows too fast for a flat 5–8, n=3→37/n=4→61) — **but this broke the Advanced-form roundtrip for every built-in catalog recipe using a small triangle/hex lattice** (Sierpinski, Trapezoid, Lattice 4, the recursive Sierpinski/tiers presets — all legitimately use rows/rings under 5), caught by the regression suite, not by inspection. Fixed by making `syncFgNOptions()` **non-destructively preserve** an out-of-band value already on the select (adds it as a one-off extra `<option>` instead of clamping it away) rather than reverting the range — new figures still default into 5–8, loading any existing recipe still round-trips exactly. Figure's Composition enum (`fg-comp`/`fg-comp2`) filled in completely from square2×2 to **square12×12** (was capped at 6×6, skipping 5×5); this exposed two latent parsing bugs that would've silently mis-rendered the new sizes — `FG_LATTICE_OF`'s `/^square(\d)/` (single-digit regex, would read "square12x12" as `n=1`) → `/^square(\d+)/`, and `getFvsGrid()`'s hardcoded `type==='square3x3'?3:...?:2` ternary (anything not in its literal list silently fell back to `2`) → a generic regex parse. `FIGURE_MAX_SHAPES` raised 20000→**40000** (a maxed Symbol × one Figure Grid level × a mirror now reaches ~36,864 — the old cap would've legitimately blocked exactly the bigger compositions this change is for; the most extreme *chained* two-grid combinations still hit the new cap, by design).
  **Verified**: full regression suite (`fvs/_test-regression.html`) — first run surfaced 14 real diffs, traced every one to an explained cause (the two grid-size test fixtures at rows/cols 2 and 4, now invalid against the new legacy-picker floor, updated to 5 in `_test-regression.html`; the rest are legitimate SVG/cell-count changes from the resized generators and rectangular clamp); after the roundtrip fix, re-ran clean at **11 changed, 0 missing**, deterministic across repeats, baseline re-recorded (`_regression-baseline.json`) → **all 331 cases match**. Live: Fractal defaults to Depth 5 → "32 cells" exactly; Radial defaults to Rings 6/Sectors 6 → "36 cells" exactly; Rectangular typing `"2,1"` (2 weights) still builds a 25-cell grid (padded to 5); legacy Square/Triangle pickers default to 5; full pipeline smoke test (Circle Element → Bento Symbol, 25 cells → "Build a Figure from this Symbol" → Grid tier square9×9, 81 tiles → Mirror right edge) renders correctly with the Checks panel green, console clean throughout (only the pre-existing benign Canvas2D `willReadFrequently` warning). Not committed — pending Diego's "porta in prod".

- **September 26, 2026 (latest) — FVS: Symbol → Figure bridge.** A new Symbol-tier floatbar button, **Build a Figure from this Symbol** (`#btn-symbol-to-figure`, group `#fb-symbol-actions`, shown by `setTier()`), hands the live Symbol to the ★ Figure tier as its first level. No new level kind: a `symbol` level may now carry `lattice: {type:'loomModel', model}` (the whole Loom grid, Canvas frame included) + `cells` (the exact `state.symbolCells`) + `componentEntries` (inline copies of every saved Component the cells use, Container/Mask references followed) + `colors`/`colorRule`/`paperColor`/`clip`. `isSealedSymbol(l)` = has `cells`; `runSealedSymbolLevel()` loads it (registers missing Components into `LIBRARY`, never overwriting a same-named one; throws on a cell/grid count mismatch). - **September 28, 2026 — FVS Component: nothing hidden + layer-ink colour variants.** Diego: debug the Component generation. (1) "see all the possibilities based on the selected dropdown, no hide"; (2) "now that we have colors per layer I want to see all the variants generated". Plan-mode pass, then a live matrix (every Rule × Circle/Triangle × 1×1/2×2/3×3/4×4). **Two kinds of hiding found and removed, `fvs/index.html`:** (a) `generate()`'s `lookAlikeFilter()` rasterised every candidate and dropped any that painted like one already shown (a Circle's 8 Pinwheels → 1, "7 look-alike candidates hidden"). Removed along with `componentVisualSignature`/`signaturesMatch`. The exact canonical fold (`dedupeComponents`, flip H+V ≡ 180°) stays, since it produces identical files. (b) Found live and not in the original plan: with the default axes (Flip off), **Mirror / Row mirror / Column mirror silently fell back to one Identity candidate**. The old baseline literally recorded 3×3/4×4 Mirror as "all cells unrotated". `familyRuleGallery` no longer gates a picked named rule on its `requiresAxis`; the pick implies the axis it needs. Only Checkerboard (its states come entirely from the axes) still falls back to Identity with every axis off, and `syncRuleAxisNote` now speaks only about that case. **Layer-ink variants:** a candidate may carry `comp.layerInks {layerId: 'cell'|slot}`. `expandLayerInks()` multiplies every geometric candidate by `layerInkCombos()`, the cartesian product of each Fill layer × (Follow cell + each palette ink), with the Element's current inks first so the default-selected candidate paints like the live Element. The result is capped at `EXHAUSTIVE_CAP` 512 with an honest "Showing 512 of N" status (`state.galleryCapNote`), and the Exhaustive hint shows the product. Rendering goes through a `layerInkOverride` sibling of `inkPaletteOverride`, read by `layerInkColor` and set around each thumbnail in `renderGallery`. Clicking a variant **adopts** its inks into `state.layers` (`adoptLayerInks`), so Export/Tile/Edit/Symbol need no change; verified byte-identical to the thumbnail bar the timestamp. `buildLibraryEntryFor` bakes a variant's inks into the saved seed (`seedWithLayerInks`), and the caption appends `inks c/2`. Manual snapshots the current inks. **Follow-up (same day):** made optional — an org-switch *Layer colour variants* (`#chk-layer-inks`, on by default, visible only while `state.layers` exists) under the Rule dropdown; off → `layerInkCombos()` returns none, geometry only. `layers:inks:toggleOff` added (361 cases). **Second follow-up — Segment turned every layer into strokes (bug):** Style (Fill/Stroke) was one Element-wide setting and picking Segment forced it to Stroke, so a Circle on layer 1 rendered `fill="none"` as soon as layer 2 became a Segment (reproduced live). Style is now per layer: `layer.style`, read by `layerFillMode(l, a)` in `stackPathMarkup`/`paintStackCanvas` (a layer saved before has no `style` and falls back to the Element's Style, so every existing hash is unchanged). With a stack the Style control shows and edits the active layer (`showLayerStyle` on select/add/remove, the `sel-element-fillmode` listener writes `layer.style`, `syncActiveLayer` stamps it). The Segment auto-switch remembers `styleBeforeSegment` on the layer itself. A new layer inherits the current layer's Style, except after a Segment, whose Stroke is forced. `layers:segment:perLayer` added (362 cases). **Third follow-up — Width on one layer stretched the whole Element (bug):** same root cause; Width/Length (and Stroke W, Rounded caps) were Element-wide, applied as one stretch around the whole stack. Generalised `layer.style` into `layer.look {fillMode, strokeW, rounded, w, l}`, read via `layerLook(l, a)`. The layer's own stretch goes inside its transform (`…scale(s) scale(w,l) translate(-50,-50)`), mirrored in `stackFlatD` and `paintStackCanvas`, and it enters the clip-uid/cache keys only when non-identity, so every existing hash is unchanged. For a live stack, `getElementAppearance()` returns `w=l=1`, so Width/Length edit only the active layer; `onAppearanceChange` stores the controls on the active layer, and `showLayerStyle` loads them on select/add/remove. Appearance Scale/Move stay Element-wide. `variantAppearance` (Export Variants) still overrides Style/Stroke W. A stack saved before this keeps its whole-stack stretch in saved thumbnails, and `applyAppearanceToUI` stamps the loaded appearance onto look-less layers when it's opened live. SVG/Canvas parity 0 px. `layers:width:*` added (364 cases). **Fourth follow-up — Element Content (Mask / Subtraction + Pick underlying component) 'doesn't work', analysed:** (1) by design (Phase 1) it only ever reached the Element step's own previews, via `buildSeedPreviewSVG`; the Component/Symbol/Grid/export steps never used it and it isn't persisted, which is the main reason it looked broken; (2) the picker lists saved Components only (empty library → empty picker); (3) it knew nothing about layers. Diego: focus on the Element for now. Content is now **per layer** in the Element step: `layer.content`/`layer.under`, edited through `elementContentTarget()`, which is the active layer with a stack and the Element-wide fields otherwise. `stackPathMarkup(…, withContent)` (only `buildSeedPreviewSVG` passes true) draws the picked Component clipped to the layer outline (Subtraction) or masked out of it (Mask), ids `stk…-c<i>-<name>`. `underlyingComponentMarkup()` was factored out of `buildSeedContentSVG` and now renders with the entry's own palette (`resolveUnderlyingComponent` returns `colors`). `contentFromElement()` moves a single shape's Content onto layer 1 on the first Add layer, and the collapse moves it back. **A real bug found while testing:** my first helper was named `contentTarget()`, which clashes with the Symbol step's `contentTarget()` (a later function declaration wins in a classic script), so the Content dropdown silently did nothing. Renamed to `elementContentTarget`. 6 `content:layer:*` cases, 370 total. Live numbers: 2×2 Triangle — Pinwheel 8, Mirror 4, Diagonal 4, Checkerboard 12, Row/Col mirror 4, Radial 8, Exhaustive 32; 2 layers × 3 inks → Pinwheel 128 (16 distinct colourings per shape), Exhaustive exactly 512; 3 layers → "Showing 512 of 2,048". Suite 360: renamed `engine:lookalike:circle` → `engine:nohide:circle` (8), added `engine:mirror:flipOff` + 9 `layers:inks:*` cases, re-baselined the mirror-family diffs (all traced to (b)). **Pre-existing flake noted:** `play:shuffle:ui` (Figure Space-key shuffle) intermittently reads `false,false,false`. It got recorded once; the baseline was restored to `true,true,true`, and the next runs pass 360/360. Not committed.

- **September 28, 2026 (later) — FVS Segment: filled bar, square ends, Repeat X/Y fill the canvas.** Diego (IT): Segment should default to no Rounded caps, default to Fill (not Stroke), and Repeat X/Y should fill the whole canvas. Before: Segment was a zero-area centre line (so choosing it forced Style → Stroke), and Repeat X/Y used a fixed pitch (Space X/Y) around the centre, which `overflowNorm` then shrank. **Now:** `segmentBar()` (FVS-local, pure maths, memoised) thickens the shared `segmentGeometry()` centre lines into closed ribbons in the final 0..100 space. It adds *Thickness* `segWeight` (1–40, default 8) and *Round ends* `segRound` (off by default); every ribbon is wound positive so overlaps (Rays, Lines) stay filled under nonzero, and a bar that overflows the canvas is fitted back in using bounds taken from its own points. The Segment auto-switch now turns **Rounded caps** off (per layer, `roundedBeforeSegment`) and restores them on leaving, instead of forcing Stroke. Shared `shapes.js` `segmentBuild` gained an opt-in `o.tile` (only FVS passes it; Genesis unchanged): repeatX × repeatY tiles, each copy's 0..100 box fitted into its tile, Space X/Y = gap % of a tile (0–90; FVS sets the sliders' min to 0). Diego chose to **convert** existing Segments: a seed with no `segWeight` is drawn as an 8-wide bar (a stroke-style saved one becomes an outlined bar). **Bug found while testing:** straight 2-point lines ribboned into a *triangle* (`if (n > 2)` skipped `right[0]`). Fixed in `segmentBar` and in the older `strokeToShapeD` (Split's open-stroke conversion), which had the same line. `seed:segment` and `layers:segment:perLayer` re-baselined, 7 `segment:*` cases added (377). Raster-verified: a single bar is 15→85 × 46→54, 4×5 at gap 0 runs edge to edge, Rays and round ends stay inside the canvas, and SVG/Canvas parity is 0 px. Not committed.

One `Organica.createZoomPan` per step canvas (`FVS_ZOOM_SURFACES`: `#element-frame`, `#component-edit-frame` (Edit mode only), `#symbol-frame`, `#figure-stage`), each `isReady` only while its step/mode is showing, so the Component gallery still scrolls; `#element-frame` is off while drawing Freehand (Paper.js maps the pointer in untransformed pixels) and resets on entering it. Regression: block 23 rewritten (`layers:ui:row`, `layers:roleCycle`, `layers:hidden`, `layers:dnd`); `content:mask/subtraction` hashes unchanged, so the drawing of visible layers is unchanged. 371/371 twice. **Follow-up (IT):** no green, design-system colours, no borders. The `.fvs-layer*` CSS no longer uses `--tool`: hover/active row + its placement block = `--panel`, active name `--w-medium`, and drop / focus / selected-chip marks use `--ink`. Every border is gone (list, rows, placement, swatches, ink popover, the + button's `.icon-btn` border overridden locally); the popover keeps only its shadow. A white ink swatch has no ring now (it reads on the `--panel` hover/active rows). Then (IT) selected = light grey `--panel`, hover = the darker `--track-bg` (also over the selected row). CSS only; the regression is unchanged. Not committed.

- **September 28, 2026 (continuity) — Element → Component lost the layer masks (bug: shared clip ids).** Diego (IT): no continuity from Element to Component, as if the overlapping layers weren't carried over. His Element: Circle + Chevron + Segment grid as Subtraction mask. The Component SVG itself was right (standalone raster 37% ink, PNG canvas 34%, the same stripes as the Element), but the gallery thumbnails showed whole circles. Cause: a stack's `<clipPath>`/`<mask>` id was per SHAPE (`stackUid`), so every drawing of the same Element in the page shared it. `url(#id)` resolves to the FIRST match in the document, which was the Element previews, and those are `display:none` while on Component, so the clip was ignored. **Fix:** each drawing now owns its ids. `stackPathMarkup` → `stk<hash>-<seq>-<layer>`, and the same latent bug in two more places got the same fix via `nextDrawId()`: the Component Role Container/Mask id (`compfx-<name>`, shared by every gallery thumbnail even though each one's cells differ) and the Symbol 'Clip to cell' ids (`clip-cell-<i>`, shared by the Symbol view / suggestions / library thumbnails). The regression `norm()` strips the per-drawing suffixes, so all 371 existing hashes are byte-identical; `layers:uniqueClipIds` asserts every stk/compfx/clip-cell id in the page is unique after a Component gallery render (372). Live: the Component gallery shows 32 clipPaths with 32 unique ids and striped thumbnails; the Symbol shows 1672 ids, all unique, with striped cells. Side note: a `location.reload()` during the debug reset Diego's unsaved Element in the shared browser pane (rebuilt approximately for the test; he was told). Not committed. **Then: transparent Paper, everywhere (Diego's pick) with a checkerboard button next to Paper.** `state.paperColor = 'none'` (`PAPER_NONE`, `isPaperNone`): all 11 SVG paper rects become `fill="none"` for free, including saved entries and nested Symbol papers. The 8 Canvas paper fills go through `fillPaper()` (a no-op for 'none'), so PNG keeps the alpha. Suggest's ink analysis treats an opaque pixel as ink when the paper is clear, and the Figure checks' `rasterMask` uses a white stand-in ground. UI: `#btn-paper-clear` (aria-pressed); `setPaperUI(c)` replaces every `paperSwatch.set` restore (snapshot / library / symbol entry / recipe / Figure), and the swatch keeps the last colour, which comes back when toggled off. `body.paper-clear` draws a token checkerboard behind every preview. 5 `paper:clear:*` cases (377). Not committed.

- **September 28, 2026 (Symbol debug) — layered Components inside a Symbol: fixed layer inks used the LIVE palette (bug).** Diego: debug the Symbol with layered components. Test Component: Circle (follow cell) + Star (Ink 2 red, Width 1.5) + hidden Triangle + Segment grid as Subtraction mask; then the live palette was changed to other colours. **Bug:** `inkPaletteOverride` ('set while rendering a saved entry with ITS palette') was declared but never assigned anywhere, so a layer with a fixed ink resolved it from `state.colors`. Every Symbol cell and even the library thumbnail painted the star in the live Ink 2 (teal, 100×) instead of the saved red; the 'follow cell' layer was right, since `entryInkAt` already used the saved palette. **Fix:** `withEntryInks(colors, fn)` sets the override around every saved-entry drawing: Symbol nested cells in SVG (`symbolSVGFromItems`) and Canvas (`drawSymbolCanvas`, via a new `nestedColors` item field), library / picker / content-overlay tiles, `componentThumbSVG`, the Suggest ink analysis, and the Component-role underlying Component in SVG + Canvas. Verified live: red 125× / teal 0 in the Symbol, thumbnail red, the hidden layer is not drawn, per-layer Width kept, 100 unique mask clips, SVG vs PNG 0.02% (AA edges), Suggest 10 proposals, 1383 unique ids page-wide. Not a bug: the cell colour override is Seed-cells-only by design (the Colour row is hidden for Component cells). Regression: `symbol:layered:thumbInks` + `symbol:layered:nested` (379). The new case first leaked control state (Width/Repeat Y/Rounded caps aren't reset by `reset()`) and changed 6 later hashes; its `finally` now restores them. Not committed.

- **September 29, 2026 — Design-system foundation: Wix Madefor Display, white dotted canvas area, one stage shadow.** Diego: (1) try a new family, Wix Madefor Display (OFL, variable wght 400–800, vendored as `shared/vendor/wix-madefor-display-variable.ttf` + its OFL text) → `@font-face` in `tokens.css`, `--font: 'Wix Madefor Display', 'Manrope', …` (Manrope stays as fallback and for the text seeds' opentype.js outlines; there is no 300, so `--w-light` renders 400). Verified per element across all 29 pages (~5,700 text elements). 6 spots bypassed `--font` and were aligned: Camo Turing's Georgia info-tip, Membrane's monospace readouts, Loom's ui-monospace readout, Pollen's retired `--mono` overlay, `shared/glyph-editor.js` canvas labels (now read `--font` at draw time; canvas accepts the full list), and the 3 Genesis redirect stubs (now link `tokens.css`). The 'Manrope only' rule → 'one interface typeface, via --font' (this file, DESIGN-SYSTEM.md §1, design-system page, THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES). (2) The canvas **area** is now white + a light cyan dot grid: new tokens `--canvas-bg` (= `--paper`), `--canvas-dot` `#a8dff0`, `--canvas-grid` (radial dots at a 24px/`--space-7` pitch), used as `background: var(--canvas-grid), var(--canvas-bg)` by `shell.css` `#canvas-wrap`/`.org-canvas-wrap` (18 tools) and by the tools with their own area: FVS, Rhizome (its warm `#e5e0d4` dots replaced), Genesis `.stage`, Apostate `#stage`, Mycel/TuneSutra `#stage-wrap`. Mote's kiosk stays black. Every remaining `--panel` background was checked by selector: all interface chrome (inputs, thumbnails, layer rows, floatbar), kept. (3) The **stage** (the sheet you draw on): Diego asked whether `#board` defines it. No — it's Apostate/Living Path's local id; the style is the class `.org-stage` in `shell.css`, while ids vary per tool because scripts find the stage by id. New token `--stage-shadow` (the old hand-typed `0 4px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.18)`): `.org-stage` uses it, the 7 local copies (Apostate, Camo Turing, Loom, Mycel, Pulsar, Trellis, TuneSutra — two were at .12) and the lighter .12 overrides on Pollen/Halide/Spore were replaced, and it was added where there was no sheet shadow (FVS's 4 step sheets, Membrane/Vortex p5 canvases, Genesis `#art` instead of its border). Rhizome is an infinite node canvas: no sheet. Live-verified: all 22 sheets compute the token's shadow, all canvas areas are white+dots, the design-system self-check passes (new tokens documented + swatches), FVS 379/379. `loom/_test-*.html` keep a raw shadow (dev benches that don't load tokens). **Follow-up (IT: 'assicurati di aggiornare il design system con le nuove regole'):** `/design-system/` gains a **Canvas area & stage** entry (nav + live demo + the 4 rules + exceptions), 3 new Common mistakes (retyped stage shadow, grey canvas area, font outside `--font`), and self-check assertions (the demo stage's shadow = `--stage-shadow`, the demo area = grid + white, the body font = Wix Madefor Display). `docs/UI-SHELL.md` §4 Canvas rules + a §6 new-tool step. The font preload in 30 pages (hub, every tool, template, legal pages) switched from Manrope to Wix Madefor Display. A sed accidentally hit the two `.claude/worktrees` checkouts too; only that line was reverted there (their own pre-existing work left untouched).

- **September 29, 2026 — Header v2: one definition, then a redesign (transparent 110px bar, mega menu, Sign Up/Login, pattern circles).** Diego: "header is transparent, height 110px, padding top/bottom, no border; on the hub too, remove 'Organica / Visual Design Language System' from the column; a nav icon in the style of Pinterest's mega dropdown; logo (empty for now) + name; Sign Up / Login on the index; 4 circle theme pattern". Decisions (asked): **in flow** (not overlay — costs 66px per canvas), hub **public** with Sign Up + Login both → `/sign-in` (tools stay gated), mega menu = the tool groups, **placeholder** logo, the circles switch the **canvas pattern**. Before that, the same day: the header collapsed from 4 variants (`--tool/--editor/--catalog`/plain) to one, the six ungated tools (Apostate, Living Path, Membrane, Sinew, Spore, Vortex) got `auth.js` + the badge, the sign-in card became `shared/auth-card.css` (`.org-auth-card`, live in the design system), Genesis's local `--border-strong` override was removed (all pushed to prod, smoke 32/32; the staging preview is behind Vercel auth so it was never smoke-tested — a reminder task checks the gate live on Sep 30). **Built:** `header.css` (`--header-h: 110px`, transparent, `padding: --space-8 --space-7`, no rule; `.org-header__nav`, `__mark`, `.org-header__auth`, `.org-pattern*`, `.org-mega*`); `shared/tools.js` (8 groups / 23 tools) + `shared/header.js` (mega menu via `Organica.popover`, pattern radiogroup with arrow keys) + `shared/pattern-init.js` (sync in `<head>`); `tokens.css` `:root[data-canvas-pattern="grid|lines|plain"]` re-point `--canvas-grid` so every canvas area follows with no per-tool edit; `auth-badge.js` shows Login / Sign Up while signed out (marks its box `data-org-auth-box`); `sign-in` reads `?mode=signup` (request-access copy — sign-ups stay invite-only, same flow); hub now carries the real `.org-header` (own `.header`/payoff/`.header-account` CSS deleted, `side-nav`/`display-area` start at `--header-h`, its background follows the pattern too, `ORGANICA_PUBLIC` set). All 29 header pages got the nav button + mark + the two scripts by one scripted replace. `scripts/check.py` gained "tools.js vs hub nav". **Verified live:** 11 pages — header 110px, transparent, workspace ends exactly at the viewport, no overflow (Terms scrolls as a document); mega menu opens/closes (click, Escape, focus back), all 23 links 200; **the mega menu was then re-styled to match Pinterest's top-left (Business Hub) menu, measured live in Diego's Chrome** — full-bleed white sheet under the bar (square top, `--radius-lg` bottom), 5 columns/row with hairline dividers via a subgrid (heading 17px bold over 14px link rows with grey hover), a Shortcuts column (Home + the current tool in a dark `--ink` pill), a tinted foot strip ("Organica: Visual Design Language System", the payoff moved off the hub), the page dimmed (`.org-mega__backdrop`, click closes) and a grey pill on the open trigger; Pinterest's 20/16px type and 16px radius snapped to `--fs-xl`/`--fs-large`/`--radius-lg` (no new tokens); each circle swaps the pattern + persists; signed-out hub shows Login/Sign Up; design-system self-check passes with 3 new assertions. **Not verified:** real Safari/Firefox; the narrow-desktop bar (~600–820px: circles shrink / suffix drops — reasoned, not looked at; phones hit the desktop-only gate so the bar never shows there); the hub public on a deployed URL; the logo is a placeholder circle. Design system: Header rewritten, Account control + sign-in buttons, new Mega menu and Pattern switcher entries. Not committed. **Follow-ups the same day:** tools' bar 64px (hub keeps 110px via its own `:root`), name in sentence case, 16px pattern circles, dropdown type 14/12px + tighter token spacing, nav icon → borderless hamburger, hub noise field removed, and the mega menu's **Shortcuts column removed** (8 groups, four per row; the current tool is just bold in its group).
- **September 29, 2026 — Typography foundation: two typefaces by role, editorial roles, `.org-prose`, a live specimen.** Diego asked for the typography foundation "from H1", then tried fonts. Path: Wix Madefor Display → DM Sans / Inter Tight trials (rolled back with `git checkout`) → Space Grotesk → **two families by role** (his call): **Space Grotesk** (`--font-display`) for display, H1–H5, buttons/controls, numbers, and panel labels/titles; **IBM Plex Mono** (`--font-mono`) for H6/overline, body, caption, panel hints, code. `--font` is now the *inherited default and points at the mono*; `button, select, input, textarea, summary` (real specificity, placed AFTER the `font-family: inherit` rule — a `:where()` version silently lost to it), h1–h5 (`:where`), `.org-header__logo`, `.org-mega`, `.org-num` and every rule that declares `tabular-nums` re-point/set the display face. The panel-label switch came from a measurement, not taste: Plex Mono is ~20% wider per character and clipped panel labels. **Tokens added** (approved via the "ask before adding a token" rule): `--fs-2xl…6xl` (20/24/32/44/64, editorial only), `--w-semibold` 600, `--lh-none/tight/heading` (`--lh-tight`/`--ls-tight` were retired Sep 6 and *reinstated* — `scripts/css-lint.py`'s retired list was updated), `--ls-display/tight/snug`, the role tokens `--t-display|h1…h6-{size,weight,lh,ls}`, `--t-body-{lg,md,sm}-size` + `--t-body-weight/lh`, `--t-caption-*`, the numeric role `--t-num-{font,variant,ls}` (+ `.org-num`), header roles `--t-header-{logo,action}-{size,weight}`, `--measure` 66ch. **`--w-light` removed** (9px Light in grey is the wrong legibility trade — the panel is 10/400 + 9/400). Accessibility decisions (Diego: "decide yourself on readability and accessibility standards"): reading pages default 14px/1.7/≤66ch; `font-synthesis: none` (Space Grotesk has no italic; `em` is the mono's *real* italic, upright inside display text); tabular figures for all readouts (default figures are proportional, "1" ≈ ⅔ of "8"); `text-wrap: balance/pretty`; `.org-truncate`; **`--accent-warm` is a non-text accent** (4.6:1 on white, 3.8:1 on `--panel`, 2.7:1 on Mote's dark overlay — the design-system nav's current item and Mote's `#note` no longer use it as text). **`shared/prose.css`** (`.org-prose`, `--compact`, `__lead/__small/__caption/__meta`) styles bare h1–h6/p/lists/links/code/tables from the roles; `/privacy` + `/terms` use it (H1 17→32px, H2 14→24px, body 12→14px, links underlined `--ink`); `/admin` reads the role tokens directly (a dense data page, not prose). Sweeps: 21 raw `font-weight` + 17 raw `font-size` values moved to tokens (sub-9px raised to the floor); left alone on purpose: TuneSutra's SVG label sizes (its SVG export may depend on them), Genesis's 15/16px icon glyphs, `genesis/page.css` (archive), Living Path's inline demo string. **Design system:** the Typography entry was rewritten as a specimen — two-face cards, "how the tokens work" (primitives → roles → components), a **19-role matrix whose face badges are asserted against the rendered CSS** (a role whose face drifts from its badge turns the page's self-check red), primitives (size ladder, weights in both faces, line-height blocks, tracking), rules; plus a Prose entry. Mistakes worth remembering: the first rewrite **swallowed the Color tokens section** (restored from HEAD before anyone saw it — when replacing a section by index range, list what lies between the anchors first); the self-check's canvas-pattern assertion assumed dots and failed for any saved pattern (now pattern-aware). Halide's "Atkinson" segment button clipped 2px at equal width → `#alg-seg .seg-btn { flex: 1 1 auto }` (local; a general `.seg-btn` change would alter every segmented control). Shipped in prod pushes `4f3f34b` (tokens + prose), `79d4606` (Space Grotesk + a11y rules), `1cab099` + `b518b33` (two-family split + specimen), smoke 32/32 each. **Not verified:** Safari/Firefox rendering of either face; the other ~16 tools' panels for label clipping in the mono (checked Halide, Loom, Pollen, Camo Turing, FVS, Genesis, Colornet); the Figma text styles were not updated to the two-family split.
- **September 29, 2026 — The Slosh slider is THE slider, everywhere (old slider removed).** Diego tried Bencho's Slosh slider (MIT) as a design-system test, then chose it as the only slider. Look, iterated live in Flexible Visual System: grey `--track-bg` well, **`--ink` liquid** (a white liquid vanished on our off-white panels), a full-height 9px handle (`--space-3`+1px, `--paper`, `--border-strong` stroke, soft all-round `--ink` shadow, corner = the track's `--radius-md`), a **dotted centre line** (2px dots every `--space-3`, drawn once with `background-blend-mode: difference` so it reads on both the grey and the ink), grab/grabbing cursor, and the number beside it **drag-to-scrub + click-to-type** with an `ew-resize` cursor and a light `--border` hover stroke. The number box and slider share one height (`--slo-h` = `--row-h` − `--space-2`, on `:root` in panel.css) and corner (`--slo-r`). **Implementation is on the NATIVE input, with no wrapper:** the first (wrapper) test would have broken Colornet/Sinew/Living Path, which do `input.nextElementSibling.textContent = …` (audit finding), and value/step/keys/disabled/`input` listeners must stay untouched. The spring (`Organica.slosh` in core.js, applied to every range by `enhanceSliders` — `organicaWireRange` — incl. ranges added later by its MutationObserver) writes `--sf` (liquid position 0..1) and, only while leaning, `--sedge` (an angled hard-stop gradient whose iso-lines ARE the slanted meniscus); until the spring exists panel.css falls back to `--fv`, the rigid value. Defaults viscosity 85 / tilt 100 (Diego asked for "even more liquid"); **momentum default 0** (a coast re-fires `input` every frame, costly in tools that regenerate an image per input, and a parameter that drifts after release is a surprise) — opt in with `data-momentum`; `data-slosh="off"` opts a range out. **Old slider deleted:** the 5px-line/round-thumb/`@property --fill` block, and the test's `.org-slosh` wrapper CSS. Also `Organica.enhanceSliders(document)` added to Rhizome (its inspector builds ranges from JS and never called it, so they were unfilled). Audit (hidden-frame sweep of all 23 tools + live checks): every range slosh-wired, 0 fill/value mismatches at rest, track and number box both 20px; Flexible Visual System regression 379/379. Known: Pollen's paired Spacing min/max row is two 25px sliders (pre-existing layout, cramped). Design system: one "Slider" entry (test entry removed), self-check passes; UI-SHELL updated. Chromium only — Safari/Firefox unchecked (the dots rely on `background-blend-mode` and the angled edge on gradient maths; Firefox needs `::-moz-range-track/-thumb/-progress`, written but not seen). Not committed.
